===== scripts/build_static_site.py =====
SHA-256: 0b9269675a2094e4d2bda5f320dfc5b9a997c0f230088178903ed44d9773240b

import hashlib
from collections import Counter
import json
import logging
import os
import re
import shutil
import sys
import time
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.catalog import build_catalog, source_metadata
from esstu_schedule.parser import (
    get_week_num,
)
from esstu_schedule.rooms import build_rooms_index
from esstu_schedule.teachers import departments_from_teachers, teachers_from_groups

from esstu_schedule.data.diagnostics import (
    parsing_status,
    reset_diagnostics,
)
from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.data.provenance import build_provenance

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEMO_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("ESSTU_SOURCE_DIR", BASE_DIR / "demo_data"))
STATIC_DIR = BASE_DIR / "static"
DIST_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("ESSTU_DIST_DIR", BASE_DIR / "dist"))


LOGGER = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def shell_paths(dist):
    return [
        "./",
        *[
            "./" + path.relative_to(dist).as_posix()
            for path in sorted(dist.rglob("*"))
            if path.is_file()
            and path.relative_to(dist).parts[0] != "api"
            and path != dist / "sw.js"
        ],
    ]


def build_id(dist: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for path in sorted(dist.rglob("*")):
        relative = path.relative_to(dist)
        if not path.is_file() or relative.parts[0] == "api":
            continue
        content = path.read_bytes()
        if path.suffix in {".html", ".js", ".css", ".json", ".svg", ".txt"}:
            content = content.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
        if relative == Path("index.html"):
            content = re.sub(
                rb'content="[^"]+" name="app-version"',
                b'content="__BUILD_ID__" name="app-version"',
                content,
            )
        if relative == Path("sw.js"):
            content = re.sub(
                rb"const CACHE_NAME = '[^']+';",
                b"const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';",
                content,
            )
            content = re.sub(
                rb"const SHELL = \[.*?\];",
                ("const SHELL = " + json.dumps(shell_paths(dist)) + ";").encode(),
                content,
            )
        digest.update(relative.as_posix().encode())
        digest.update(b"\0")
        digest.update(len(content).to_bytes(8, "big"))
        digest.update(content)
    return digest.hexdigest()[:16]


def stamp_shell(dist: Path) -> str:
    shell_bytes = sum(
        path.stat().st_size
        for path in dist.rglob("*")
        if path.is_file() and path.relative_to(dist).parts[0] != "api"
    )
    if shell_bytes > 5 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise RuntimeError("Offline shell exceeds the 5 MiB budget")
    identity = build_id(dist)
    worker = dist / "sw.js"
    worker_text, worker_count = re.subn(
        r"const CACHE_NAME = '[^']+';",
        f"const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-{identity}';",
        worker.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
    )
    index = dist / "index.html"
    index_text, index_count = re.subn(
        r'content="[^"]+" name="app-version"',
        f'content="{identity}" name="app-version"',
        index.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
    )
    if worker_count != 1 or index_count != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Expected exactly one CACHE_NAME and app-version marker; shell was not stamped"
        )
    worker_text = re.sub(
        r"const SHELL = \[.*?\];",
        "const SHELL = " + json.dumps(shell_paths(dist)) + ";",
        worker_text,
    )
    worker.write_text(worker_text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    index.write_text(index_text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    return identity


def configure_console():
    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
            stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="backslashreplace")


def source_modified_iso(value):
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        parsed = parsedate_to_datetime(value)
        return parsed.astimezone(UTC).isoformat() if parsed.tzinfo else None
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        try:
            parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            return parsed.astimezone(UTC).isoformat() if parsed.tzinfo else None
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return None


def write_api_json(path, payload):
    write_json_atomic(path, {**payload, "schema_version": 2})


def build():
    configure_console()
    reset_diagnostics()
    css_path = STATIC_DIR / "app.css"
    if not css_path.is_file() or not (
        css_path.stat().st_size >= 8000
        and "tailwindcss" in css_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        and "--campus-bg" in css_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Сначала соберите полный CSS: npm run build:css")
    print("🚀 Starting static build for GitHub Pages...")
    start_time = time.time()

    if (
        not DIST_DIR.resolve().is_relative_to(BASE_DIR.resolve())
        or DIST_DIR.resolve() == BASE_DIR.resolve()
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Build output must stay inside the workspace")
    if DIST_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(DIST_DIR)
    DIST_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 1. Copy static assets (index.html, manifest.json, sw.js, icons)
    for item in STATIC_DIR.iterdir():
        if item.is_file():
            shutil.copy2(item, DIST_DIR / item.name)
        elif item.is_dir():
            shutil.copytree(item, DIST_DIR / item.name)
    for asset in DIST_DIR.rglob("*"):
        if asset.is_file() and asset.suffix in {
            ".html",
            ".js",
            ".css",
            ".json",
            ".svg",
            ".txt",
        }:
            asset.write_bytes(asset.read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n"))
    print("✓ Copied static files to dist/")

    # 2. Create API directory structure
    api_dir = DIST_DIR / "api"
    api_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "groups").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "schedule").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "departments").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "teachers" / "all").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (api_dir / "department").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Status
    built_at = datetime.now(UTC)
    generated_at = built_at.isoformat()
    now_week = get_week_num(built_at)
    status_payload = {
        "university": "ВСГУТУ",
        "timezone": "Asia/Irkutsk (UTC+8)",
        "server_time_ulan_ude": built_at.astimezone(
            timezone(timedelta(hours=8))
        ).isoformat(),
        "week_calculated_at": generated_at,
        **now_week,
    }

    from scripts.group_build import build_groups

    group_schedules, group_failures, catalog_repairs, group_expected = build_groups(
        DEMO_DIR,
        api_dir,
        metadata=source_metadata(DEMO_DIR, with_hashes=True),
        now=built_at,
        baseline=os.environ.get("ESSTU_BASELINE_DIR"),
        data_update=os.environ.get("ESSTU_DATA_UPDATE") == "1",
    )
    metadata = source_metadata(DEMO_DIR, with_hashes=True)

    catalog, institutes, departments = build_catalog(
        DEMO_DIR, teachers_from_groups(group_schedules)
    )
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet"):
        write_api_json(
            api_dir / "teachers" / "all" / f"{section}.json",
            {
                "count": len(catalog),
                "teachers": catalog,
                "generated_at": generated_at,
            },
        )
        write_api_json(
            api_dir / "departments" / f"{section}.json",
            {
                "institutes": institutes[section] or departments_from_teachers(catalog),
                "generated_at": generated_at,
            },
        )
    for section, caf, payload in departments:
        target = api_dir / "department" / section
        target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        write_api_json(target / f"{caf}.json", payload)
    print(
        f"Official department files: {len(departments)}; combined teachers: {len(catalog)}"
    )

    room_sources = [*group_schedules, *catalog]
    sync_status = (
        json.loads((DEMO_DIR / "department-sync.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if (DEMO_DIR / "department-sync.json").exists()
        else {}
    )
    provenance = build_provenance(metadata, BASE_DIR)
    write_json_atomic(api_dir / "sources.json", metadata)
    diagnostics = {**parsing_status(), **provenance}
    diagnostics["incomplete_schedule_count"] = sum(
        bool(item.get("is_incomplete")) for item in [*group_schedules, *catalog]
    )
    from esstu_schedule.identity import SOURCE_REASONS
    from scripts.parser_revision import parser_revision
    from scripts.group_freshness import group_freshness

    diagnostics.update(group_freshness(group_schedules, built_at))
    diagnostics["parser_revision"] = parser_revision()
    diagnostics["catalog_repairs"] = catalog_repairs
    diagnostics["catalog_recovered_count"] = len(catalog_repairs)
    diagnostics["pipeline_failed_count"] = sum(
        row["reason"] not in SOURCE_REASONS for row in group_failures
    )
    diagnostics["source_issue_count"] = sum(
        row["reason"] in SOURCE_REASONS for row in group_failures
    )
    diagnostics["group_failures"] = group_failures
    diagnostics["group_failure_reasons"] = dict(
        Counter(row["reason"] for row in group_failures)
    )
    diagnostics["fallback_group_count"] = sum(
        item.get("source_mode") == "last_good_json" for item in group_schedules
    )
    diagnostics["group_expected_count"] = group_expected
    diagnostics["group_failed_count"] = group_expected - sum(
        not item.get("is_incomplete") for item in group_schedules
    )
    write_api_json(
        api_dir / "catalog-status.json",
        {**sync_status, **diagnostics, "generated_at": generated_at},
    )
    identity = stamp_shell(DIST_DIR)
    write_api_json(
        api_dir / "status.json",
        {
            **status_payload,
            **diagnostics,
            "generated_at": generated_at,
            "build_id": identity,
        },
    )
    rooms_payload = {
        **build_rooms_index(room_sources),
        "generated_at": generated_at,
        "parse_error_count": diagnostics["parse_error_count"],
        "schema_version": 2,
    }
    write_api_json(api_dir / "rooms.json", rooms_payload)
    # Runtime index survives replacing dist/ during the next background build.
    runtime_root = Path(os.environ.get("ESSTU_SNAPSHOT_DIR", BASE_DIR / "cache_store"))
    write_json_atomic(
        Path(os.environ.get("ESSTU_ROOMS_INDEX", runtime_root / "rooms.json")),
        rooms_payload,
    )
    print(
        f"🎉 Build complete in {time.time() - start_time:.2f}s! All output saved to dist/"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    build()


===== scripts/check_sync.py =====
SHA-256: a192b482048ec950f44ae39b49414f69853aafaec238cbf38caf7e541c64448b

"""A partial failed download is visible to Actions and cannot be published as success."""

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path


def check_reports(root, allow_partial=False):
    validated = 0
    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        report = json.loads((root / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        validated += len(report.get("downloaded", []))
        if not allow_partial and (not report.get("downloaded") or report.get("failed")):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"{name}: downloaded={len(report.get('downloaded', []))}; failed={len(report.get('failed', []))}"
            )

    if not validated:
        raise RuntimeError("No source validated; keep the previous data publication")


def check_parse_quality(site, max_error_rate=None, baseline=None, max_regression=None):
    if max_error_rate is None:
        max_error_rate = float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_MAX_PARSE_ERROR_RATE", "0.05"))
    if max_regression is None:
        max_regression = float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_MAX_PARSE_REGRESSION", "0.01"))
    if not 0 <= max_regression <= 1:
        raise ValueError("max_regression must be between 0 and 1")
    if not 0 <= max_error_rate <= 1:
        raise ValueError("max_error_rate must be between 0 and 1")
    status = json.loads((Path(site) / "api/status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    total, failed = (
        status.get("group_expected_count", 0),
        status.get("pipeline_failed_count", status.get("group_failed_count", 0)),
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(total, int)
        or total <= 0
        or not isinstance(failed, int)
        or not 0 <= failed <= total
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Parser coverage is missing or invalid")
    ratio = failed / total
    allowed = max_error_rate
    if baseline and (Path(baseline) / "api/status.json").exists():
        previous = json.loads(
            (Path(baseline) / "api/status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )
        previous_total = previous.get("group_expected_count", 0)
        previous_failed = previous.get("pipeline_failed_count")
        if previous_failed is None:
            from esstu_schedule.identity import SOURCE_REASONS

            previous_failed = sum(
                row.get("reason") not in SOURCE_REASONS
                for row in previous.get("group_failures", [])
            )
            if not previous.get("group_failures"):
                # Legacy APIs contain parse_errors but no source/pipeline distinction.
                previous_failed = sum(
                    not any(
                        marker in row.get("message", "")
                        for marker in (
                            "Имя группы не совпадает",
                            "Название на странице",
                        )
                    )
                    for row in previous.get("parse_errors", [])
                    if "/Caf" not in row.get("source", "")
                )
        if previous_total:
            allowed = min(allowed, previous_failed / previous_total + max_regression)
    if ratio > allowed:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Publication blocked: {failed}/{total} pipeline failures ({ratio:.1%}), allowed {allowed:.1%}"
        )
    print(f"Parser quality OK: {failed}/{total} pipeline failures ({ratio:.1%})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--site", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--allow-partial", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-error-rate",
        type=float,
        default=float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_MAX_PARSE_ERROR_RATE", "0.05")),
    )
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.site:
        check_parse_quality(args.site, args.max_error_rate, args.baseline)
    else:
        check_reports(
            Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "demo_data", args.allow_partial
        )


===== scripts/contract_site.py =====
SHA-256: 13ddf935e967ba9348e8bd5e6109ffe4420d58dc39399749f0f4dcaf40db153c

"""Run the new shell against the exact previous API, including its old schema."""

import shutil
import json
from pathlib import Path


def prepare(
    site=Path("dist"),
    baseline=Path("preview/data"),
    target=Path("preview/contract-site"),
):
    count = 0
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        groups = json.loads(
            (baseline / f"api/groups/{section}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )["groups"]
        if not groups:
            raise RuntimeError("Empty published group catalog: " + section)
        for group in groups:
            schedule = json.loads(
                (baseline / f"api/schedule/{section}/{group['id']}.json").read_text(
                    encoding="utf-8"
                )
            )
            if not isinstance(schedule.get("weeks"), dict):
                raise RuntimeError("Incompatible published group schedule")
        count += len(groups)
    if count < 100:
        raise RuntimeError("Published catalog is unexpectedly small")
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet"):
        teachers = json.loads(
            (baseline / f"api/teachers/all/{section}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )["teachers"]
        if not teachers:
            raise RuntimeError("Empty published teacher catalog")
    rooms = json.loads((baseline / "api/rooms.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[
        "rooms"
    ]
    if not isinstance(rooms, list) or not rooms:
        raise RuntimeError("Empty/incompatible published rooms")
    if target.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(target)
    shutil.copytree(site, target, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("api"))
    shutil.copytree(baseline / "api", target / "api")
    print(
        "Published API contract smoke OK:",
        count,
        "groups; original API bytes preserved",
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    prepare()


===== scripts/data_branch.py =====
SHA-256: 6c2b4b6655f5baf0ca321b652216c327128401daecba8dbba174d38655f3392a

"""Durable validated data snapshots, stored separately from application history."""

import argparse
import base64
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path


def git(root, *arguments, check=True):
    environment = os.environ.copy()
    if environment.get("GITHUB_TOKEN"):
        environment.update(
            GIT_CONFIG_COUNT="1",
            GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0="http.https://github.com/.extraheader",
            GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0="AUTHORIZATION: basic "
            + base64.b64encode(
                ("x-access-token:" + environment["GITHUB_TOKEN"]).encode()
            ).decode(),
        )
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["git", *arguments], cwd=root, env=environment, capture_output=True, text=True
    )
    if check and result.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError("Data branch git operation failed: " + arguments[0])
    return result


def initialize(directory, repository):
    directory = Path(directory)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    git(directory, "init", "-q")
    git(directory, "config", "core.autocrlf", "false")
    git(directory, "remote", "add", "origin", repository)
    available = git(
        directory, "ls-remote", "--heads", "origin", "refs/heads/data"
    ).stdout.strip()
    if available:
        git(directory, "fetch", "--depth=1", "origin", "data")
        git(directory, "checkout", "-B", "data", "FETCH_HEAD")
    return bool(available)


def restore(directory, repository, sources):
    directory = Path(directory)
    available = initialize(directory, repository)
    if not available:
        from scripts.published_data import bootstrap

        owner, repo = repository.removesuffix(".git").rstrip("/").split("/")[-2:]
        bootstrap(directory, f"https://{owner}.github.io/{repo}/")
        from scripts.source_artifact import restore_artifact
        import json

        expected = json.loads(
            (directory / "api/sources.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )
        if not restore_artifact(f"{owner}/{repo}", sources, expected=expected):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "No source snapshot matches the published API; parser rebuild cannot safely use unrelated sources"
            )
        return False
    if not (directory / "api/status.json").exists():
        raise RuntimeError("Data branch is missing a validated API")
    shutil.copytree(directory / "sources", sources, dirs_exist_ok=True)
    print("Restored durable validated sources and API")
    return True


def save(directory, site, sources, revision):
    directory = Path(directory)
    for name, source in [("api", Path(site) / "api"), ("sources", Path(sources))]:
        target = directory / name
        if target.resolve() == source.resolve():
            continue
        if target.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(target)
        shutil.copytree(source, target)
    (directory / "README.md").write_text(
        "Validated API and source snapshots. Application revision: " + revision + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    git(directory, "config", "user.name", "github-actions[bot]")
    git(
        directory,
        "config",
        "user.email",
        "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com",
    )
    git(directory, "add", "api", "sources", "README.md")
    if git(directory, "diff", "--cached", "--quiet", check=False).returncode == 0:
        print("Data unchanged")
        return
    git(directory, "checkout", "-B", "data")
    git(
        directory, "commit", "-m", "Update validated schedule data for " + revision[:12]
    )
    git(directory, "push", "origin", "HEAD:refs/heads/data")
    print("Validated data saved in branch data")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("mode", choices=["restore", "save", "initialize"])
    parser.add_argument("--directory", type=Path, default=Path("preview/data"))
    parser.add_argument("--site", type=Path, default=Path("dist"))
    parser.add_argument("--sources", type=Path, default=Path("demo_data"))
    parser.add_argument(
        "--repository",
        default="https://github.com/"
        + os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "stasdondukov2-sys/esstu-schedule")
        + ".git",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.mode == "restore":
        restore(args.directory, args.repository, args.sources)
    elif args.mode == "initialize":
        initialize(args.directory, args.repository)
    else:
        save(
            args.directory,
            args.site,
            args.sources,
            os.environ.get("GITHUB_SHA", "local"),
        )


===== scripts/freshness.cjs =====
SHA-256: ea7de8777c18c0a6733994bf3ff73f629967f07ad3548994f3a145a7e2bcb355

const TITLE='Расписание: выгрузка устарела';
function freshnessProblem(status,now=Date.now()){
  const issues=[];
  for(const [key,label] of [['generated_at','Сборка'],['source_checked_at_latest','Проверка источников']]){
    const stamp=Date.parse(status?.[key]);
    if(!Number.isFinite(stamp))issues.push(`${label}: дата неизвестна`);
    else if(stamp>now+300000)issues.push(`${label}: дата в будущем`);
    else if(now-stamp>12*3600000)issues.push(`${label}: более 12 часов назад (${new Date(stamp).toISOString()})`);
  }
  const dates=Object.values(status?.group_check_dates||{});
  const fresh=dates.filter(value=>{const stamp=Date.parse(value);return Number.isFinite(stamp)&&stamp<=now+300000&&now-stamp<=24*3600000;}).length;
  if(!dates.length)issues.push('Покрытие групп: даты проверки неизвестны');
  else if(fresh/dates.length<0.9)issues.push(`Покрытие групп за 24 часа: ${fresh}/${dates.length} (${(100*fresh/dates.length).toFixed(1)}%), требуется 90%`);
  return issues.join('\n');
}
async function monitor({github,context,fetchImpl=fetch,now=Date.now()}){
  const {owner,repo}=context.repo;
  const url=`https://${owner}.github.io/${repo}/api/status.json`;
  let problem;
  try{
    const response=await fetchImpl(`${url}?freshness=${now}`,{cache:'no-store',signal:AbortSignal.timeout(15000)});
    if(!response.ok)throw Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
    problem=freshnessProblem(await response.json(),now);
  }catch(error){problem=`Не удалось проверить опубликованный status.json: ${error.message}`;}
  const issues=await github.paginate(github.rest.issues.listForRepo,{owner,repo,state:'open',creator:'github-actions[bot]'});
  const existing=issues.find(issue=>!issue.pull_request && issue.title===TITLE);
  if(problem){
    const body=`Опубликованные данные требуют проверки.\n\n${problem}\n\n[Статус сайта](${url}) · [Проверка](https://github.com/${owner}/${repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}).`;
    if(existing)await github.rest.issues.update({owner,repo,issue_number:existing.number,body});
    else await github.rest.issues.create({owner,repo,title:TITLE,body});
  }else if(existing)await github.rest.issues.update({owner,repo,issue_number:existing.number,state:'closed',body:`Свежесть опубликованной выгрузки восстановлена. [Статус сайта](${url}).`});
  return problem;
}
module.exports={freshnessProblem,monitor};


===== scripts/group_build.py =====
SHA-256: 7e0adc78a6fde6b1f5b0601ab156443d8ba6d9452bf67b53c028e1af890281d3

"""Build independent group outputs, preserving source defects as explicit states."""

import json
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from esstu_schedule.data.repository import group_schedule
from esstu_schedule.data.diagnostics import record_error
from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.identity import resolve_catalog, issue_text
from esstu_schedule.parser import (
    SECTIONS,
    parse_groups_list_html,
    normalize_group_name,
    get_week_num,
)
from esstu_schedule.catalog import stamp_schedule
from scripts.publication import previous_groups, last_good_group, source_is_recent


def build_groups(root, api, metadata, now, baseline=None, data_update=False):
    root, api = Path(root), Path(api)
    previous = previous_groups(baseline)
    result = []
    failures = []
    repairs = []
    expected = 0
    failed_downloads = {
        row["path"]
        for report in root.glob("*sync.json")
        for row in json.loads(report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("failed", [])
        if "path" in row
    }
    for section in SECTIONS:
        catalog = root / section / "raspisan.htm"
        if not catalog.exists():
            raise RuntimeError("Missing group catalog: " + section)
        groups = parse_groups_list_html(
            catalog.read_bytes().decode("cp1251", errors="replace"), section
        )
        if not groups:
            raise RuntimeError("Empty group catalog: " + section)
        expected += len(groups)
        parsed = {}
        observed = {}
        pipeline = {}
        for group in groups:
            gid = str(group["id"])
            path = root / section / (gid + ".htm")
            try:
                negative = root / ".negative" / section / (gid + ".htm")
                negative_meta = negative.with_suffix(".json")
                rejected = (
                    json.loads(negative_meta.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                    if negative_meta.exists()
                    else {}
                )
                valid_negative = (
                    negative.exists()
                    and rejected.get("catalog_sha256")
                    == hashlib.sha256(catalog.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                    and rejected.get("sha256")
                    == hashlib.sha256(negative.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                )
                selected = negative if valid_negative else path
                observed[gid] = (
                    selected,
                    {
                        **metadata.get(f"{section}/{gid}.htm", {}),
                        **(
                            {
                                "retrieved_at": rejected["checked_at"],
                                "sha256": rejected["sha256"],
                                "last_modified": rejected.get("last_modified"),
                            }
                            if valid_negative
                            else {}
                        ),
                    },
                )
                parsed[gid] = group_schedule(
                    selected.read_bytes().decode("cp1251", errors="replace"),
                    gid,
                    section,
                )
            except Exception as error:
                pipeline[gid] = {
                    "reason": "missing_file"
                    if isinstance(error, FileNotFoundError)
                    else "parse_exception",
                    "category": "pipeline",
                    "error": str(error),
                }
        groups, identity_issues, section_repairs = resolve_catalog(groups, parsed)
        repairs.extend({"section": section, **repair} for repair in section_repairs)
        write_json_atomic(
            api / "groups" / f"{section}.json",
            {
                "schema_version": 2,
                "section": section,
                "count": len(groups),
                "groups": groups,
            },
        )
        for group in groups:
            gid = str(group["id"])
            relative = f"{section}/{gid}.htm"
            path = root / relative
            selected, entry = observed.get(gid, (path, metadata.get(relative, {})))
            sched = parsed.get(
                gid,
                {
                    "mode": "student",
                    "source_file": relative,
                    "group_id": gid,
                    "group_name": group["name"],
                    "section": section,
                    "is_incomplete": True,
                    "parse_errors": ["Файл расписания не прочитан."],
                    "weeks": {"1": {}, "2": {}},
                    "bells": [],
                    "total_lessons": 0,
                },
            )
            issue = identity_issues.get(gid) or pipeline.get(gid)
            if not issue and sched.get("is_incomplete"):
                issue = {
                    "reason": "table_structure",
                    "category": "pipeline",
                    "errors": sched.get("parse_errors", []),
                }
            if issue:
                text = (
                    issue_text(issue)
                    if gid in identity_issues
                    else (
                        " ".join(issue.get("errors", []))
                        or "Файл расписания не прочитан."
                    )
                )
                if issue["category"] == "source":
                    text = issue_text(issue)
                sched.update(
                    source_group_name=sched.get("group_name"),
                    group_name=group["name"],
                    norm_group_name=group["norm_name"],
                    weeks={"1": {}, "2": {}},
                    total_lessons=0,
                    is_incomplete=True,
                    parse_errors=[text],
                    incomplete_reason=text,
                    source_issue=issue if issue["category"] == "source" else None,
                )
            sched["resolved_group_id"] = gid
            sched["week_now"] = get_week_num(now)
            sched["week_calculated_at"] = now.isoformat()
            stamp_schedule(sched, {**metadata, relative: entry}, now.isoformat())
            from scripts.build_static_site import source_modified_iso

            sched["last_modified"] = source_modified_iso(entry.get("last_modified"))
            if data_update and relative in failed_downloads:
                sched.update(
                    is_stale_fallback=True,
                    fallback_reason=["Не удалось обновить источник"],
                )
            if (
                data_update
                and not sched.get("is_incomplete")
                and not source_is_recent(sched, now)
            ):
                issue = {"reason": "source_expired", "category": "pipeline"}
                sched.update(
                    is_incomplete=True,
                    parse_errors=[
                        "Источник старше 7 дней или дата проверки неизвестна"
                    ],
                    incomplete_reason="Источник старше 7 дней или дата проверки неизвестна",
                    weeks={"1": {}, "2": {}},
                    total_lessons=0,
                )
            if issue:
                failures.append(
                    {
                        "source": relative,
                        **issue,
                        "bytes": selected.stat().st_size if selected.exists() else 0,
                        "sha256": entry.get("sha256"),
                    }
                )
                if issue["category"] == "pipeline":
                    record_error(relative, issue["reason"])
            sched = last_good_group(
                sched, previous.get((section, normalize_group_name(group["name"]))), now
            )
            write_json_atomic(
                api / "schedule" / section / (gid + ".json"),
                {**sched, "schema_version": 2},
            )
            result.append(sched)
        print(f"Groups {section}: {len(groups)}")
    return result, failures, repairs, expected


===== scripts/group_freshness.py =====
SHA-256: fc865815ea28142aabd56771c3c81704d3b8e10b9b56848995f322838bfaeeeb

"""Group coverage uses all check dates, including unknown entries."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta


def group_freshness(schedules, now=None):
    now = now or datetime.now(UTC)
    dates = {
        f"{item['section']}/{item['group_id']}": item.get("source_checked_at")
        for item in schedules
    }
    valid = []
    fresh = 0
    for value in dates.values():
        try:
            stamp = datetime.fromisoformat((value or "").replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            age = now - stamp
            if age < timedelta(0):
                continue
            valid.append(stamp)
            fresh += age <= timedelta(hours=24)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass
    total = len(dates)
    return {
        "group_check_dates": dates,
        "groups_checked_24h": fresh,
        "groups_checked_24h_ratio": fresh / total if total else 0,
        "group_freshness_calculated_at": now.isoformat(),
        "group_checked_at_p10": sorted(valid)[max(0, len(valid) // 10 - 1)].isoformat()
        if valid
        else None,
    }


===== scripts/parser_revision.py =====
SHA-256: 8cd369401ce013f2fa563760a83aa9572e53c74e42af0b30d69020f26f2ce208

"""Fingerprint the transformation applied to saved sources, not the UI release."""

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PARSER_FILES = [
    "esstu_schedule/parser.py",
    "esstu_schedule/identity.py",
    "esstu_schedule/catalog.py",
    "esstu_schedule/teachers.py",
    "esstu_schedule/rooms.py",
    "esstu_schedule/data/models.py",
    "esstu_schedule/data/repository.py",
    "scripts/group_build.py",
    "scripts/group_freshness.py",
    "scripts/parser_revision.py",
    "scripts/build_static_site.py",
    "scripts/publication.py",
    "esstu_schedule/data/provenance.py",
    "requirements.txt",
]


def parser_revision():
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for name in PARSER_FILES:
        digest.update(name.encode())
        digest.update(b"\0")
        digest.update((ROOT / name).read_bytes().replace(b"\r\n", b"\n"))
    return digest.hexdigest()[:16]


===== scripts/publication.py =====
SHA-256: 4b892abae3fbdff8110e87cdba458dbd0b0011caac04ebb9cefc5bf5e3dcf21b

"""Select per-group fallbacks and keep code-only releases independent of data quality."""

import argparse
import json
import shutil
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.parser import normalize_group_name


def read_json(path):
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def source_is_recent(schedule, now, max_age_days=7):
    try:
        checked = datetime.fromisoformat(
            (schedule.get("source_checked_at") or "").replace("Z", "+00:00")
        )
        return timedelta(0) <= now - checked <= timedelta(days=max_age_days)
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


def last_good_group(candidate, previous, now=None, max_age_days=7):
    if not candidate.get("is_incomplete") or not previous:
        return candidate
    if not any(character.isalnum() for character in candidate.get("group_name", "")):
        return candidate
    if previous.get("is_incomplete") or normalize_group_name(
        previous.get("group_name", "")
    ) != normalize_group_name(candidate.get("group_name", "")):
        return candidate
    try:
        checked = datetime.fromisoformat(
            (previous.get("source_checked_at") or "").replace("Z", "+00:00")
        )
        age = (now or datetime.now(UTC)) - checked
        if not timedelta(0) <= age <= timedelta(days=max_age_days):
            return candidate
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return candidate
    result = deepcopy(previous)
    result.update(
        group_id=candidate["group_id"],
        resolved_group_id=candidate["group_id"],
        is_stale_fallback=True,
        source_mode="last_good_json",
        fallback_reason=candidate.get("parse_errors", []),
        origin_source_file=previous.get("source_file"),
        incomplete_reason=candidate.get("incomplete_reason"),
        source_issue=candidate.get("source_issue"),
    )
    return result


def previous_groups(root):
    if not root:
        return {}
    groups = {}
    for path in (Path(root) / "api/schedule").glob("*/*.json"):
        item = read_json(path)
        if not item.get("is_incomplete"):
            groups[
                (item.get("section"), normalize_group_name(item.get("group_name", "")))
            ] = item
    return groups


def reuse_api(site, baseline, expected_build):
    site, baseline = Path(site), Path(baseline)
    current = read_json(site / "api/status.json")
    previous = read_json(baseline / "api/status.json")
    if current["build_id"] != expected_build:
        raise RuntimeError("Publication shell differs from tested shell")
    # Parser changes must reach the API even for a release without portal access.
    if current.get("parser_revision") and current.get(
        "parser_revision"
    ) != previous.get("parser_revision"):
        from scripts.check_sync import check_parse_quality

        try:
            check_parse_quality(site, baseline=baseline)
        except RuntimeError as error:
            # A rejected data candidate must not prevent a tested shell release.
            previous.update(
                parser_rebuild_error=str(error),
                parser_rebuild_candidate_revision=current["parser_revision"],
            )
            print(
                "Parser rebuild rejected; keep previous API and publish tested shell:",
                error,
            )
        else:
            current.update(
                api_reparsed_from_sources=True,
                previous_parser_revision=previous.get("parser_revision"),
                code_deployed_at=current["generated_at"],
            )
            write_json_atomic(site / "api/status.json", current)
            print(
                "Parser changed: publish API rebuilt from restored source bytes; source check dates preserved"
            )
            return
    staged = site / "previous-api"
    shutil.copytree(baseline / "api", staged)
    shutil.rmtree(site / "api")
    staged.rename(site / "api")
    previous.update(
        build_id=expected_build,
        code_revision=current["code_revision"],
        code_deployed_at=current["generated_at"],
    )
    write_json_atomic(site / "api/status.json", previous)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--site", type=Path, default=Path("dist"))
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["code", "data"], required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-build", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    status = read_json(args.site / "api/status.json")
    if status["build_id"] != args.expected_build:
        raise RuntimeError("Publication shell differs from tested shell")
    if (
        args.mode == "code"
        and args.baseline
        and (args.baseline / "api/status.json").exists()
    ):
        reuse_api(args.site, args.baseline, args.expected_build)
        print("Code release: tested shell prepared; source check dates preserved")
    elif (
        args.mode == "code"
        and __import__("os").environ.get("GITHUB_REF") == "refs/heads/main"
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Code publication requires previous published API or durable data branch"
        )
    elif args.mode == "code":
        print(
            "Review build: committed test sources retained; data quality gate applies only to data releases"
        )
    else:
        from scripts.check_sync import check_parse_quality

        check_parse_quality(args.site, baseline=args.baseline)


===== scripts/published_data.py =====
SHA-256: 4665ebac113785cfc2a48e4b992f7859fd7e782663e1a4f0acc4b2c3c5948842

"""Bootstrap an immutable API snapshot from the currently published Pages site."""

import json
import urllib.request
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic


def bootstrap(directory, base):
    api = Path(directory) / "api"

    def fetch(path):
        request = urllib.request.Request(
            base.rstrip("/") + "/" + path,
            headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache", "User-Agent": "ESSTU-Schedule/2.5"},
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=20) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read())

    status = fetch("api/status.json")
    paths = [
        "api/groups/" + section + ".json"
        for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2")
    ]
    paths += [
        "api/teachers/all/" + section + ".json"
        for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")
    ]
    paths += [
        "api/departments/" + section + ".json"
        for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")
    ]
    paths += ["api/rooms.json", "api/sources.json", "api/catalog-status.json"]
    payloads = {}

    def download(path):
        value = fetch(path)
        write_json_atomic(Path(directory) / path, value)
        return path, value

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
        for path, value in pool.map(download, paths):
            payloads[path] = value
        schedules = [
            f"api/schedule/{section}/{group['id']}.json"
            for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2")
            for group in payloads[f"api/groups/{section}.json"]["groups"]
        ]
        departments = [
            f"api/department/{section}/{department['caf_id']}.json"
            for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")
            for institute in payloads[f"api/departments/{section}.json"]["institutes"]
            for department in institute["departments"]
        ]
        list(pool.map(download, sorted(set(schedules + departments))))
    after = fetch("api/status.json")
    if any(
        after.get(key) != status.get(key)
        for key in ("generated_at", "sources_sha256", "code_revision")
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Published data changed during bootstrap; retry using one complete release"
        )
    status.setdefault("group_expected_count", len(schedules))
    status.setdefault(
        "group_failed_count",
        sum(
            json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("is_incomplete", False)
            for path in (api / "schedule").glob("*/*.json")
        ),
    )
    status["baseline_origin"] = base
    write_json_atomic(api / "status.json", status)
    print("Bootstrapped published API:", len(schedules), "groups")


===== scripts/smoke_site.py =====
SHA-256: c086a8c5078bdfc0a381d7827bc7242e67f6a4f762ee1e54db4f830c2dbbfa3d

"""Validate a publication without tying live portal data to browser fixtures."""

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path


def check_site(root: Path, now: datetime | None = None, minimum_groups: int = 100):
    now = now or datetime.now(UTC)
    status = json.loads((root / "api/status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    generated = datetime.fromisoformat(
        status.get("code_deployed_at", status["generated_at"]).replace("Z", "+00:00")
    )
    if generated.tzinfo is None or not -300 <= (now - generated).total_seconds() <= 900:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Publication status is missing a recent timezone-aware generated_at"
        )
    total = 0
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        groups = json.loads(
            (root / f"api/groups/{section}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )["groups"]
        if not groups:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Empty group catalog: {section}")
        total += len(groups)
        for group in groups:
            path = root / f"api/schedule/{section}/{group['id']}.json"
            if not path.is_file():
                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing schedule: {section}/{group['id']}")
    if total < minimum_groups:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Too few groups: {total} < {minimum_groups}")
    if status.get("schema_version", 1) not in (1, 2) or not status.get("build_id"):
        raise RuntimeError("Missing publication schema/build identity")
    print(
        f"Publication smoke OK: {total} groups, recent status, compatible schema, build {status['build_id']}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    check_site(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "dist")


===== scripts/source_artifact.py =====
SHA-256: a3c8ca8dfa050a9046a2769c937b1b4ef92fa3603abac14eeadebfa69a155efe

"""Restore a bounded retained source artifact; never use PR artifacts as trusted input."""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
import shutil
from pathlib import Path


def run_gh(*args):
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["gh", *args], capture_output=True, text=True, env=os.environ.copy()
    )
    if result.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError("Cannot read retained source artifact")
    return result.stdout


def restore_artifact(repository, destination, expected=None, name="source-snapshot"):
    destination = Path(destination)
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    data = json.loads(
        run_gh("api", f"repos/{repository}/actions/artifacts?name={name}&per_page=20")
    )
    for artifact in data["artifacts"][:20]:
        if (
            artifact["expired"]
            or artifact.get("workflow_run", {}).get("head_branch") != "main"
        ):
            continue
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=destination.parent) as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            run_gh(
                "run",
                "download",
                str(artifact["workflow_run"]["id"]),
                "--repo",
                repository,
                "--name",
                name,
                "--dir",
                str(root),
            )
            manifests = list(root.rglob("sources.json"))
            source = next(
                (
                    path.parent
                    for path in manifests
                    if (path.parent / "bakalavriat").exists()
                ),
                None,
            )
            if not source:
                continue
            if expected and any(
                not (source / path).is_file()
                or hashlib.sha256((source / path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                != entry["sha256"]
                for path, entry in expected.items()
            ):
                continue
            shutil.copytree(source, destination, dirs_exist_ok=True)
            if expected:
                from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic

                write_json_atomic(destination / "sources.json", expected)
            print(
                "Restored source artifact",
                artifact["id"],
                "with verified source hashes" if expected else "",
            )
            return True
    return False


def restore_progress(repository, destination):
    """Resume only newer, hash-verified files; never replace the published API."""
    from datetime import datetime
    from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic

    destination = Path(destination)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=destination.parent) as temporary:
        root = Path(temporary) / "progress"
        if not restore_artifact(repository, root):
            return
        previous = json.loads(
            (destination / "sources.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )
        incoming = json.loads((root / "sources.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        copied = 0
        for name, entry in incoming.items():
            # Source manifests can only refer to a single section and an HTML file.
            relative = Path(name)
            if (
                relative.is_absolute()
                or len(relative.parts) != 2
                or ".." in relative.parts
                or relative.suffix != ".htm"
            ):
                continue
            source = root / relative
            if not source.is_file() or hashlib.sha256(
                source.read_bytes()
            ).hexdigest() != entry.get("sha256"):
                continue
            try:
                checked = datetime.fromisoformat(entry["retrieved_at"])
                old = datetime.fromisoformat(
                    previous.get(name, {}).get("retrieved_at")
                    or "1970-01-01T00:00:00+00:00"
                )
                if checked <= old:
                    continue
            except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
                continue
            (destination / relative).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(source, destination / relative)
            previous[name] = entry
            copied += 1
        # Negative observations never overwrite a last good positive source.
        for rejected in (root / ".negative").glob("*/*.json"):
            entry = json.loads(rejected.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            raw = rejected.with_suffix(".htm")
            relative = (
                rejected.relative_to(root / ".negative").with_suffix(".htm").as_posix()
            )
            current = (
                destination / ".negative" / rejected.relative_to(root / ".negative")
            )
            saved = (
                json.loads(current.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                if current.exists()
                else {}
            )
            try:
                stamp = datetime.fromisoformat(entry["checked_at"])
                latest = max(
                    datetime.fromisoformat(value or "1970-01-01T00:00:00+00:00")
                    for value in (
                        saved.get("checked_at"),
                        previous.get(relative, {}).get("retrieved_at"),
                    )
                )
                if (
                    stamp <= latest
                    or not raw.is_file()
                    or hashlib.sha256(raw.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                    != entry.get("sha256")
                ):
                    continue
            except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
                continue
            current.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(raw, current.with_suffix(".htm"))
            shutil.copy2(rejected, current)
        write_json_atomic(destination / "sources.json", previous)
        print("Resumed newer verified source files:", copied)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    restore_progress(os.environ["GITHUB_REPOSITORY"], Path("demo_data"))

def build_raspisan_table_html(courses_dict):
    max_rows = max(len(v) for v in courses_dict.values())
    header_tds = "".join(
        f'  <TD WIDTH="17%" VALIGN="TOP"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE=5 COLOR="#ff00ff"><P ALIGN="CENTER"> {c} курс </FONT></TD>\n'
        for c in range(1, 7)
    )
    body_trs = []
    for r in range(max_rows):
        tds = []
        for c in range(1, 7):
            items = courses_dict.get(c, [])
            if r < len(items):
                gid, gname = items[r]
                tds.append(
                    f'  <TD><P ALIGN="CENTER"><A HREF="{gid}.htm"><FONT FACE="Times New Roman">{gname}</FONT></A></TD>'
                )
            else:
                tds.append('  <TD><P ALIGN="CENTER"></TD>')
        body_trs.append("<TR>\n" + "\n".join(tds) + "\n</TR>")

    return f"""<HTML>
<HEAD><META HTTP-EQUIV="Content-Type" CONTENT="text/html; charset=windows-1251"><TITLE>raspisan</TITLE></HEAD>
<BODY LINK="#0000ff" VLINK="#800080">
<FONT FACE="Times New Roman" SIZE=6 COLOR="#0000ff"><P>Расписание занятий студентов</P></FONT>
<TABLE BORDER CELLSPACING=1 CELLPADDING=3 WIDTH=623>
<TR>\n{header_tds}</TR>
{chr(10).join(body_trs)}
</TABLE>
</BODY></HTML>"""

def seed_offline_groups():
    sections_map = [
        ("bakalavriat", BAK_COURSES, False),
        ("spezialitet", SPEZ_COURSES, False),
        ("zo1", ZO1_COURSES, True),
        ("zo2", ZO2_COURSES, True),
    ]
    count = 0
    for sec_name, c_dict, is_zo in sections_map:
        sec_dir = DEMO_DIR / sec_name
        sec_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (sec_dir / "raspisan.htm").write_bytes(
            build_raspisan_table_html(c_dict).encode("cp1251")
        )
        for _, g_list in c_dict.items():
            for gid, gname in g_list:
                html_g = build_group_schedule_html(gid, gname, is_zo=is_zo)
                (sec_dir / f"{gid}.htm").write_bytes(html_g.encode("cp1251"))
                count += 1
    print(f"📦 Локальная база предзаполнена: {count} групп во всех 4 разделах!")


def create_ssl_ctx() -> ssl.SSLContext:
    ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
    ctx.check_hostname = False
    ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE
    if hasattr(ssl, "OP_LEGACY_SERVER_CONNECT"):
        ctx.options |= ssl.OP_LEGACY_SERVER_CONNECT
    return ctx


SSL_CTX = create_ssl_ctx()


def download_raw(rel_path: str) -> bytes:
    url = f"{ESSTU_BASE_URL}/{rel_path.lstrip('/')}"
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "ESSTU-Schedule-Sync/2.1"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=8, context=SSL_CTX) as resp:
        return resp.read()


def sync_all_from_portal():
    seed_offline_groups()
    print(
        "🌐 Пробуем синхронизировать живые таблицы напрямую с https://portal.esstu.ru ..."
    )
    total_groups = 0
    for sec in SECTIONS:
        sec_dir = DEMO_DIR / sec
        try:
            raw_index = download_raw(f"{sec}/raspisan.htm")
            (sec_dir / "raspisan.htm").write_bytes(raw_index)
            soup = BeautifulSoup(raw_index.decode("cp1251", errors="replace"), "lxml")
            hrefs = {
                (a.get("href") or "").strip()
                for a in soup.find_all("a")
                if (a.get("href") or "").strip().endswith(".htm")
                and (a.get_text() or "").strip()
            }
            print(f"📂 Раздел [{sec}]: скачиваем {len(hrefs)} групп с портала...")
            for href in sorted(hrefs):
                try:
                    (sec_dir / href).write_bytes(download_raw(f"{sec}/{href}"))
                    total_groups += 1
                except Exception:
                    logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                        "Source synchronization failed: %s/%s", sec, href
                    )
                time.sleep(0.03)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"ℹ️ Раздел [{sec}] работает из локальной базы ({e})")

    if total_groups > 0:
        print(f"✅ С портала ВСГУТУ успешно обновлено групп: {total_groups}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sync_all_from_portal()


===== scripts/sync_departments.py =====
SHA-256: cd671a99f0b1ceba723a961d973b73bbca03166c10743508812da455a17845f4

"""Download only official department pages; retain the last good copy on failure."""

import json
import hashlib
import logging
import re
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import os
import time
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.data.repository import (
    department_schedules as parse_department_teachers_html,
)
from esstu_schedule.data.repository import group_schedule as parse_group_schedule_html
from esstu_schedule.parser import parse_departments_list_html, parse_groups_list_html
from esstu_schedule.identity import resolve_catalog, issue_text
from scripts.sync_runtime import download_targets, require_time, remaining
from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "demo_data"
SECTIONS = ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet")


class SourceDefect(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, issue):
        self.issue = issue
        super().__init__(issue_text(issue))


def validate_page(relative, raw):
    html = raw.decode("cp1251", errors="replace")
    section, name = relative.split("/")
    if name == "craspisanEdt.htm":
        valid = parse_departments_list_html(html, section)
    elif name == "raspisan.htm":
        valid = parse_groups_list_html(html, section)
    elif re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.htm", name):
        schedule = parse_group_schedule_html(html, name[:-4], section)
        catalog = ROOT / section / "raspisan.htm"
        groups = (
            parse_groups_list_html(catalog.read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), section)
            if catalog.exists()
            else []
        )
        if not any(group["id"] == name[:-4] for group in groups):
            raise ValueError("Group identity does not match the current catalog")
        _, issues, _ = resolve_catalog(groups, {name[:-4]: schedule})
        if name[:-4] in issues:
            if issues[name[:-4]]["category"] == "source":
                raise SourceDefect(issues[name[:-4]])
            raise ValueError("Group identity does not match the current catalog")
        valid = not schedule.get("is_incomplete") and bool(
            schedule["weeks"]["1"] or schedule["weeks"]["2"]
        )
    else:
        valid = parse_department_teachers_html(html, name[:-4], section)
    if isinstance(valid, list) and any(item.get("is_incomplete") for item in valid):
        valid = False
    if not valid:
        raise ValueError("Page contains no valid schedule data")


def fetch_page(relative):
    require_time()
    target = ROOT / relative
    rejected = ROOT / ".negative" / relative
    rejected_meta = rejected.with_suffix(".json")
    if rejected_meta.exists():
        saved = json.loads(rejected_meta.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if (
            saved.get("catalog_sha256")
            == hashlib.sha256(
                (ROOT / relative.split("/")[0] / "raspisan.htm").read_bytes()
            ).hexdigest()
        ):
            age = (
                datetime.now(UTC) - datetime.fromisoformat(saved["checked_at"])
            ).total_seconds()
            if 0 <= age < 24 * 3600:
                raise SourceDefect(saved["issue"])
    manifest_path = ROOT / "sources.json"
    manifest = (
        json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if manifest_path.exists()
        else {}
    )
    old = manifest.get(relative, {})
    previous = target.read_bytes() if target.exists() else None
    valid_saved = previous is not None and hashlib.sha256(
        previous
    ).hexdigest() == old.get("sha256")
    failed_before = any(
        relative == row.get("path")
        for report in ROOT.glob("*sync.json")
        for row in json.loads(report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("failed", [])
    )
    if (
        valid_saved
        and not failed_before
        and relative.split("/")[-1] not in ("raspisan.htm", "craspisanEdt.htm")
    ):
        try:
            age = (
                datetime.now(UTC) - datetime.fromisoformat(old["retrieved_at"])
            ).total_seconds()
            if 0 <= age < 25 * 60:
                validate_page(relative, previous)
                return relative, {**old, "reused": True}
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            pass
    headers = {"User-Agent": "ESSTU-Schedule/2.5"}
    if valid_saved:
        if old.get("etag"):
            headers["If-None-Match"] = old["etag"]
        if old.get("last_modified"):
            headers["If-Modified-Since"] = old["last_modified"]
    for attempt in range(3):
        require_time()
        try:
            request = urllib.request.Request(
                "https://portal.esstu.ru/" + relative, headers=headers
            )
            try:
                with urllib.request.urlopen(
                    request, timeout=min(10, remaining())
                ) as response:
                    chunks = []
                    while True:
                        require_time()
                        try:
                            chunk = response.read(65536)
                        except TypeError:
                            chunks = [response.read()]
                            break
                        if not chunk:
                            break
                        chunks.append(chunk)
                    raw = b"".join(chunks)
                    modified = response.headers.get("Last-Modified")
                    etag = response.headers.get("ETag")
                    length = response.headers.get("Content-Length")
                    if length and int(length) != len(raw):
                        raise OSError("Truncated HTTP response")
            except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
                if error.code != 304 or not valid_saved:
                    raise
                raw = previous
                modified = old.get("last_modified")
                etag = old.get("etag")
            try:
                validate_page(relative, raw)
            except SourceDefect as error:
                rejected.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                rejected.write_bytes(raw)
                write_json_atomic(
                    rejected_meta,
                    {
                        "checked_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
                        "issue": error.issue,
                        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
                        "etag": etag,
                        "last_modified": modified,
                        "catalog_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
                            (
                                ROOT / relative.split("/")[0] / "raspisan.htm"
                            ).read_bytes()
                        ).hexdigest(),
                    },
                )
                raise
            if rejected_meta.exists():
                rejected_meta.unlink()
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if raw != previous:
                temporary = target.with_suffix(target.suffix + ".tmp")
                temporary.write_bytes(raw)
                os.replace(temporary, target)
            return relative, {
                "retrieved_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
                "last_modified": modified,
                "etag": etag,
                "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
                "bytes": len(raw),
                "reused": raw == previous,
            }
        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as error:
            if isinstance(error, urllib.error.HTTPError) and error.code < 500:
                raise
            if attempt == 2:
                raise
            time.sleep(min(0.5 * 2**attempt, remaining()))


def sync():
    manifest_path = ROOT / "sources.json"
    manifest = (
        json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if manifest_path.exists()
        else {}
    )
    targets, failures, successes = [], [], []
    for section in SECTIONS:
        relative = f"{section}/craspisanEdt.htm"
        try:
            path, meta = fetch_page(relative)
            manifest[path] = meta
            write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
            successes.append(path)
        except Exception as exc:
            logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                "Source synchronization failed: %s", relative
            )
            failures.append({"path": relative, "error": str(exc)})
            # A failed live catalog is not a reason to scan every stale page on an unreachable host.
            continue
        index = ROOT / relative
        if index.exists():
            for institute in parse_departments_list_html(
                index.read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), section
            ):
                for department in institute["departments"]:
                    if re.fullmatch(r"Caf\d+", department["caf_id"], re.IGNORECASE):
                        targets.append(f"{section}/{department['caf_id']}.htm")
    for relative, result, error in download_targets(targets, fetch_page):
        if error:
            failures.append(
                {
                    "path": relative,
                    "error": str(error),
                    "reason": error.issue["reason"]
                    if isinstance(error, SourceDefect)
                    else "download_failed",
                }
            )
        else:
            path, meta = result
            manifest[path] = meta
            write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
            successes.append(path)
    write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
    report = {
        "checked_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "downloaded": successes,
        "failed": failures,
        "department_pages_expected": len(set(targets)),
        "deadline_reached": remaining() <= 0,
        "deferred": sorted(
            set(targets) - set(successes) - {row["path"] for row in failures}
        ),
    }
    write_json_atomic(ROOT / "department-sync.json", report)
    print(f"Official pages downloaded: {len(successes)}; failed: {len(failures)}")
    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sync()


===== scripts/sync_esstu.py =====
SHA-256: 6b9e88ac0b793e9ab3498d41825319628a63702e52ab63dcbf54d45c93e961c8

"""Refresh official department and group snapshots without generating test data."""

import json
import logging
import os
import re
from scripts.sync_runtime import download_targets, remaining, set_deadline
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic
from esstu_schedule.parser import SECTIONS, parse_groups_list_html
from scripts.sync_departments import ROOT, fetch_page
from scripts.sync_departments import sync as sync_departments


def sync_groups():
    manifest_path = ROOT / "sources.json"
    manifest = (
        json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if manifest_path.exists()
        else {}
    )
    targets, failures, successes = [], [], []
    for section in SECTIONS:
        relative = f"{section}/raspisan.htm"
        try:
            path, meta = fetch_page(relative)
            manifest[path] = meta
            write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
            successes.append(path)
        except Exception as exc:
            logging.getLogger(__name__).exception(
                "Source synchronization failed: %s", relative
            )
            failures.append({"path": relative, "error": str(exc)})
            continue
        index = ROOT / relative
        if index.exists():
            groups = parse_groups_list_html(
                index.read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), section
            )
            targets.extend(
                f"{section}/{g['id']}.htm"
                for g in groups
                if re.fullmatch(r"\d+", str(g["id"]))
            )
    from scripts.sync_departments import SourceDefect

    for relative, result, error in download_targets(targets, fetch_page):
        if error:
            failures.append(
                {
                    "path": relative,
                    "error": str(error),
                    "reason": error.issue["reason"]
                    if isinstance(error, SourceDefect)
                    else "download_failed",
                }
            )
        else:
            path, meta = result
            manifest[path] = meta
            write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
            successes.append(path)
    write_json_atomic(manifest_path, manifest)
    report = {
        "checked_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "downloaded": successes,
        "failed": failures,
        "group_pages_expected": len(set(targets)),
        "deadline_reached": remaining() <= 0,
        "deferred": sorted(
            set(targets) - set(successes) - {row["path"] for row in failures}
        ),
    }
    write_json_atomic(ROOT / "group-sync.json", report)
    print(f"Group pages downloaded: {len(successes)}; failed: {len(failures)}")
    return report


def sync_all():
    set_deadline(float(os.environ.get("ESSTU_SYNC_SECONDS", "360")))
    sync_departments()
    return sync_groups()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sync_all()


===== scripts/sync_runtime.py =====
SHA-256: 84589ca0e55a4342e30b37e2c6a5482494e5f3bd14c51c60fe9cad90f1cfb11c

"""A shared soft deadline stops scheduling work and lets publication continue."""

import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, wait, FIRST_COMPLETED

_deadline = None


def set_deadline(seconds):
    global _deadline
    _deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds


def remaining():
    return (
        max(0, _deadline - time.monotonic()) if _deadline is not None else float("inf")
    )


def require_time():
    if remaining() <= 0:
        raise TimeoutError("Synchronization soft deadline reached")


def download_targets(targets, fetch):
    targets = iter(sorted(set(targets)))
    pending = {}
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:

        def fill():
            while len(pending) < 4 and remaining() > 0:
                path = next(targets, None)
                if path is None:
                    break
                pending[pool.submit(fetch, path)] = path

        fill()
        while pending:
            completed, _ = wait(
                pending, timeout=min(1, remaining()), return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED
            )
            for future in completed:
                path = pending.pop(future)
                try:
                    yield path, future.result(), None
                except Exception as error:
                    yield path, None, error
            if remaining() <= 0:
                for future, path in list(pending.items()):
                    if future.cancel():
                        pending.pop(future)
                # At most four in-flight reads complete with their bounded timeout.
                for future, path in list(pending.items()):
                    try:
                        yield path, future.result(), None
                    except Exception as error:
                        yield path, None, error
                break
            fill()


===== scripts/verify_deployment.py =====
SHA-256: e6dcdc7b6c7566794e29dba9b05d57f37c3f6e52d53f4dc76b2c910b7152b26e

"""Assert that deployed HTML and API belong to the tested shell."""

import argparse
import json
import re
import time
import urllib.request


def verify(base, expected, attempts=5):
    for attempt in range(attempts):
        try:

            def fetch(path):
                request = urllib.request.Request(
                    base.rstrip("/")
                    + "/"
                    + path
                    + "?verification="
                    + str(time.time_ns()),
                    headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"},
                )
                with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
                    return response.read().decode("utf-8")

            status = json.loads(fetch("api/status.json"))
            html = fetch("index.html")
            version = re.search(r'content="([^"]+)" name="app-version"', html)
            if (
                status.get("build_id") != expected
                or not version
                or version[1] != expected
            ):
                raise RuntimeError("Published build differs from tested build")
            print("Published HTML/API match tested build:", expected)
            return
        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):
            if attempt + 1 == attempts:
                raise
            time.sleep(2**attempt)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--url", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--expected", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    verify(args.url, args.expected)

// One focus stack for all modal dialogs, including nested dialogs and notes.
const modalStack=[];
let dialogInputMode='keyboard';
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',()=>{dialogInputMode='pointer';document.body.dataset.input='pointer';},true);
document.addEventListener('keydown',()=>{dialogInputMode='keyboard';document.body.dataset.input='keyboard';},true);
function visibleControls(dialog){return [...dialog.querySelectorAll('button,a[href],input,textarea,select,summary,[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')].filter(node=>!node.disabled && !node.closest('[inert]') && node.getClientRects().length);}
function updateModalBackground(){
  const top=modalStack.at(-1)?.dialog;
  for(const child of document.body.children){
    if(child.tagName==='SCRIPT')continue;
    child.inert=!!top && child!==top && !child.contains(top);
  }
  document.body.style.overflow=top?'hidden':'';
}
function presentDialog(id){
  const dialog=document.getElementById(id);if(!dialog)return;
  if(!modalStack.some(entry=>entry.dialog===dialog))modalStack.push({dialog,launcher:document.activeElement});
  const inner=dialog.querySelector('[role="dialog"]');if(inner){inner.removeAttribute('role');inner.removeAttribute('aria-modal');}
  const title=dialog.querySelector('h2,h3');if(title){title.id ||=id+'Title';dialog.setAttribute('aria-labelledby',title.id);}
  dialog.tabIndex=-1;
  for(const button of dialog.querySelectorAll('button'))if(/^[×✕]$/.test(button.textContent.trim())){button.setAttribute('aria-label',button.getAttribute('aria-label') || 'Закрыть окно');button.innerHTML=uiIcon('close');}
  dialog.classList.remove('hidden');dialog.setAttribute('role','dialog');dialog.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');
  const sheet=dialog.querySelector('.simple-sheet');
  if(sheet)prepareSheetHandle(dialog,sheet);
  if(sheet && dialogInputMode==='pointer' && !matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches){
    sheet.classList.add('motion-enter');
    sheet.addEventListener('animationend',()=>sheet.classList.remove('motion-enter'),{once:true});
  }
  updateModalBackground();
  (visibleControls(dialog)[0] || dialog).focus();
}
function dismissDialog(id){
  const index=modalStack.findIndex(entry=>entry.dialog.id===id);
  const entry=index>=0?modalStack.splice(index,1)[0]:null;
  document.getElementById(id)?.dispatchEvent(new Event('sheetreset'));
  document.getElementById(id)?.classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById(id)?.querySelector('.motion-enter')?.classList.remove('motion-enter');
  updateModalBackground();
  const top=modalStack.at(-1)?.dialog;
  if(entry?.launcher?.isConnected && entry.launcher.getClientRects().length && !entry.launcher.closest('[inert]'))entry.launcher.focus();
  else if(top)(visibleControls(top)[0] || top).focus();
  const command=entry?.launcher?.getAttribute('onclick');
  if(command)queueMicrotask(()=>{if(!entry.launcher.isConnected && document.activeElement===document.body){const replacement=[...document.querySelectorAll('button[onclick]')].find(button=>button.getAttribute('onclick')===command && !button.closest('[inert]'));replacement?.focus();}});
}
document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
  const dialog=modalStack.at(-1)?.dialog;if(!dialog)return;
  if(event.key==='Escape'){
    event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if(dialog.id==='noteModal')closeNoteModal();else closeSimpleDialog(dialog.id);
  }else if(event.key==='Tab'){
    const controls=visibleControls(dialog),first=controls[0],last=controls.at(-1);
    if(!first){event.preventDefault();dialog.focus();return;}
    if(event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement===first || !dialog.contains(document.activeElement))){event.preventDefault();last.focus();}
    else if(!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement===last || !dialog.contains(document.activeElement))){event.preventDefault();first.focus();}
  }
},true);

function retryScheduleLoad(){if(startupLinkFailed){location.reload();return;}return appMode==='teacher'?loadTeacherSchedule(savedState.teacherKey,savedState.teacherCafId,true):loadStudentSchedule(savedState.section,savedState.groupId,savedState.groupName,true);}
function relativeChecked(value,now=Date.now()){
  const stamp=Date.parse(value);if(!Number.isFinite(stamp))return 'Дата проверки неизвестна';
  const minutes=Math.floor((now-stamp)/60000);
  if(minutes<0)return 'Проверено '+displayDate(value);
  if(minutes<60)return minutes<1?'Проверено только что':`Проверено ${minutes} мин назад`;
  return `Проверено ${Math.floor(minutes/60)} ч назад`;
}
function daySourceText(schedule,delivery,online=true){
  const offline=!online || (delivery?.cached && !['server-error','refreshing'].includes(delivery.reason));
  const serverError=delivery?.reason==='server-error',refreshing=delivery?.reason==='refreshing';
  const fallback=schedule.is_stale_fallback || ['local_fallback','saved_snapshot'].includes(schedule.source_mode) || serverError || refreshing;
  const checked=relativeChecked(schedule.source_checked_at);
  return `${schedule.is_incomplete?'Данные неполные · ':''}${offline?'Офлайн-копия · ':refreshing?'Обновляется · Сохранённая копия · ':serverError?'Ошибка сервера · Сохранённая копия · ':fallback?'Сохранённая копия · ':''}${checked.replace(/^Проверено /,offline || fallback?'':'Проверено ')}`;
}
function renderDaySource(){
  const target=document.getElementById('daySourceStatus');if(!target || !currentSchedule)return;
  const chip=document.getElementById('compactFreshnessText');
  chip.innerHTML=escapeHTML(daySourceText(currentSchedule,lastDelivery,navigator.onLine))+' '+uiIcon('info');
  chip.title='Источник и время проверки: '+displayDate(currentSchedule.source_checked_at);
  target.dataset.source=(!navigator.onLine || (lastDelivery?.cached && !['server-error','refreshing'].includes(lastDelivery.reason)))?'offline':(lastDelivery?.reason==='server-error' || currentSchedule.is_stale_fallback || ['local_fallback','saved_snapshot'].includes(currentSchedule.source_mode))?'fallback':'fresh';
  const stamp=Date.parse(currentSchedule.source_checked_at);
  target.dataset.stale=String(!Number.isFinite(stamp) || Date.now()-stamp>86400000 || !!currentSchedule.is_incomplete);
  chip.dataset.source=target.dataset.source;
}
