function uiIcon(name){return `<svg class="ui-icon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="${ICON_PATHS[name] || ICON_PATHS.dot}"/></svg>`;}
const EMOJI_ICONS={'ⓘ':'info','⌄':'chevron','🎓':'building','👨‍🏫':'person','👥':'person','📍':'pin','🏛':'building','⭐':'star','☆':'star','🌙':'dot','☀️':'dot','💻':'dot','📅':'calendar','🗂':'calendar','🚪':'door','📆':'calendar','🔗':'link','⏱':'clock','📝':'note','✏️':'note','📌':'note','⚡':'dot','🔄':'refresh','💾':'note','🌐':'link','🚀':'dot','🎉':'check','✅':'check','⚠️':'warning','☕':'clock','🍽':'clock','⏳':'clock','🏃':'pin','🟢':'dot','🔴':'dot','⚪':'dot','⏭':'clock','⏰':'clock','🌴':'dot'};
const emojiPattern=new RegExp(Object.keys(EMOJI_ICONS).sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length).join('|'),'gu');
function replaceDecorativeIcons(root){
  if(root.nodeType===1 && (root.closest('script,style,svg,textarea,option,title,.whitespace-pre-wrap') || root.matches('script,style,svg,textarea,option,title,.whitespace-pre-wrap')))return;
  const nodes=root.nodeType===3?[root]:[...function*(){const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);while(walker.nextNode())yield walker.currentNode;}()];
  for(const node of nodes){
    if(!node.parentElement || node.parentElement.closest('script,style,svg,textarea,option,title,.whitespace-pre-wrap'))continue;
    const matches=[...node.data.matchAll(emojiPattern)];if(!matches.length)continue;
    const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment();let offset=0;
    for(const match of matches){fragment.append(document.createTextNode(node.data.slice(offset,match.index)));const span=document.createElement('span');span.innerHTML=uiIcon(EMOJI_ICONS[match[0]]);fragment.append(span.firstChild);offset=match.index+match[0].length;}
    fragment.append(document.createTextNode(node.data.slice(offset)));node.replaceWith(fragment);
  }
}

function prepareSheetHandle(dialog,sheet){
  if(sheet.querySelector('.sheet-handle'))return;
  const handle=document.createElement('div');handle.className='sheet-handle';handle.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  handle.append(document.createElement('span'));sheet.prepend(handle);
  let drag=null;
  dialog.addEventListener('sheetreset',()=>{drag=null;sheet.style.transform='';sheet.style.transition='';});
  handle.addEventListener('pointerdown',event=>{
    if(innerWidth>700)return;
    drag={id:event.pointerId,y:event.clientY,delta:0};handle.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);sheet.style.transition='none';
  });
  handle.addEventListener('pointermove',event=>{if(!drag || drag.id!==event.pointerId)return;drag.delta=Math.max(0,event.clientY-drag.y);sheet.style.transform=`translateY(${drag.delta}px)`;});
  function finish(cancel=false){
    if(!drag)return;const distance=drag.delta;drag=null;
    sheet.style.transition=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches?'none':'transform 160ms var(--campus-ease-out)';sheet.style.transform='';
    setTimeout(()=>{sheet.style.transition='';},180);
    if(!cancel && distance>80){if(dialog.id==='noteModal')closeNoteModal();else if(dialog.id==='roomsModal')closeRoomsModal();else closeSimpleDialog(dialog.id);}
  }
  handle.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>finish());handle.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>finish(true));
}

SHA-256: 92f2210c3b0a3b382a5536b9af71ac4add89ccef59821a0dab8f7e569cfe05e1

const APP_VERSION = '2.5.0';
let scheduleChanges = [], highlightChanges = true, lastDelivery = null;
function flattenSchedule(schedule) {
  const rows = [];
  for (const [week, days] of Object.entries(schedule.weeks || {})) {
    for (const [day, data] of Object.entries(days)) for (const pair of data.pairs || []) {
      for (const lesson of pair.lessons || []) rows.push({week, day, pair:pair.number, start:pair.start, end:pair.end,
        subject:lesson.subject || '', room:lesson.room || '', teacher:lesson.teacher || '',
        groups:lesson.groups || '', tag:lesson.tag || '', type:lesson.type || ''});
    }
  }
  return rows.sort((a,b) => JSON.stringify(a).localeCompare(JSON.stringify(b)));
}
function compareSchedules(previous, current) {
  if (!previous) return [];
  const before = [...previous], changes = [];
  for (const row of current) {
    const exact = before.findIndex(old => JSON.stringify(old) === JSON.stringify(row));
    if (exact >= 0) { before.splice(exact,1); continue; }
    let index = before.findIndex(old => old.week === row.week && old.day === row.day && old.pair === row.pair && old.subject === row.subject && old.tag === row.tag && old.groups === row.groups);
    // Call something a move only when the remaining subject has one unambiguous match.
    if (index < 0) {
      const matches = before.map((old,i)=>({old,i})).filter(({old}) => old.week === row.week && old.subject === row.subject && old.tag === row.tag && old.groups === row.groups);
      if (matches.length === 1 && current.filter(item => item.week === row.week && item.subject === row.subject && item.tag === row.tag && item.groups === row.groups).length === 1) index = matches[0].i;
    }
    if (index < 0) changes.push({kind:'added',label:'Добавлено занятие',current:row});
    else {
      const old = before.splice(index,1)[0], labels = [];
      if (old.day !== row.day || old.pair !== row.pair || old.start !== row.start || old.end !== row.end) labels.push(`Время: ${old.day}, ${old.start} → ${row.day}, ${row.start}`);
      if (old.room !== row.room) labels.push(`Аудитория: ${old.room || '—'} → ${row.room || '—'}`);
      if (old.teacher !== row.teacher) labels.push(`Преподаватель: ${old.teacher || '—'} → ${row.teacher || '—'}`);
      if (old.type !== row.type) labels.push(`Вид занятия: ${old.type} → ${row.type}`);
      changes.push({kind:'changed',label:labels.join(' • ') || 'Изменены данные занятия',previous:old,current:row});
    }
  }
  return [...changes,...before.map(previous=>({kind:'removed',label:'Занятие удалено из выгрузки',previous}))];
}
function trackSchedule(schedule) {
  lastDelivery = schedule._delivery || null;
  const owner = schedule.mode === 'teacher' ? `teacher:${schedule.teacher_key}` : `${schedule.section}:${schedule.group_name || schedule.group_id}`;
  const key = `esstu_snapshot_v2:${owner}`, rows = flattenSchedule(schedule), signature = JSON.stringify(rows);
  try {
    const previous = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || 'null');
    if (previous && JSON.stringify(previous.rows) === signature) scheduleChanges = previous.changes || [];
    else {
      scheduleChanges = compareSchedules(previous?.rows, rows);
      localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify({rows,changes:scheduleChanges}));
    }
  } catch { scheduleChanges = []; }
}
function hasDayChanges(week,day) {
  return highlightChanges && scheduleChanges.some(change => [change.current,change.previous].some(row=>row && String(row.week)===String(week) && row.day===day));
}

  const changes = highlightChanges ? scheduleChanges.filter(c => c.current && String(c.current.week)===String(selectedWeek) && c.current.day===selectedDayKey && c.current.pair===pair && c.current.subject===lesson.subject) : [];
  return `<div class="mt-2">${source}${changes.map(c=>`<div class="text-xs text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-300 mt-1">⚡ ${escapeHTML(c.label)}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}
function displayDate(value) {
  if (!value || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value))) return 'дата неизвестна';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU',{timeZone:'Asia/Irkutsk',dateStyle:'short',timeStyle:'short'}).format(new Date(value));
}

async function scheduleJSON(response) {
  if (!response.ok) throw Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
  const data = await response.json();
  const reason=response.headers.get('X-ESSTU-Cache');
  data._delivery = {cached:['offline','server-error','refreshing'].includes(reason), reason, at:new Date().toISOString()};
  return data;
}

async function forceRefreshSchedule() {
  const button = document.getElementById('refreshSchedule'); button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Проверка…';
  try {
    const ok = appMode==='student' ? await loadStudentSchedule(savedState.section,savedState.groupId,savedState.groupName,true) : await loadTeacherSchedule(savedState.teacherKey,savedState.teacherCafId,true);
    if (!ok) throw Error();
    updateExperienceUI();
  } catch { document.getElementById('freshnessBadge').textContent='Не удалось обновить. Попробуйте позже.'; }
  finally { button.disabled=false;button.textContent='🔄 Обновить'; }
}

  const now=getUlanUdeNow(),day=(now.getDay()+6)%7,minutes=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();

  target.replaceChildren();

  }
}

async function applyAppUpdate(event) {
  const button=event?.currentTarget || document.getElementById('applyAppUpdateButton');
  button.disabled=true;button.textContent='Обновление…';
  if(!pendingWorker){reloadAppOnce();return;}
  updateFallbackTimer=setTimeout(reloadAppOnce,4000);
  try{pendingWorker.postMessage({type:'SKIP_WAITING'});}
  catch(error){console.warn('Не удалось активировать обновление',error);reloadAppOnce();}
}
async function initAppUpdates() {
  if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return;
  let hadController=!!navigator.serviceWorker.controller;
  navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange',()=>{
    if(hadController)reloadAppOnce();
    hadController=true;
  });
  try {
    const registration=await navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js',{updateViaCache:'none'});
    if (registration.waiting) showUpdateBanner(registration.waiting);
    registration.addEventListener('updatefound',()=>{
      const worker=registration.installing;
      worker?.addEventListener('statechange',()=>{ if(worker.state==='installed' && navigator.serviceWorker.controller) showUpdateBanner(worker); });
    });
    const check=()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='visible')registration.update().catch(()=>{});};
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',check);
    setInterval(check,15*60000);
  } catch (error) { console.error('Не удалось включить офлайн-режим',error); }
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  document.getElementById('applyAppUpdateButton').addEventListener('click',applyAppUpdate);
  initAppUpdates();
  window.addEventListener('online',updateExperienceUI);window.addEventListener('offline',updateExperienceUI);
});

let catalogQuality = null;
function renderDataQuality() {
  const ageWarning=document.getElementById('publicationFreshnessWarning');
  const generated=Date.parse(catalogQuality?.generated_at);
  if(ageWarning){
    const stale=Number.isFinite(generated) && Date.now()-generated>12*3600000;
    ageWarning.hidden=!stale;
    ageWarning.textContent=stale?'Выгрузка не обновлялась более 12 часов. Проверьте занятия на портале ВСГУТУ.':'';
  }
  const warning=document.getElementById('incompleteWarning');
  if(warning){warning.classList.toggle('hidden',!currentSchedule?.is_incomplete && !currentSchedule?.incomplete_reason);warning.textContent=currentSchedule?.is_incomplete?'Данные неполные: '+scheduleIssueText(currentSchedule):'Показана сохранённая копия: '+scheduleIssueText(currentSchedule);}
  const status=document.getElementById('parseQualityStatus');
  if(status)status.textContent=catalogQuality ? `Ошибок разбора: ${catalogQuality.parse_error_count ?? 0} · Неполных расписаний: ${catalogQuality.incomplete_schedule_count ?? '—'} · Ячеек для сверки: ${catalogQuality.review_cell_count ?? catalogQuality.unparsed_cell_count ?? '—'}` : 'Статистика качества пока недоступна';
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',async()=>{
  try { const response=await fetch(getApiUrl('/api/status')); if(!response.ok)throw Error(response.status); catalogQuality=await response.json(); }
  catch(error){console.warn('Статистика разбора недоступна',error);}
  renderDataQuality();
  setInterval(renderDataQuality,60000);
});


function protectOfflineSchedules(){
  if(!navigator.serviceWorker?.controller)return;
  const choices=[currentFavorite(),...getFavorites()].slice(0,30);
  const urls=choices.flatMap(choice=>choice.type==='student'?
    [getApiUrl(`/api/schedule/${choice.section}/${encodeURIComponent(choice.id)}`),getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${choice.section}`)]:
    [getApiUrl('/api/teachers/all?section=bakalavriat')]);
  navigator.serviceWorker.controller.postMessage({type:'PROTECT_DATA',urls:[...new Set(urls.map(url=>new URL(url,location.href).href))]});
}
async function receiveBackgroundData(event){
  const message=event.data;
  if(message?.type!=='DATA_UPDATED')return;
  const url=new URL(message.url,location.href);
  if(url.origin!==location.origin || !url.href.startsWith(new URL('api/',location.href).href))return;
  try{
    const data=await scheduleJSON(await fetch(url,{headers:{'X-ESSTU-Cache-Only':'1'}}));
    if(url.pathname.endsWith('/rooms.json')){roomDirectory=data;if(!document.getElementById('roomsModal')?.classList.contains('hidden'))renderRoomsModalList();}
    else if(data.mode==='student' && appMode==='student' && data.section===savedState.section && String(data.group_id)===String(savedState.groupId)){
      currentSchedule=data;trackSchedule(data);renderAll();
    }else if(Array.isArray(data.teachers)){
      allTeachersDirectory=data.teachers.map(teacher=>({...teacher,_delivery:data._delivery}));
      if(appMode==='teacher'){const current=allTeachersDirectory.find(teacher=>normSearch(teacher.teacher_key)===normSearch(savedState.teacherKey));if(current){currentSchedule=current;trackSchedule(current);renderAll();}}
    }
  }catch(error){console.warn('Не удалось показать фоновое обновление',error);}
}
if('serviceWorker' in navigator)navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('message',receiveBackgroundData);

<script>
    // =========================================================================
    // 1. АВТО-РАСШИФРОВКА КОРПУСОВ И АУДИТОРИЙ ВСГУТУ (ФИШКА ВАРИАНТА 3)
    // =========================================================================
    function decodeEsstuRoom(rawRoom) {
      if (!rawRoom || !String(rawRoom).trim()) return null;
      const raw = String(rawRoom).trim(), board = /и\/д/i.test(raw);
      const clean = raw.replace(/\s*и\/[пд]/ig, '').trim();
      if(/^(?:online|онлайн|дист\.?|д\.о\.?)$/i.test(clean))return {raw,badge:'Онлайн',building:null,location:'Онлайн-занятие',online:true};
      const dash = clean.match(/^(\d{1,2})-(?:\d{2,4}[а-яa-z]?|спорт\d*)$/i);
      const zero = clean.match(/^0([1-9])\d{2}[а-яa-z]?$/i);
      const four = clean.match(/^([1-9])\d{3}[а-яa-z]?$/i);
      // Building prefixes describe the room notation; no floor or street is inferred.
      const id = dash?.[1] || zero?.[1] || four?.[1];
      const building = /вуц/i.test(clean) ? 'ВУЦ' : id ? `Корпус ${Number(id)}` : null;
      return {raw, badge:`ауд. ${raw}`, building,
        location:[building ? `${building} (по обозначению аудитории)` : '', board ? 'Интерактивная доска' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' • ')};
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. ОФИЦИАНАЯ ФОРМУЛА РАСЧЁТА I / II НЕДЕЛИ ВСГУТУ (из menu.htm)
    // =========================================================================
    function getReferenceDate(targetDateTime = undefined) {
      const currentDateTime = targetDateTime === undefined ? new Date() : targetDateTime;
      const currentYear = currentDateTime.getFullYear();
      const currentMonth = currentDateTime.getMonth();
      const referenceYear = currentMonth <= 6 ? currentYear - 1 : currentYear;

      const firstSeptember = new Date(referenceYear, 8, 1);
      const firstSeptemberDayOfWeek = firstSeptember.getDay();

      let referenceMonth = 8;
      let referenceDate = 1;
      switch (firstSeptemberDayOfWeek) {
        case 1: referenceMonth = 8; referenceDate = 1; break;
        case 2: case 3: case 4: case 5:
          referenceMonth = 7; referenceDate = 31 - firstSeptemberDayOfWeek + 2; break;
        case 6: referenceMonth = 8; referenceDate = 3; break;
        case 0: referenceMonth = 8; referenceDate = 2; break;
      }
      return new Date(referenceYear, referenceMonth, referenceDate);
    }

    function getWeekNum(targetDateTime = undefined) {
      const currentDateTime = targetDateTime === undefined ? new Date() : targetDateTime;
      const referenceDate = getReferenceDate(currentDateTime);
      const calendarMillis = date => Date.UTC(date.getFullYear(),date.getMonth(),date.getDate(),date.getHours(),date.getMinutes(),date.getSeconds());
      const diff = (calendarMillis(currentDateTime) - calendarMillis(referenceDate)) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 7);
      return diff >= 0
        ? (Math.trunc(diff) % 2 === 0 ? 1 : 2)
        : (Math.trunc(diff + 1 / 7) % 2 === 0 ? 2 : 1);
    }

    let simOverride = null;

    function getUlanUdeNow() {
      const now = new Date();
      const parts=Object.fromEntries(new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Irkutsk',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'}).formatToParts(now).map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
      const uuDate = new Date(+parts.year,+parts.month-1,+parts.day,+parts.hour,+parts.minute,+parts.second);
      if (simOverride) {
        const currentDayIdx = (uuDate.getDay() + 6) % 7;
        uuDate.setDate(uuDate.getDate() + (simOverride.dayIndex - currentDayIdx));
        uuDate.setHours(simOverride.hours, simOverride.minutes, now.getSeconds(), 0);
      }
      return uuDate;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. ТЕМА ОФОРМЛЕНИЯ И СОСТОЯНИЕ
    // =========================================================================
    let themeMode = localStorage.getItem('esstu_theme') || 'dark';

    function applyTheme() {
      const root = document.documentElement;
      const btn = document.getElementById('themeToggleBtn');
      let effectiveDark = true;
      if (themeMode === 'dark') { effectiveDark = true; btn.textContent = 'Тёмная тема'; }
      else if (themeMode === 'light') { effectiveDark = false; btn.textContent = 'Светлая тема'; }
      else { effectiveDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches; btn.textContent = 'Системная тема'; }
      root.classList.toggle('dark', effectiveDark);
    }

    function cycleTheme() {
      const order = ['dark', 'light', 'system'];
      themeMode = order[(order.indexOf(themeMode) + 1) % order.length];
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_theme', themeMode);
      applyTheme();
    }

    const DAY_KEYS = ['monday', 'tuesday', 'wednesday', 'thursday', 'friday', 'saturday'];

      section: localStorage.getItem('esstu_section') || 'bakalavriat',

    };

    // Заметки
    function getNotesMap() {
      try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('esstu_notes_v1') || '{}'); }
      catch (e) { return {}; }
    }

    let editingNoteKey = null;

    function makeNoteKey(subject, pairNum, dayKey = selectedDayKey) {
      const owner = appMode === 'student' ? savedState.groupName : savedState.teacherKey;
      return `${owner}::${selectedWeek}::${dayKey}::${pairNum}::${subject}`;
    }

    function openNoteModal(noteKey, subjectTitle) {
      editingNoteKey = noteKey;
      const notes = getNotesMap();
      document.getElementById('noteModalTitle').textContent = `📝 Заметка: ${subjectTitle}`;
      document.getElementById('noteTextarea').value = notes[noteKey] || '';
      openSimpleDialog('noteModal');
      document.getElementById('noteTextarea').focus();
    }

    function closeNoteModal() {
      editingNoteKey = null;
      closeSimpleDialog('noteModal');
    }

    function saveCurrentNote() {
      if (!editingNoteKey) return;
      const val = document.getElementById('noteTextarea').value.trim();
      const notes = getNotesMap();
      if (val) notes[editingNoteKey] = val;
      else delete notes[editingNoteKey];
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_notes_v1', JSON.stringify(notes));
      closeNoteModal();
      renderPairsList();
    }

    function deleteCurrentNote() {
      if (!editingNoteKey) return;
      const notes = getNotesMap();
      delete notes[editingNoteKey];
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_notes_v1', JSON.stringify(notes));
      closeNoteModal();
      renderPairsList();
    }

    function filterLessons(lessons) { return (lessons || []).filter(lesson=>matchesSubgroup(lesson,activeSubgroup())); }
    function timeToMinutes(hhmm) {
      const [h, m] = hhmm.split(':').map(Number);
      return h * 60 + m;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. BENTO КАРТОЧКА РЕАЛЬНОГО ВРЕМЕНИ С КОЛЬЦЕВЫМ SVG-ТАЙМЕРОМ
    // =========================================================================
    function updateLiveHero() { updateCampusHero(); }

    // =========================================================================
    // 5. ОТРИСОВКА ТАЙМЛАЙНА С ПЕРЕМЕНАМИ И СЕТКИ НА ВСЮ НЕДЕЛЮ
    // =========================================================================
    function renderHeaderAndNav() {
      const profileCard = document.getElementById('teacherProfileCard');

      if (appMode === 'student') {
        profileCard.classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent = `🎓 ${savedState.groupName}`;
      } else {
        if (currentSchedule && currentSchedule.profile) {
          const p = currentSchedule.profile;
          profileCard.classList.remove('hidden');
          document.getElementById('teacherAvatarImg').src = teacherAvatarURL(p);
          document.getElementById('teacherAvatarImg').onerror = function() { this.onerror=null;this.src=initialsAvatar(p.short_name || p.full_name); };
          document.getElementById('teacherFullName').textContent = p.full_name || p.short_name;
          document.getElementById('teacherDegreeAndDept').textContent = `${p.degree} • ${escapeHTML(p.department)}`;
          document.getElementById('teacherBuildingInfo').textContent = (p.building ? `📍 ${p.building}` : '') + (currentSchedule.partial ? ' • По расписаниям групп: данные могут быть неполными' : '');
          document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent = `👨🏫 ${p.short_name}`;
        }
      }

      // Кнопки недель
      document.getElementById('btnWeek1').className = selectedWeek === 1
        ? 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';
      document.getElementById('btnWeek2').className = selectedWeek === 2
        ? 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';

      // Кнопки вида (День / Неделя)
      document.getElementById('btnViewDay').className = viewMode === 'day'
        ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';
      document.getElementById('btnViewWeek').className = viewMode === 'week'
        ? 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white shadow'
        : 'px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400';

      document.getElementById('dayTabs').classList.toggle('hidden', viewMode === 'week');
    }

    function renderDayTabs() { renderCalendarDays(); }

    function getBadgeStyle(typeCode) {
      switch (typeCode) {
        case 'lecture':
          return 'bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 border-purple-500/20';
        case 'practice':
          return 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30';
        case 'lab':
          return 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 border-amber-500/20';
        case 'sport':
          return 'bg-cyan-500/15 text-cyan-700 dark:text-cyan-300 border-cyan-500/30';
        default:
          return 'bg-slate-500/15 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 border-slate-500/30';
      }
    }

    // Формирует плашку перемены между двумя парами
    function buildBreakIndicator(prevPair, nextPair) {
      if (!prevPair || !nextPair) return '';
      const endPrev = timeToMinutes(prevPair.end);
      const startNext = timeToMinutes(nextPair.start);
      const diffMin = startNext - endPrev;
      if (diffMin <= 0) return '';

      const previous = decodeEsstuRoom(prevPair.lessons?.[0]?.room);
      const next = decodeEsstuRoom(nextPair.lessons?.[0]?.room);
      let label = `☕ Перемена ${diffMin} мин`;
      const transfer = previous?.building && next?.building && previous.building !== next.building
        ? ` • 🏃 Смена корпуса: ${previous.building} → ${next.building}` : '';
      let style = 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-[#0b1524]';

      if (diffMin === 40) {
        label = `🍽 Большой обеденный перерыв • 40 мин (${prevPair.end}–${nextPair.start})`;
        style = 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5';
      } else if (diffMin > 60) {
        const hrs = Math.floor(diffMin / 60);
        const mins = diffMin % 60;
        label = `⏳ Свободное окно между парами • ${hrs} ч ${mins} мин (${prevPair.end}–${nextPair.start})`;
        style = 'text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 border-teal-500/30 bg-teal-500/5';
      }

      return `
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 pl-6 py-0.5">
          <div class="w-0.5 h-4 bg-slate-300 dark:bg-slate-800"></div>
          <span class="text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${style}">
            ${label}${transfer}
          </span>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    function renderPairsList() {
      const container = document.getElementById('pairsContainer');
      if (currentSchedule?.is_incomplete) { container.textContent='Данные неполные — '+scheduleIssueText(currentSchedule); return; }
      if (!currentSchedule) { container.textContent='Загрузка расписания…'; return; }
      if ((hasDatedSchedule(currentSchedule) || viewMode === 'dates') && scheduleTab !== 'session') { renderDatedTimetable(container); return; }
      if (scheduleTab === 'session') { renderSessionTimeline(container); return; }
      if (viewMode === 'week') { renderFullWeekGrid(container); return; }
      renderCompactDay(container);
    }

    function renderFullWeekGrid(container) {
      const weekObj = currentSchedule?.weeks?.[String(selectedWeek)] || {};
      const dayCards = DAY_NAMES.map((d) => {
        const dayData = weekObj[d.key];
        const activePairs = (dayData?.pairs || [])
          .map((p) => ({ ...p, filtered: filterLessons(p.lessons) }))
          .filter((p) => p.filtered.length > 0);

        const rowsHtml = groupOnlinePairs(activePairs).map((block) => {
          const p=activePairs[block.start],last=activePairs[block.end],count=block.end-block.start+1;
          const l = p.filtered[0];
          const extraCount = p.filtered.length > 1 ? ` (+${p.filtered.length - 1})` : '';
          return `
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 py-1.5 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800/60 last:border-0 text-xs">
              <div class="flex items-start gap-2 min-w-0">
                <span class="font-semibold text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 shrink-0">${count>1?`${p.start}–${last.end} · ${count} ${pairWord(count)}`:`${p.number} пара · ${p.start}`}</span>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                  <div class="font-bold truncate">${escapeHTML(displaySubject(l))}${extraCount}</div>
                  <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">${l.type} ${l.teacher ? '• ' + l.teacher : (l.groups || '')}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              ${lessonRoomLabel(l) ? `<span class="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300">${escapeHTML(lessonRoomLabel(l))}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');

        return `
          <div class="rounded-3xl p-4 bg-white dark:bg-[#101c2e] border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <button onclick="selectDayAndSwitch('${d.key}')" class="font-extrabold text-sm text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 hover:underline">
                ${dayData?.day_title || d.full} →
              </button>
              <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">${activePairs.length} ${pairWord(activePairs.length)}</span>
            </div>
            ${rowsHtml || '<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 italic py-2">Выходной / пар нет</div>'}
          </div>
        `;
      });

      container.innerHTML = `<div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">${dayCards.join('')}</div>`;
    }

    function selectDayAndSwitch(dayKey) {
      selectedDayKey = dayKey;
      setViewMode('day');
    }

    function setViewMode(mode) {
      scheduleTab = 'semester';
      viewMode = mode;
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_view_mode', mode);
      renderAll();
    }

    function bottomNavToday() {
      scheduleTab = 'semester';
      const now = getUlanUdeNow();
      selectedWeek = getWeekNum(now);
      const dayIdx = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
      selectedDayKey = dayIdx < 6 ? DAY_KEYS[dayIdx] : 'monday';
      viewMode = 'day';
      renderAll();
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }

    function bottomNavToggleWeekView() {
      setViewMode(viewMode === 'day' ? 'week' : 'day');
    }

    function switchModeAndOpen(targetMode) {
      if (targetMode === 'student') {
        toggleGroupModal(true);
      } else {
        toggleTeacherModal(true);
      }
    }

    function renderAll() {
      renderFavoritesBar();
      renderHeaderAndNav();
      renderDayTabs();
      renderPairsList();
      updateLiveHero();
      updateExperienceUI();
      updateCompactUI();
      updateStudyUI();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 6. ЗАГРУЗКА ДАННЫХ И МОДАЛЬНЫЕ ОКНА (Поддержка Localhost + GitHub Pages)
    // =========================================================================
    function getApiUrl(path) {
      const isGhPages = window.location.hostname.includes('github.io') || window.location.pathname.startsWith('/esstu-schedule');
      const base = isGhPages ? '/esstu-schedule/api' : '/api';
      if (!isGhPages) {
        return path;
      }
      if (path === '/api/catalog-status') return `${base}/catalog-status.json`;
      if (path === '/api/rooms') return `${base}/rooms.json`;
      // GitHub Pages / Static JSON mapping
      if (path.startsWith('/api/groups')) {
        const sec = (path.match(/section=([^&]+)/) || [])[1] || 'bakalavriat';
        return `${base}/groups/${sec}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/schedule/')) {
        const parts = path.replace('/api/schedule/', '').split('?')[0].split('/');
        return `${base}/schedule/${parts[0]}/${parts[1]}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/departments')) {
        const sec = (path.match(/section=([^&]+)/) || [])[1] || 'bakalavriat';
        return `${base}/departments/${sec}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/teachers/all')) {
        const sec = (path.match(/section=([^&]+)/) || [])[1] || 'bakalavriat';
        return `${base}/teachers/all/${sec}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/department/')) {
        const parts = path.replace('/api/department/', '').split('?')[0].split('/');
        return `${base}/department/${parts[0]}/${parts[1]}.json`;
      }
      if (path.startsWith('/api/status')) {
        return `${base}/status.json`;
      }
      return path;
    }

    async function loadGroups(section = 'bakalavriat') {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${section}`));
        const data = await res.json();
        allGroups = data.groups || [];
        renderSectionTabs();
        renderGroupsModalList();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Ошибка загрузки групп:', e);
      }
    }

    async function loadStudentSchedule(section, groupId, groupName = null, forceFresh = false) {
      const version=beginScheduleLoad(),previous=currentSchedule;
      try {
        if(groupName){
          const catalogResponse=await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${section}`),{cache:forceFresh?'reload':'default'});
          if(!catalogResponse.ok)throw Error('Каталог групп недоступен');
          const catalog=(await catalogResponse.json()).groups || [];
          const actual=catalog.find(group=>normSearch(group.name)===normSearch(groupName));
          if(!actual)throw Error('Выбранная группа не найдена в каталоге');
          groupId=actual.id;
        }
        const nameParam = groupName ? `?group_name=${encodeURIComponent(groupName)}` : '';
        const res = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/schedule/${section}/${encodeURIComponent(groupId)}${nameParam}`), {cache:forceFresh ? 'reload' : 'default'});
        const data = await scheduleJSON(res);
        if(groupName && normSearch(data.group_name)!==normSearch(groupName))throw Error('Название группы не совпало с источником');
        if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        finishScheduleLoad(version);
        appMode = 'student';
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_mode', 'student');
        currentSchedule = data;
        trackSchedule(data);

        const finalName = data.group_name || groupName || groupId;
        const resolvedId = data.resolved_group_id || data.group_id || groupId;
        savedState.section = section;
        savedState.groupId = resolvedId;
        savedState.groupName = finalName;

        localStorage.setItem('esstu_section', section);
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_group_id', resolvedId);
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_group_name', finalName);

        if (data.last_modified) {
          document.getElementById('lastModifiedText').textContent = `Источник: portal.esstu.ru • ${data.last_modified}`;
        }
        rememberRecentSchedule();
        renderAll();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Ошибка загрузки расписания:', e);
        if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        if(forceFresh && previous){currentSchedule={...previous,is_stale_fallback:true};renderAll();showToast('Источник недоступен. Показана сохранённая версия.');}
        else{currentSchedule=null;document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent=groupName || groupId;scheduleUnavailable('Источник расписания недоступен');}
        return false;
      } finally {finishScheduleLoad(version);}
    }

    let teacherDirectoryLoading = null;
    let teacherDirectoryError = '';
    let selectedCafFilter = '';

    function normSearch(value) {
      return String(value || '').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase().replace(/ё/g, 'е').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    }
    function teacherMatches(t, query, caf = '') {
      if (caf && !(t.caf_ids || [t.caf_id]).some(id => normSearch(id) === normSearch(caf))) return false;
      const p = t.profile || {};
      const lessons = Object.values(t.weeks || {}).flatMap(days => Object.values(days).flatMap(d => (d.pairs || []).flatMap(p => p.lessons || [])));
      const text = normSearch([t.teacher_key, p.full_name, p.short_name, p.department, p.degree, p.building, ...(t.caf_ids || [t.caf_id]),
        ...lessons.map(l => `${l.subject || ''} ${l.room || ''} ${decodeEsstuRoom(l.room)?.building || ''}`)].join(' '));
      return normSearch(query).split(' ').every(word => text.includes(word));
    }
    async function loadDepartmentsAndTeachers(forceFresh = false) {
      if (teacherDirectoryLoading) return teacherDirectoryLoading;
      teacherDirectoryLoading = (async () => {
        teacherDirectoryError = '';
        try {
          const results = await Promise.allSettled([
            fetch(getApiUrl('/api/departments?section=bakalavriat')).then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw Error(); return r.json(); }),
            fetch(getApiUrl('/api/teachers/all?section=bakalavriat'),{cache:forceFresh ? 'reload' : 'default'}).then(scheduleJSON),
          ]);
          if (results[0].status === 'fulfilled') departmentsHierarchy = results[0].value.institutes || [];
          if (results[1].status === 'fulfilled') allTeachersDirectory = (results[1].value.teachers || []).map(t=>({...t,_delivery:results[1].value._delivery}));
          else if (!allTeachersDirectory.length) teacherDirectoryError = 'Не удалось загрузить преподавателей. Проверьте соединение и повторите загрузку.';
          if (forceFresh && results[1].status !== 'fulfilled') throw Error('Не удалось проверить новую версию каталога');
        } finally { teacherDirectoryLoading = null; renderTeacherModalContent(); }
      })();
      renderTeacherModalContent();
      return teacherDirectoryLoading;
    }

    async function loadTeacherSchedule(teacherKey, cafId = '', forceFresh = false) {
      const version=beginScheduleLoad(),previous=currentSchedule;
      try {
        if (forceFresh || !allTeachersDirectory.length) await loadDepartmentsAndTeachers(forceFresh);
        let found = allTeachersDirectory.find(t => normSearch(t.teacher_key) === normSearch(teacherKey));
        if (!found && cafId) {
          const res = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/department/bakalavriat/${encodeURIComponent(cafId)}?merge=true`));
          if (res.ok) found = ((await res.json()).teachers || []).find(t => normSearch(t.teacher_key) === normSearch(teacherKey));
        }
        if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        if(!found)throw Error('Преподаватель не найден в сохранённых расписаниях');
        appMode = 'teacher'; localStorage.setItem('esstu_mode', 'teacher');
        savedState.teacherKey = found.teacher_key; savedState.teacherCafId = found.caf_id || '';
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_teacher_key', savedState.teacherKey);
        localStorage.setItem('esstu_teacher_caf', savedState.teacherCafId);
        currentSchedule = found; trackSchedule(found);finishScheduleLoad(version);rememberRecentSchedule();renderAll();return true;
      }catch(error){
        console.error(error);if(version!==scheduleLoadVersion)return false;
        if(forceFresh && previous){currentSchedule={...previous,is_stale_fallback:true};renderAll();showToast('Не удалось обновить преподавателя.');}
        else{currentSchedule=null;scheduleUnavailable('Расписание преподавателя недоступно');}
        return false;
      }finally{finishScheduleLoad(version);}
    }

    function openSelectorModal() {
      if (appMode === 'student') toggleGroupModal(true);
      else toggleTeacherModal(true);
    }

    function openTeacherFromLesson(teacherKey, cafId) {
      if (!teacherKey) return;
      loadTeacherSchedule(teacherKey, cafId || 'Caf5');
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }

    function toggleGroupModal(show) { if (show) openSearchModal('student'); else closeSearchModal(); }
    function toggleTeacherModal(show) { if (show) openSearchModal('teacher'); else closeSearchModal(); }

    function changeStudentSection(sec) {
      savedState.section = sec;
      loadGroups(sec);
    }

    function renderSectionTabs() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.sec-btn').forEach((btn) => {
        const s = btn.getAttribute('data-sec');
        btn.className = s === savedState.section
          ? 'sec-btn shrink-0 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white'
          : 'sec-btn shrink-0 px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300';
      });
    }

    function setCourseFilter(course) {
      selectedCourseFilter = course;
      document.querySelectorAll('.course-btn').forEach((btn) => {
        const c = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-course'));
        btn.className = c === course
          ? 'course-btn shrink-0 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-teal-600 text-white'
          : 'course-btn shrink-0 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800';
      });
      renderGroupsModalList();
    }

    function renderGroupsModalList() {
      const q = (document.getElementById('groupSearchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const container = document.getElementById('groupsListContainer');

      const filtered = allGroups.filter((g) => {
        if (selectedCourseFilter !== 0 && g.course !== selectedCourseFilter) return false;
        if (q && !g.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
        return true;
      }).sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.course)||99)-(Number(b.course)||99) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name,'ru',{numeric:true}));

      if (!filtered.length) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div class="col-span-2 text-center text-xs text-slate-400 py-6">Группы не найдены</div>`;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = filtered.map((g) => `
        <button
          onclick="chooseGroup('${g.section}', '${g.id}', '${g.name.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')"
          aria-pressed="${g.section===savedState.section && g.name===savedState.groupName && appMode==='student'}"
          class="p-2.5 rounded-2xl border text-left transition ${
            g.name === savedState.groupName && appMode === 'student'
              ? 'bg-teal-600/15 border-teal-500'
              : 'bg-slate-100/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/70 hover:border-teal-500/50'
          }"
        >
          <div class="group-name">${escapeHTML(g.name)}${g.section===savedState.section && g.name===savedState.groupName && appMode==='student'?`<span class="group-current">${uiIcon('check')}<span class="sr-only">Выбрана</span></span>`:''}</div>
          <div class="group-course">${g.course} курс</div>
        </button>
      `).join('');
    }

    function chooseGroup(section, id, name) {
      toggleGroupModal(false);
      loadStudentSchedule(section, id, name);
    }

    function renderTeacherModalContent() {
      const q = (document.getElementById('teacherSearchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const container = document.getElementById('teacherModalContent');

      const filteredTeachers = allTeachersDirectory.filter(t => teacherMatches(t, q, selectedCafFilter));

      visibleTeacherChoices = filteredTeachers;
      const teachersCards = filteredTeachers.map((t, teacherIndex) => {
        const p = t.profile || {};
        return `
          <button
            onclick="chooseTeacherByIndex(${teacherIndex})"
            class="w-full p-2.5 rounded-2xl border flex items-center gap-3 text-left transition ${
              t.teacher_key === savedState.teacherKey && appMode === 'teacher'
                ? 'bg-teal-600/15 border-teal-500'
                : 'bg-slate-100/70 dark:bg-slate-800/60 border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/70 hover:border-teal-500/50'
            }"
          >
            <img src="${escapeHTML(teacherAvatarURL(p))}" data-name="${escapeHTML(p.short_name || p.full_name)}" onerror="this.onerror=null;this.src=initialsAvatar(this.dataset.name)" alt="" class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl object-cover shrink-0 bg-slate-700" />
            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div class="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm truncate">${escapeHTML(p.full_name || p.short_name)}</div>
              <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate">${escapeHTML(p.degree || "")}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-teal-500">${getTeacherLiveStatus(t)}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300">${escapeHTML((t.caf_ids?.length ? t.caf_ids : [t.caf_id]).filter(Boolean).map(id => `🏛 ${id}.htm`).join(' • ') || '📄 По расписаниям групп')}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400 truncate">${escapeHTML(p.department)}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">${escapeHTML([...new Set(Object.values(t.weeks || {}).flatMap(w => Object.values(w).flatMap(d => (d.pairs || []).flatMap(p => (p.lessons || []).map(l => l.subject)))))].join(', '))}</div>
            </div>
          </button>
        `;
      }).join('');

      const deptsHtml = departmentsHierarchy.map((inst, instituteIndex) => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100/60 dark:bg-slate-800/40 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-1.5">
          <div class="text-xs font-extrabold text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400">${escapeHTML(inst.institute)}</div>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            ${(inst.departments || []).map((d, departmentIndex) => `
              <button
                onclick="filterCafByIndex(${instituteIndex},${departmentIndex})"
                class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-medium bg-white dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:border-teal-500 transition"
              >
                🏛 ${escapeHTML(d.name)} <span class="opacity-60">(${escapeHTML(d.caf_id)})</span>
              </button>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');

      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="space-y-2">
          <div class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Преподаватели (${filteredTeachers.length})
            ${selectedCafFilter ? `<span class="normal-case text-teal-500">• ${escapeHTML(selectedCafFilter)}</span>` : ''}
            <button onclick="selectedCafFilter='';document.getElementById('teacherSearchInput').value='';renderTeacherModalContent()" class="ml-2 text-teal-500">Сбросить фильтры</button>
          </div>
          <div class="space-y-2">${teachersCards || `<div class="text-xs text-slate-400 py-3 text-center">${teacherDirectoryLoading ? 'Загрузка преподавателей…' : escapeHTML(teacherDirectoryError || 'По запросу преподаватели не найдены. Попробуйте другую фамилию или предмет.')}<button onclick="loadDepartmentsAndTeachers()" class="block mx-auto mt-2 text-teal-500">Повторить загрузку</button></div>`}</div>
        </div>
        <details class="pt-3 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 space-y-2">
          <summary class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 cursor-pointer py-2">Институты и кафедры ВСГУТУ</summary>
          ${deptsHtml}
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">Часть расписаний преподавателей восстановлена из расписаний групп и может быть неполной.</p>
        </details>
      `;
    }

    function filterByCaf(cafId) {
      selectedCafFilter = cafId;
      document.getElementById('teacherSearchInput').value = '';
      renderTeacherModalContent();
    }

    function chooseTeacher(teacherKey, cafId) {
      toggleTeacherModal(false);
      loadTeacherSchedule(teacherKey, cafId);
    }

    function selectWeek(w) { selectedWeek = w; renderAll(); }
    function selectDay(dayKey) { selectedDayKey = dayKey; renderAll(); }

    function saveFiltersAndRender() {
      const hideEmpty = true;
      localStorage.setItem('esstu_hide_empty', String(hideEmpty));
      renderAll();
    }

    function toggleTimeSim() {
      document.getElementById('timeSimPanel').classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function applyTimeSim() {
      const dayIndex = Number(document.getElementById('simDay').value);
      const timeVal = document.getElementById('simTime').value || '09:30';
      const [hours, minutes] = timeVal.split(':').map(Number);
      simOverride = { dayIndex, hours, minutes };
      selectedDayKey = DAY_KEYS[dayIndex];
      renderAll();
    }

    function resetTimeSim() {
      simOverride = null;
      const now = getUlanUdeNow();
      selectedWeek = getWeekNum(now);
      const dayIdx = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
      selectedDayKey = dayIdx < 6 ? DAY_KEYS[dayIdx] : 'monday';
      renderAll();
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
      applyTheme();

      const now = getUlanUdeNow();
      selectedWeek = getWeekNum(now);
      const dayIdx = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7;
      selectedDayKey = dayIdx < 6 ? DAY_KEYS[dayIdx] : 'monday';

      setInterval(updateLiveHero, 1000);
      const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
      if (params.has('group')) {
        appMode = 'student';
        const requested=params.get('group'),section=params.get('section') || 'bakalavriat';
        try {
          if(!['bakalavriat','spezialitet','zo1','zo2'].includes(section))throw Error('Неизвестный раздел в ссылке');
          const response=await fetch(getApiUrl(`/api/groups?section=${section}`));
          if(!response.ok)throw Error('Каталог групп недоступен');
          const groups=(await response.json()).groups || [];
          const group=groups.find(g=>normSearch(g.name)===normSearch(requested) || String(g.id)===requested);
          if(!group)throw Error('Группа из ссылки не найдена');
          savedState.section=section;savedState.groupId=group.id;savedState.groupName=group.name;
        }catch(error){
          startupLinkFailed=true;
          document.getElementById('pairsContainer').setAttribute('aria-busy','false');
          document.getElementById('currentEntityBtnText').textContent=requested || 'Выберите группу';
          scheduleUnavailable(error.message);loadGroups(savedState.section);loadDepartmentsAndTeachers();return;
        }
      } else if (params.has('teacher')) {
        appMode = 'teacher'; savedState.teacherKey = params.get('teacher');
        savedState.teacherCafId = params.get('caf') || 'Caf5';
      }
      loadGroups(savedState.section);
      loadDepartmentsAndTeachers();

      if (appMode === 'teacher') {
        await loadTeacherSchedule(savedState.teacherKey, savedState.teacherCafId);
      } else {
        await loadStudentSchedule(savedState.section, savedState.groupId, savedState.groupName);
      }


      await handleUrlShortcuts();


    });
  </script>

function escapeHTML(value) {
  return String(value || '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
}
function initialsAvatar(name) {
  const initials=String(name || '').split(/\s+/).map(word=>word.match(/\p{L}/u)?.[0] || '').filter(Boolean).slice(0,2).join('').toUpperCase() || 'П';
  const svg=`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 96 96"><rect width="96" height="96" rx="18" fill="#0f2942"/><text x="48" y="58" text-anchor="middle" font-family="sans-serif" font-size="30" fill="#5eead4">${escapeHTML(initials)}</text></svg>`;
  return 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);
}
function teacherAvatarURL(profile) {
  const photo=profile?.photo;
  return typeof photo==='string' && /^avatars\/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.(png|jpe?g|webp)$/i.test(photo) ? photo : initialsAvatar(profile?.short_name || profile?.full_name);
}
let visibleTeacherChoices=[];
function chooseTeacherByIndex(index) {const teacher=visibleTeacherChoices[index];if(teacher)chooseTeacher(teacher.teacher_key,teacher.caf_id || '');}
function filterCafByIndex(instituteIndex,departmentIndex) {const department=departmentsHierarchy[instituteIndex]?.departments?.[departmentIndex];if(department)filterByCaf(department.caf_id);}
function getFavorites() {
  try { const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('esstu_favorites_v1') || '[]'); return Array.isArray(value) ? value : []; } catch { return []; }
}
function currentFavorite() {
  return appMode === 'student' ? {type: appMode, section: savedState.section, id: savedState.groupId, label: savedState.groupName} : {type: appMode, teacherKey: savedState.teacherKey, cafId: savedState.teacherCafId, label: currentSchedule?.profile?.short_name || savedState.teacherKey};
}
function favoriteKey(f) { return f.type === 'student' ? `${f.section}:${f.label}` : `teacher:${f.teacherKey}`; }
function toggleCurrentFavorite() {
  const current = currentFavorite(), list = getFavorites(), key = favoriteKey(current);
  const next = list.some(f => favoriteKey(f) === key) ? list.filter(f => favoriteKey(f) !== key) : [...list, current];
  localStorage.setItem('esstu_favorites_v1', JSON.stringify(next)); renderFavoritesBar();
}
function renderFavoritesBar() {
  const bar = document.getElementById('favoritesBar'); bar.replaceChildren();
  getFavorites().forEach(f => {
    const button = document.createElement('button'); button.textContent = `${f.type === 'student' ? '🎓' : '👨‍🏫'} ${f.label}`;
    button.className = 'shrink-0 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 min-h-[44px] bg-teal-600 text-white';
    button.onclick = () => {
      if(typeof closeSearchModal==='function')closeSearchModal();
      return f.type === 'student' ? loadStudentSchedule(f.section, f.id, f.label) : loadTeacherSchedule(f.teacherKey, f.cafId);
    };
    bar.append(button);
  });
  const selected=getFavorites().some(f => favoriteKey(f) === favoriteKey(currentFavorite()));
  const toggle=document.getElementById('favStarBtn');
  toggle.innerHTML=uiIcon('star')+(selected?' В избранном':' Добавить');
  toggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(selected));
  if(!bar.children.length)bar.innerHTML='<p class="search-empty-hint">Добавьте расписание, чтобы быстро открывать его здесь.</p>';
}
function activeSlots(schedule, now = getUlanUdeNow()) {
  const day = (now.getDay() + 6) % 7, minutes = now.getHours()*60 + now.getMinutes();
  if (day === 6) return [];
  return (schedule.weeks?.[getWeekNum(now)]?.[DAY_KEYS[day]]?.pairs || []).filter(p => minutes >= timeToMinutes(p.start) && minutes < timeToMinutes(p.end));
}
function getTeacherLiveStatus(t) {
  const lessons = activeSlots(t).flatMap(p => p.lessons || []);
  return lessons.length ? escapeHTML('🟢 По расписанию: ' + lessons.map(l => l.room || l.subject).join(', ')) : '⚪ Сейчас нет занятий по расписанию';
}
let roomDirectory = null, roomFilter = 'all', roomTimer;
let roomCheck = null;
const ROOM_BELLS = ['09:00','10:45','13:00','14:45','16:25','18:05'];
function roomContext(now = getUlanUdeNow()) {
  if (roomCheck) return roomCheck;
  return {week:getWeekNum(now), day:(now.getDay()+6)%7, pair:null, minutes:now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes()};
}
function setRoomPreset(preset) {
  roomCheck = null;
  if (preset === 'next') {
    const date = getUlanUdeNow();
    let minutes=date.getHours()*60+date.getMinutes(), index=ROOM_BELLS.findIndex(time=>timeToMinutes(time)>minutes);
    if (index<0 || date.getDay()===0) { date.setDate(date.getDate()+1);index=0; }
    if (date.getDay()===0) date.setDate(date.getDate()+1);
    roomCheck={week:getWeekNum(date),day:(date.getDay()+6)%7,pair:index+1,mode:'next'};
  }
  document.getElementById('roomTimeControls').innerHTML=roomTimeControls();renderRoomsModalList();
}
function setRoomManual() {
  roomCheck={week:Number(document.getElementById('roomWeek').value),day:Number(document.getElementById('roomDay').value),pair:Number(document.getElementById('roomPair').value) || 1,mode:'manual'};
  document.getElementById('roomPair').value=String(roomCheck.pair);
  renderRoomsModalList();
}
function roomTimeControls() {
  const context=roomContext();
  const options=(values,selected)=>values.map(([value,label])=>`<option value="${value}" ${value===selected?'selected':''}>${label}</option>`).join('');
  return `<div class="segmented room-presets" role="group" aria-label="Время проверки"><button data-room-preset="now" aria-pressed="${!roomCheck}" onclick="setRoomPreset('now')">Сейчас</button><button data-room-preset="next" aria-pressed="${context.mode==='next'}" onclick="setRoomPreset('next')">Следующая пара</button></div><div class="room-selects"><select id="roomWeek" aria-label="Неделя проверки" onchange="setRoomManual()">${options([[1,'I неделя'],[2,'II неделя']],context.week)}</select><select id="roomDay" aria-label="День проверки" onchange="setRoomManual()">${options(['Пн','Вт','Ср','Чт','Пт','Сб','Вс'].map((label,i)=>[i,label]),context.day)}</select><select id="roomPair" aria-label="Пара для проверки" onchange="setRoomManual()">${!context.pair?'<option value="0" selected disabled>Текущее время</option>':''}${options(ROOM_BELLS.map((time,i)=>[i+1,`${i+1} пара · ${time}`]),context.pair || 0)}</select></div>`;
}
function roomOccupancy(room, context) {
  const prefix=`${context.week}:${DAY_KEYS[context.day]}:`;
  const lessons=(context.day===6?[]:Object.entries(room.slots).filter(([key])=>key.startsWith(prefix)).flatMap(([,slots])=>Array.isArray(slots)?slots:[slots])).sort((a,b)=>timeToMinutes(a.start)-timeToMinutes(b.start) || timeToMinutes(a.end)-timeToMinutes(b.end) || String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),'ru'));
  return {lessons,busy:lessons.filter(l=>context.pair ? Number(l.pair)===Number(context.pair) : context.minutes>=timeToMinutes(l.start) && context.minutes<timeToMinutes(l.end))};
}
function roomLessonLines(lessons) {
  // Several groups and department schedules may describe the same lesson.
  const rows=new Map();
  for(const lesson of lessons){
    const key=JSON.stringify([lesson.start,lesson.end,lesson.subject]);
    if(!rows.has(key))rows.set(key,lesson);
  }
  return [...rows.values()].map(lesson=>`<li><span class="room-lesson-time">${escapeHTML(lesson.start)}–${escapeHTML(lesson.end)}</span> <span>${escapeHTML(lesson.subject || 'Предмет не указан')}</span></li>`).join('');
}
function roomSlotTimeline(lessons,context) {
  const count=Math.max(ROOM_BELLS.length,...lessons.map(lesson=>Number(lesson.pair)||0));
  return `<div class="room-timeline" aria-label="Слоты дня: заполненные — есть занятия">${Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>{
    const number=i+1,busy=lessons.some(lesson=>Number(lesson.pair)===number),label=`${number} пара${ROOM_BELLS[i]?' · '+ROOM_BELLS[i]:''}: ${busy?'есть занятия':'нет известных занятий'}`;
    return `<span class="room-slot ${busy?'is-busy':''}" data-selected="${Number(context.pair)===number}" title="${label}"><span class="sr-only">${label}</span>${number}</span>`;
  }).join('')}</div>`;
}
function setRoomFilter(filter){roomFilter=filter;renderRoomsModalList();}
async function openRoomsModal(prefill = '') {
  let modal = document.getElementById('roomsModal');
  if (!modal) {
    modal = document.createElement('div'); modal.id = 'roomsModal';
    modal.className = 'simple-overlay hidden';
    modal.innerHTML = `<div class="simple-sheet"><div class="flex justify-between items-center gap-3"><h2 class="font-bold">Радар аудиторий</h2><button onclick="closeRoomsModal()" aria-label="Закрыть" class="w-10">${uiIcon('close')}</button></div><p class="room-hint">Корпуса определены по обозначению аудитории.</p><p class="room-hint">${uiIcon('info')} Занятость по известным расписаниям. Отсутствие пары не гарантирует доступ.</p><input id="roomSearchInput" aria-label="Поиск аудитории" placeholder="Аудитория или корпус" class="w-full rounded-xl p-2" oninput="renderRoomsModalList()"><div class="segmented room-filters" role="group" aria-label="Занятость аудитории"><button data-room-filter="all" onclick="setRoomFilter('all')">Все</button><button data-room-filter="free" onclick="setRoomFilter('free')">Нет пар</button><button data-room-filter="busy" onclick="setRoomFilter('busy')">Есть пары</button></div><div id="roomsListContainer"></div></div>`;
    document.body.append(modal);
  }
  openSimpleDialog('roomsModal'); document.getElementById('roomSearchInput').value = prefill;
  document.getElementById('roomsListContainer').textContent = 'Загрузка индекса аудиторий…';
  try {
    if (!roomDirectory) { const res = await fetch(getApiUrl('/api/rooms')); if (!res.ok) throw Error(); roomDirectory = await res.json(); }
    renderRoomsModalList(); clearInterval(roomTimer); roomTimer = setInterval(renderRoomsModalList, 15000);
  } catch { document.getElementById('roomsListContainer').textContent = 'Не удалось загрузить индекс. Закройте окно и попробуйте снова.'; }
}
function closeRoomsModal() { closeSimpleDialog('roomsModal'); clearInterval(roomTimer); }
function renderRoomsModalList() {
  const context = roomContext();
  let controls=document.getElementById('roomTimeControls');
  if(!controls){controls=document.createElement('div');controls.id='roomTimeControls';document.getElementById('roomSearchInput').before(controls);controls.innerHTML=roomTimeControls();}
  // Keep time presets in sync without replacing the focused manual selectors.
  for(const button of controls.querySelectorAll('[data-room-preset]'))button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(button.dataset.roomPreset==='now'?!roomCheck:context.mode==='next'));
  for(const button of document.querySelectorAll('[data-room-filter]'))button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(button.dataset.roomFilter===roomFilter));
  if(!roomCheck){document.getElementById('roomWeek').value=String(context.week);document.getElementById('roomDay').value=String(context.day);}
  const query = document.getElementById('roomSearchInput').value.toLowerCase().trim();
  const rows = (roomDirectory?.rooms || []).map(room => {
    const {lessons,busy} = roomOccupancy(room,context);
    const building = decodeEsstuRoom(room.room)?.building || 'Корпус не определён';
    return {...room,building,lessons,busy};
  }).filter(room=>`${room.room} ${room.building}`.toLowerCase().includes(query) && (roomFilter!=='free' || !room.busy.length) && (roomFilter!=='busy' || room.busy.length))
    .sort((a,b)=>Number(!!a.busy.length)-Number(!!b.busy.length) || a.building.localeCompare(b.building,'ru',{numeric:true}) || a.room.localeCompare(b.room,'ru',{numeric:true}));
  let previousGroup='';
  const cards=rows.map(room=>{
    const busy=room.busy.length>0,group=`${busy?'busy':'free'}:${room.building}`;
    const heading=group===previousGroup?'':`<h3 class="room-group-title">${busy?(context.pair?'Занятые в выбранную пару':'Занятые сейчас'):(context.pair?'Свободные в выбранную пару':'Свободные сейчас')} · ${escapeHTML(room.building)}</h3>`;
    previousGroup=group;
    const end=busy?room.busy.map(lesson=>lesson.end).sort().at(-1):'';
    const status=busy?`Занята до ${end}`:'Свободна';
    const busyInfo=[...new Set(room.busy.map(lesson=>[lesson.subject,lesson.teacher,lesson.groups].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')))].map(escapeHTML).join('<br>');
    return `${heading}<article class="room-card" data-room="${escapeHTML(room.room)}"><div class="room-card-heading"><h4>${escapeHTML(room.room)}</h4><span class="chip-status chip-status--${busy?'busy':'free'}">${uiIcon(busy?'clock':'check')}${escapeHTML(status)}</span></div>${busyInfo?`<p class="room-busy-info">${busyInfo}</p>`:''}${roomSlotTimeline(room.lessons,context)}<ul class="room-day-lessons">${roomLessonLines(room.lessons)}</ul></article>`;
  });
  const label=`${context.week===1?'I':'II'} неделя · ${['Пн','Вт','Ср','Чт','Пт','Сб','Вс'][context.day]} · ${context.pair ? context.pair+' пара · '+ROOM_BELLS[context.pair-1] : 'сейчас'}`;
  document.getElementById('roomsListContainer').innerHTML = `<p class="room-context">${label}</p><p class="room-hint">Слоты дня: заполненные — есть занятия.</p>`+(cards.join('') || '<p class="room-hint">Аудитории не найдены</p>');
}
async function shareCurrentScheduleLink() {
  const url = new URL(location.href); url.search = ''; url.hash = '';
  const f = currentFavorite(); url.searchParams.set('mode', f.type);
  if (f.type === 'student') { url.searchParams.set('group', f.label); url.searchParams.set('section', f.section); }
  else { url.searchParams.set('teacher', f.teacherKey); url.searchParams.set('caf', f.cafId); }
  try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url.href); alert('Ссылка скопирована'); } catch { prompt('Ссылка на расписание:', url.href); }
}
function icsEscape(value) { return String(value || '').replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/\r?\n/g,'\\n').replace(/,/g,'\\,').replace(/;/g,'\\;'); }
function exportScheduleToICS() {
  if (!currentSchedule) return;
  if(scheduleTab==='session'){exportSessionICS();return;}
  const monday = getWeekRangeDates(selectedWeek).monday.date;
  const stamp = (d,time) => `${d.getFullYear()}${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}T${time.replace(':','')}00`;
  const lines = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR','VERSION:2.0','PRODID:-//ESSTU//Schedule//RU','CALSCALE:GREGORIAN'];
  DAY_KEYS.forEach((day,index) => {
    const date = new Date(monday); date.setDate(monday.getDate()+index);
    if(currentSchedule.weeks?.[selectedWeek]?.[day]?.session_date)return;
    (currentSchedule.weeks?.[selectedWeek]?.[day]?.pairs || []).forEach(p => filterLessons(p.lessons).forEach((l,idx) => {
      lines.push('BEGIN:VEVENT',`UID:${encodeURIComponent(favoriteKey(currentFavorite()))}-${stamp(date,p.start)}-${idx}@esstu-schedule`, `DTSTAMP:${new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g,'').replace(/\.\d{3}/,'')}`,`DTSTART;TZID=Asia/Irkutsk:${stamp(date,p.start)}`,`DTEND;TZID=Asia/Irkutsk:${stamp(date,p.end)}`,`SUMMARY:${icsEscape(l.subject)}`,`LOCATION:${icsEscape(l.room)}`,`DESCRIPTION:${icsEscape(l.teacher || l.groups)}`,'END:VEVENT');
    }));
  });
  lines.push('END:VCALENDAR');
  const folded = lines.map(line => { let result='', width=0; for (const char of line) { const size=new TextEncoder().encode(char).length; if(width+size>75){result+='\r\n ';width=1;} result+=char;width+=size;} return result; });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([folded.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'], {type:'text/calendar;charset=utf-8'}));
  const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href=url;a.download=`esstu_week${selectedWeek}.ics`;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
}

let searchMode = 'student';
function getTypeBorderClass(code) {
  return {lecture:'border-l-purple-500',practice:'border-l-emerald-500',lab:'border-l-amber-500',sport:'border-l-sky-500',exam:'border-l-rose-500',test:'border-l-rose-500'}[code] || 'border-l-slate-400';
}
function singleBuilding(pair) {
  const buildings=(pair.lessons || []).map(lesson=>decodeEsstuRoom(lesson.room)?.building);
  return buildings.length && buildings.every(building=>building && building===buildings[0]) ? buildings[0] : null;
}
function getWeekRangeDates(weekNumber, now=getUlanUdeNow()) {
  const monday=new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),now.getDate()-(now.getDay()+6)%7);
  // I/II select the current week or its neighbour, matching the existing week selector.
  const current=getWeekNum(now),shift=Number(weekNumber)===current?0:(Number(weekNumber)>current?7:-7);
  monday.setDate(monday.getDate()+shift);
  return Object.fromEntries(DAY_KEYS.map((key,index)=>{
    const date=new Date(monday);date.setDate(monday.getDate()+index);
    return [key,{date,shortLabel:date.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU',{day:'numeric',month:'short'}).replace(/\.$/,''),isToday:date.toDateString()===now.toDateString()}];
  }));
}
function dayDateMeta(day,key,dates) {
  // Session dates are explicit source data, not a recurring weekday template.
  if(day?.session_date)return {shortLabel:day.session_date,isToday:false};
  return dates[key];
}
function renderCalendarDays() {
  const dates=getWeekRangeDates(selectedWeek),week=currentSchedule?.weeks?.[selectedWeek] || {};
  document.getElementById('dayTabs').innerHTML=DAY_NAMES.map(day=>{
    const meta=dayDateMeta(week[day.key],day.key,dates),active=day.key===selectedDayKey;
    const count=(week[day.key]?.pairs || []).filter(pair=>filterLessons(pair.lessons).length).length;
    const changes=hasDayChanges(selectedWeek,day.key)?scheduleChanges.filter(change=>[change.current,change.previous].some(row=>row && String(row.week)===String(selectedWeek) && row.day===day.key)).length:0;
    const past=meta.date && !meta.isToday && meta.date<getUlanUdeNow();
    return `<button onclick="selectDay('${day.key}')" aria-pressed="${active}" data-past="${!!past}" ${meta.isToday?'aria-current="date"':''} class="calendar-day"><span class="calendar-weekday">${day.short}${meta.isToday?' <span class="calendar-today" aria-label="Сегодня">•</span>':''}${changes?`<span class="calendar-changed" title="Изменений: ${changes}" aria-label="Изменений: ${changes}"><i aria-hidden="true"></i>${changes}</span>`:''}</span><span class="calendar-date">${escapeHTML(meta.shortLabel)}</span><span class="calendar-count ${count>0?'has-lessons':''}">${currentSchedule?.is_incomplete?'нет данных':count?count+' '+pairWord(count):'нет пар'}</span></button>`;
  }).join('');
  let legend=document.getElementById('calendarChangesLegend');
  if(!legend){legend=document.createElement('p');legend.id='calendarChangesLegend';document.getElementById('dayTabs').after(legend);}
  legend.hidden=viewMode!=='day' || scheduleTab==='session' || hasDatedSchedule(currentSchedule) || !DAY_KEYS.some(day=>hasDayChanges(selectedWeek,day));
  legend.innerHTML='<span class="calendar-changed"><i aria-hidden="true"></i></span> Число рядом с днём — изменения расписания';
  const day=DAY_NAMES.find(day=>day.key===selectedDayKey),meta=dayDateMeta(week[selectedDayKey],selectedDayKey,dates);
  document.getElementById('selectedDaySummary').textContent=day?`${day.full}, ${meta.shortLabel}${meta.isToday?' · Сегодня':''}`:'';
}
const pairPluralRules=new Intl.PluralRules('ru');
function pairWord(count) {return {one:'пара',few:'пары',many:'пар',other:'пар'}[pairPluralRules.select(count)];}
function durationText(minutes) {
  const whole=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(minutes)),hours=Math.floor(whole/60),rest=whole%60;
  return hours?`${hours} ч${rest?' '+rest+' мин':''}`:`${rest} мин`;
}
function isOnlineLesson(lesson) {
  return !!decodeEsstuRoom(lesson.room)?.online || (!lesson.room && /онлайн[-\s]?курс|online course/i.test(lesson.subject || ''));
}
function displaySubject(lesson) {
  return isOnlineLesson(lesson)?String(lesson.subject || '').replace(/\s*\((?:онлайн[-\s]?курс|online course)\)/ig,'').trim() || lesson.subject:lesson.subject;
}
function lessonRoomLabel(lesson) {return isOnlineLesson(lesson)?'Онлайн':lesson.room?'ауд. '+lesson.room:'';}
function groupOnlinePairs(pairs) {
  const groups=[];
  const key=pair=>pair?.lessons?.length===1 && isOnlineLesson(pair.lessons[0]) && !pair.lessons[0].parse_warning && !pair.lessons[0].needs_review
    ? JSON.stringify(['subject','type_code','type','teacher_key','teacher','room','groups','tag','source_file'].map(field=>pair.lessons[0][field] || '')):null;
  for(let index=0;index<pairs.length;index++){
    const previous=groups.at(-1),signature=key(pairs[index]),last=previous && pairs[previous.end];
    const gap=last?timeToMinutes(pairs[index].start)-timeToMinutes(last.end):null;
    if(previous && signature && signature===previous.signature && Number(pairs[index].number)===Number(last.number)+1 && gap>=0 && gap<=30)previous.end=index;
    else groups.push({start:index,end:index,signature});
  }
  return groups;
}
function heroState(schedule,now) {
  if(schedule?.is_incomplete)return {kind:'incomplete'};
  if(hasDatedSchedule(schedule))return {kind:'dated'};
  const dayIndex=(now.getDay()+6)%7,minutes=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes()+now.getSeconds()/60;
  if(dayIndex===6)return {kind:'empty'};
  const day=schedule.weeks?.[getWeekNum(now)]?.[DAY_KEYS[dayIndex]];
  if(day?.session_date)return {kind:'dated'}; // A weekday alone cannot prove a session is today.
  const pairs=(day?.pairs || []).map(pair=>({...pair,lessons:filterLessons(pair.lessons)})).filter(pair=>pair.lessons.length).sort((a,b)=>timeToMinutes(a.start)-timeToMinutes(b.start));
  const current=pairs.find(pair=>minutes>=timeToMinutes(pair.start) && minutes<timeToMinutes(pair.end));
  const next=pairs.find(pair=>minutes<timeToMinutes(pair.start));
  if(current)return {kind:'current',pair:current,next,remaining:Math.ceil(timeToMinutes(current.end)-minutes),progress:Math.min(100,Math.max(0,100*(minutes-timeToMinutes(current.start))/(timeToMinutes(current.end)-timeToMinutes(current.start))))};
  if(next)return {kind:'next',pair:next,remaining:Math.ceil(timeToMinutes(next.start)-minutes)};
  return {kind:pairs.length?'finished':'empty'};
}
let campusDayStamp='';

  const now=getUlanUdeNow(),week=getWeekNum(now);

'+room).join(', ')):''} ${uiIcon('arrowUpRight')}</span></button>`:''}`;

    return `<div class="hero-lesson ${index?'hero-parallel':''}"><p class="hero-lesson-meta">${escapeHTML(pair.start)}–${escapeHTML(pair.end)} · ${escapeHTML(lesson.type || 'Занятие')}${lesson.tag?' · '+escapeHTML(lesson.tag):''}</p><h3 class="hero-subject">${escapeHTML(displaySubject(lesson))}</h3><p class="hero-teacher">${escapeHTML(subtitle || '')}</p>${isOnlineLesson(lesson)?`<span class="online-badge">${uiIcon('link')} Онлайн</span>`:room?`<div class="hero-location"><button onclick="heroRoomAction(${index})" class="hero-room" aria-label="Аудитория ${escapeHTML(room.raw)}"><span>Аудитория</span><strong>${escapeHTML(room.raw)}</strong></button>${room.building?`<span class="hero-building" title="По обозначению аудитории">${escapeHTML(room.building)}<span aria-hidden="true">${uiIcon('arrowUpRight')}</span></span>`:''}</div>`:''}</div>`;
  }).join('');

  if(state.next)next.textContent=`Далее в ${state.next.start} · через ${durationText(timeToMinutes(state.next.start)-now.getHours()*60-now.getMinutes()-now.getSeconds()/60)} · `+state.next.lessons.map(lesson=>`${displaySubject(lesson)}${lessonRoomLabel(lesson)?' · '+lessonRoomLabel(lesson):''}`).join(' / ');
}

function updateCurrentCards(now) {
  if(viewMode!=='day')return;
  const day=DAY_KEYS[(now.getDay()+6)%7],minutes=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
  const pairs=currentSchedule?.weeks?.[selectedWeek]?.[selectedDayKey]?.pairs || [];
  for(const card of document.querySelectorAll('[data-pair-index]')){
    const pair=pairs[Number(card.dataset.pairIndex)];
    const active=pair && !currentSchedule.weeks[selectedWeek][selectedDayKey].session_date && day===selectedDayKey && getWeekNum(now)===selectedWeek && minutes>=timeToMinutes(pair.start) && minutes<timeToMinutes(pair.end);
    card.dataset.current=String(active);
    card.classList.toggle('border-teal-500',active);card.classList.toggle('border-slate-200',!active);card.classList.toggle('dark:border-slate-800',!active);
    card.querySelector('[data-current-label]')?.classList.toggle('hidden',!active);
  }
  for(const block of document.querySelectorAll('[data-online-pair-indices]')){
    const active=day===selectedDayKey && getWeekNum(now)===selectedWeek && block.dataset.onlinePairIndices.split(',').some(index=>pairs[index] && minutes>=timeToMinutes(pairs[index].start) && minutes<timeToMinutes(pairs[index].end));
    block.dataset.current=String(active);block.querySelector('[data-merged-current-label]').hidden=!active;
  }
}
