function compactLessonAction(action,pairIndex,lessonIndex) {
  const pair=currentSchedule?.weeks?.[selectedWeek]?.[selectedDayKey]?.pairs?.[pairIndex];
  const lesson=pair?.lessons?.[lessonIndex];
  if(!lesson)return;
  if(action==='note')openNoteModal(makeNoteKey(lesson.subject,pair.number),lesson.subject);
  if(action==='teacher')openTeacherFromLesson(lesson.teacher_key,lesson.teacher_caf_id || '');
  if(action==='room')openRoomsModal(lesson.room);
}
function renderCompactDay(container) {
  const day=currentSchedule.weeks?.[selectedWeek]?.[selectedDayKey];
  if(!day){container.innerHTML='<p class="text-sm text-slate-500 py-8 text-center">Нет данных на этот день</p>';return;}
  const now=getUlanUdeNow(),today=DAY_KEYS[(now.getDay()+6)%7];
  const isToday=!day.session_date && today===selectedDayKey && getWeekNum(now)===selectedWeek;
  const minutes=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes(),notes=getNotesMap();
  let previous=null;
  const rows=(day.pairs || []).map((pair,pairIndex)=>{
    const visible=filterLessons(pair.lessons);
    if(!visible.length)return '';
    const current=isToday && minutes>=timeToMinutes(pair.start) && minutes<timeToMinutes(pair.end);
    let gap='';
    if(previous){
      const duration=timeToMinutes(pair.start)-timeToMinutes(previous.end);
      const before=singleBuilding(previous),after=singleBuilding({...pair,lessons:visible});
      if(duration>0)gap=`<p class="lesson-break"><span>${escapeHTML(previous.end)}–${escapeHTML(pair.start)} · ${formatBreak(duration)}${before && after && before!==after ? ` · <span class="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300">Смена корпуса по расписанию: ${escapeHTML(before)} → ${escapeHTML(after)}</span>`:''}</span></p>`;
    }
    previous={...pair,lessons:visible};
    const lessons=pair.lessons.map((lesson,index)=>{
      if(!visible.includes(lesson))return '';
      const room=decodeEsstuRoom(lesson.room),note=notes[makeNoteKey(lesson.subject,pair.number)];
      const subtitle=appMode==='student'?lesson.teacher:lesson.groups;
      const warning=!!lesson.parse_warning || lesson.type==='Требуется сверка';
      return `<details class="lesson-detail ${warning?'lesson-warning ':''}${index?'lesson-parallel':''}" data-type="${escapeHTML(lesson.type_code)}">
        <summary class="lesson-summary">
          <div class="lesson-kind">${warning?uiIcon('warning'):lessonTypeIcon(lesson.type_code)}${escapeHTML(lesson.type || 'Занятие')}${lesson.tag ? ' · '+escapeHTML(lesson.tag):''}</div>
          <div class="lesson-title-row"><span class="lesson-subject">${escapeHTML(displaySubject(lesson))}</span><span class="lesson-room">${isOnlineLesson(lesson)?uiIcon('link')+' ':''}${escapeHTML(lessonRoomLabel(lesson))}</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 mt-1"><span class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${escapeHTML(subtitle || (warning?'Откройте для сверки с источником':appMode==='student'?'Преподаватель не указан':'Группы не указаны'))}${note?' · '+uiIcon('note'):''}</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="detail-chevron">${uiIcon('chevron')}</span></div>
        </summary>
        <div class="pt-1 pb-2 space-y-2 text-xs"><p class="text-slate-500">${escapeHTML([lesson.type,lesson.tag].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p>
          ${lesson.parse_warning?`<p class="text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300">${escapeHTML(lesson.parse_warning)}</p><p class="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHTML(lesson.raw)}</p>`:''}
          ${room?`<p class="text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300">${escapeHTML(room.location)}</p>`:''}
          ${renderLessonMeta(lesson,pair.number)}
          ${note?`<p class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-200 whitespace-pre-wrap">${escapeHTML(note)}</p>`:''}
          <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            ${lesson.room && !isOnlineLesson(lesson)?`<button onclick="compactLessonAction('room',${pairIndex},${index})" class="px-3 rounded-xl bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300">Аудитория</button>`:''}
            ${appMode==='student' && lesson.teacher_key?`<button onclick="compactLessonAction('teacher',${pairIndex},${index})" class="px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">Преподаватель</button>`:''}
            <button onclick="compactLessonAction('note',${pairIndex},${index})" class="px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">${note?'Изменить заметку':'Добавить заметку'}</button>
          </div>
        </div></details>`;
    }).join('');
    return `${gap}<article data-pair-index="${pairIndex}" data-current="${current}" class="schedule-pair ${current?'border-teal-500':'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800'}">
      <div class="pair-time"><div class="font-semibold text-xs">${escapeHTML(pair.start)}</div><div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">${escapeHTML(pair.end)}</div><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-2">${pair.number} пара</div><span data-current-label class="${current?'':'hidden'} text-[9px] text-teal-500 font-bold">СЕЙЧАС</span></div><div class="pair-lessons">${lessons}</div></article>`;
  });
  const pairs=day.pairs || [];
  const grouped=groupOnlinePairs(pairs).map(group=>{
    if(group.start===group.end || !rows[group.start])return rows[group.start];
    const first=pairs[group.start],last=pairs[group.end],lesson=first.lessons[0],count=group.end-group.start+1;
    const gap=rows[group.start].match(/^<p class="lesson-break">[\s\S]*?<\/p>/)?.[0] || '';
    const originals=rows.slice(group.start,group.end+1);originals[0]=originals[0].slice(gap.length);
    const indices=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>group.start+i).join(',');
    const active=isToday && pairs.slice(group.start,group.end+1).some(pair=>minutes>=timeToMinutes(pair.start) && minutes<timeToMinutes(pair.end));
    return `${gap}<details class="merged-online-block" data-online-pair-indices="${indices}" data-current="${active}"><summary class="lesson-summary"><div class="merged-online-meta">${first.start}–${last.end} · ${count} ${pairWord(count)}<span data-merged-current-label ${active?'':'hidden'}>Сейчас</span></div><div class="lesson-title-row"><span class="lesson-subject">${escapeHTML(displaySubject(lesson))}</span><span class="lesson-room">${uiIcon('link')} Онлайн</span></div><div class="merged-online-bottom"><span>Раскрыть время каждой пары и заметки</span><span class="detail-chevron" aria-hidden="true">${uiIcon('chevron')}</span></div></summary><div class="merged-online-originals">${originals.join('')}</div></details>`;
  });
  container.innerHTML=grouped.join('') || renderEmptyStudyDay();
}

function formatBreak(minutes){
  if(minutes<60)return `Перерыв ${minutes} мин`;
  return `Окно ${Math.floor(minutes/60)} ч${minutes%60?' '+minutes%60+' мин':''}`;
}
function lessonTypeIcon(code){
  return uiIcon({lecture:'book',practice:'pen',lab:'flask',sport:'activity',exam:'check',test:'check'}[code] || 'calendar');
}
function nextStudyDay(schedule=currentSchedule,origin=getWeekRangeDates(selectedWeek)[selectedDayKey]?.date){
  if(!schedule || schedule.is_incomplete || hasDatedSchedule(schedule))return null;
  if(!origin)return null;
  for(let offset=1;offset<=14;offset++){
    const date=new Date(origin);date.setDate(date.getDate()+offset);
    const day=DAY_KEYS[(date.getDay()+6)%7];if(!day)continue;
    const week=getWeekNum(date),data=schedule.weeks?.[week]?.[day];
    const pairs=(data?.pairs || []).map(pair=>({...pair,lessons:filterLessons(pair.lessons)})).filter(pair=>pair.lessons.length).sort((a,b)=>timeToMinutes(a.start)-timeToMinutes(b.start));
    if(data && !data.session_date && pairs.length)
      return {day,week,pairs,offset,label:date.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'}).replace(/\.$/,'')};
  }
  return null;
}
function openUpcomingStudyDay(){const next=nextStudyDay(currentSchedule,getUlanUdeNow());if(next){selectedWeek=next.week;selectedDayKey=next.day;setViewMode('day');}}
function renderEmptyStudyDay(){
  const next=nextStudyDay();
  return `<div class="empty-state"><p class="font-semibold">В этот день занятий нет</p>${next?`<button class="next-study-day" onclick="openNextStudyDay()">Ближайший день с парами: ${escapeHTML(next.label)} ${uiIcon('arrowUpRight')}</button>`:'<p class="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Выберите другой день</p>'}</div>`;
}
function openNextStudyDay(){const next=nextStudyDay();if(next){selectedWeek=next.week;selectedDayKey=next.day;setViewMode('day');}}
function moveCalendarDay(direction,keyboard=false){
  if(viewMode!=='day' || hasDatedSchedule(currentSchedule))return;
  const index=DAY_KEYS.indexOf(selectedDayKey),next=index+direction;
  if(index<0 || next<0 || next>=DAY_KEYS.length)return;
  selectDay(DAY_KEYS[next]);
  if(keyboard)document.querySelector('#dayTabs [aria-pressed="true"]')?.focus();
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
  const tabs=document.getElementById('dayTabs');let gesture=null,suppressClickUntil=0;
  tabs.addEventListener('pointerdown',event=>{if(event.pointerType==='touch'){gesture={x:event.clientX,y:event.clientY};tabs.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);}});
  tabs.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{gesture=null;});
  tabs.addEventListener('pointerup',event=>{
    if(!gesture)return;const dx=event.clientX-gesture.x,dy=event.clientY-gesture.y;gesture=null;
    if(Math.abs(dx)>55 && Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.5){event.preventDefault();suppressClickUntil=Date.now()+350;moveCalendarDay(dx<0?1:-1);}
  });
  tabs.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(Date.now()<suppressClickUntil){event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();}},true);
  tabs.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(event.key)){event.preventDefault();moveCalendarDay(event.key==='ArrowRight'?1:-1,true);}});
});

function recentSchedules(){try{const rows=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('esstu_recent_v1') || '[]');return Array.isArray(rows)?rows.filter(row=>row && ['student','teacher'].includes(row.type) && typeof row.label==='string').slice(0,6):[];}catch{return [];}}
function rememberRecentSchedule(){
  if(!currentSchedule || currentSchedule.is_incomplete)return;
  const entry=currentFavorite(),key=favoriteKey(entry);
  try{localStorage.setItem('esstu_recent_v1',JSON.stringify([entry,...recentSchedules().filter(row=>favoriteKey(row)!==key)].slice(0,6)));}catch(error){console.warn('Не удалось сохранить недавние расписания',error);}
}
function renderRecentSchedules(){
  const box=document.getElementById('recentSchedules');box.replaceChildren();
  for(const row of recentSchedules().filter(row=>row.type===searchMode)){
    const button=document.createElement('button');button.className='recent-schedule';button.textContent=row.label;
    button.onclick=()=>{closeSearchModal();if(row.type==='student')loadStudentSchedule(row.section,row.id,row.label);else loadTeacherSchedule(row.teacherKey,row.cafId);};box.append(button);
  }
  document.getElementById('recentSection').hidden=!box.children.length;
}

let scheduleTab='semester';
function storedJSON(key,fallback) {try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)) ?? fallback;}catch{return fallback;}}
function studyOwner() {return appMode==='student'?`group:${savedState.section}:${savedState.groupName}`:`teacher:${savedState.teacherKey}`;}
function activeSubgroup() {
  if(appMode!=='student')return 'all';
  const value=localStorage.getItem('esstu_subgroup_v2:'+studyOwner());
  return ['1','2'].includes(value)?value:'all';
}
function matchesSubgroup(lesson,value) {
  if(!value || value==='all')return true;
  const text=[lesson.tag,...(Array.isArray(lesson.tags)?lesson.tags:[]),lesson.raw,lesson.subject].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase();
  if(/(?:^|[^\d])(?:1\s*(?:[,/]|и)\s*2|2\s*(?:[,/]|и)\s*1)\s*(?:п\s*\/?\s*г|подгр)/u.test(text))return true;
  const marks=[...text.matchAll(/(?:^|[^\d])(1|2)\s*(?:[-–]\s*[яй]\s*)?(?:п\s*\/?\s*г(?:р)?\.?|подгрупп[а-яё]*|подгр\.?)(?=$|[^а-яё\d])/gu)].map(match=>match[1]);
  // Unknown/ambiguous notation stays visible. No bare '1' in subject names is a subgroup.
  return !marks.length || marks.includes(value);
}
function setSubgroupFilter(value) {
  if(!['all','1','2'].includes(value))return;
  localStorage.setItem('esstu_subgroup_v2:'+studyOwner(),value);renderAll();
}
function setScheduleTab(tab) {
  scheduleTab=tab==='session'?'session':'semester';
  closeSimpleDialog('moreMenuModal');renderAll();
}
function updateStudyUI() {
  const session=scheduleTab==='session';
  const dated=hasDatedSchedule(currentSchedule) || viewMode==='dates';
  document.getElementById('datedViewBtn').classList.toggle('hidden',!hasDatedRows(currentSchedule));
  document.getElementById('weekControls').classList.toggle('hidden',session || dated);
  document.getElementById('dayTabs').classList.toggle('hidden',session || dated || viewMode==='week');
  document.getElementById('selectedDaySummary').classList.toggle('hidden',session || dated);
  const subgroup=activeSubgroup(),wrap=document.getElementById('subgroupFilterWrap');
  wrap.classList.toggle('hidden',appMode!=='student');
  for(const value of ['all','1','2']){
    const button=document.getElementById('subgroupBtn_'+value),active=value===subgroup;
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active));
    button.className='px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold '+(active?'bg-teal-600 text-white':'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800');
  }
  const chip=document.getElementById('activeSubgroupChip');
  chip.classList.toggle('hidden',subgroup==='all');chip.textContent=`${subgroup} подгруппа · изменить`;
}
function hasDatedRows(schedule) {return Object.values(schedule?.weeks || {}).some(days=>Object.values(days).some(day=>day.session_date));}
function hasDatedSchedule(schedule) {
  const days=Object.values(schedule?.weeks || {}).flatMap(week=>Object.values(week));
  return days.some(day=>day.session_date) && !days.some(day=>!day.session_date && (day.pairs || []).some(pair=>pair.lessons?.length));
}
let datedLessons=[];
function renderDatedTimetable(container) {
  datedLessons=[];
  const seen=new Set(),days=[];
  for(const [week,entries] of Object.entries(currentSchedule.weeks || {}))for(const [key,day] of Object.entries(entries)){
    const signature=JSON.stringify([day.session_date || `${week}:${key}`,day.pairs]);if(seen.has(signature))continue;seen.add(signature);
    days.push({week,key,day});
  }
  container.innerHTML='<h2 class="font-bold">Занятия по датам</h2><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Даты сохранены из источника. Без указанного года день недели не определяется.</p>'+days.map(({week,key,day})=>`<section class="rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-[#101c2e] border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-4"><h3 class="font-bold text-sm">${escapeHTML(day.session_date || `${day.day_title || key} · ${week==='1'?'I':'II'} неделя`)}</h3>${(day.pairs || []).map(pair=>filterLessons(pair.lessons).map(lesson=>{
    const index=datedLessons.push({week,key,pair,lesson})-1;
    return `<div class="border-l-[3px] ${getTypeBorderClass(lesson.type_code)} pl-3 mt-3"><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${escapeHTML(pair.start)}–${escapeHTML(pair.end)} · ${escapeHTML(lesson.type)}</p><p class="text-sm font-semibold mt-1">${escapeHTML(lesson.subject)}</p><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">${escapeHTML(appMode==='teacher'?lesson.groups:lesson.teacher)}</p><div class="flex gap-2 text-xs mt-1">${lesson.room?`<button class="px-2 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300" onclick="datedLessonAction(${index},'room')">ауд. ${escapeHTML(lesson.room)}</button>`:''}<button class="px-2 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300" onclick="datedLessonAction(${index},'note')">Заметка</button></div></div>`;
  }).join('')).join('') || '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">Занятия не указаны</p>'}</section>`).join('');
}
function datedLessonAction(index,action) {
  const item=datedLessons[index];if(!item)return;
  if(action==='room')openRoomsModal(item.lesson.room);
  if(action==='note'){
    const owner=appMode==='student'?savedState.groupName:savedState.teacherKey;
    openNoteModal(`${owner}::${item.week}::${item.key}::${item.pair.number}::${item.lesson.subject}`,item.lesson.subject);
  }
}
function examKind(lesson) {
  if(['exam','test','consultation'].includes(lesson.type_code))return lesson.type_code;
  const type=String(lesson.type || '').toLowerCase(),raw=String(lesson.raw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if(/экзамен/.test(type) || /^экз(?:\.|амен)/u.test(raw))return 'exam';
  if(/зач[её]т/.test(type) || /^зач(?:\.|[её]т)/u.test(raw))return 'test';
  if(/консультац/.test(type) || /^конс(?:\.|ультац)/u.test(raw))return 'consultation';
  return null;
}
function explicitISODate(value) {
  const raw=String(value || ''),iso=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/),ru=raw.match(/^(\d{2})\.(\d{2})\.(\d{4})$/);
  if(!iso && !ru)return null;
  const year=Number(iso?iso[1]:ru[3]),month=Number(iso?iso[2]:ru[2]),day=Number(iso?iso[3]:ru[1]);
  const date=new Date(Date.UTC(year,month-1,day));
  return date.getUTCFullYear()===year && date.getUTCMonth()===month-1 && date.getUTCDate()===day?`${year}-${String(month).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(day).padStart(2,'0')}`:null;
}
function getCustomExams() {
  const rows=storedJSON('esstu_exams_v1:'+studyOwner(),[]);
  return Array.isArray(rows)?rows.filter(row=>row && typeof row.id==='string' && typeof row.subject==='string' && explicitISODate(row.date)):[];
}
function saveCustomExams(rows) {localStorage.setItem('esstu_exams_v1:'+studyOwner(),JSON.stringify(rows));}
function collectSessionEvents(schedule=currentSchedule) {
  const events=[],seen=new Set();
  for(const [week,days] of Object.entries(schedule?.weeks || {}))for(const [dayKey,day] of Object.entries(days || {}))for(const pair of day.pairs || [])for(const lesson of filterLessons(pair.lessons)){
    const kind=examKind(lesson);if(!kind)continue;
    const date=explicitISODate(day.session_date || lesson.date),rawDate=day.session_date || '';
    const key=JSON.stringify([date || rawDate || week,date || rawDate?'':dayKey,pair.start,pair.end,lesson.subject,kind,lesson.room,lesson.teacher,lesson.groups,lesson.tag]);
    if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);
    events.push({id:'official:'+key,subject:lesson.subject,kind,date,rawDate,time:pair.start,end:pair.end,room:lesson.room,teacher:appMode==='teacher'?lesson.groups:lesson.teacher,tag:lesson.tag,source:'official',source_file:lesson.source_file,week,dayKey});
  }
  return [...events,...getCustomExams().map(row=>({...row,source:'custom'}))].sort((a,b)=>(a.date || '9999').localeCompare(b.date || '9999') || String(a.time || '').localeCompare(String(b.time || '')));
}
function countdownText(event,now=getUlanUdeNow()) {
  if(event.done)return 'Отмечено как сданное';
  if(!event.date)return event.rawDate?'Год не указан в источнике':'Дата не указана';
  const days=Math.round((Date.parse(event.date+'T00:00:00Z')-Date.UTC(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),now.getDate()))/86400000);
  return days===0?'Сегодня':days===1?'Завтра':days>1?`Через ${days} дн.`:'Дата прошла';
}
const EXAM_LABELS={exam:'Экзамен',test:'Зачёт',consultation:'Консультация'};
let sessionEvents=[];
function renderSessionTimeline(container) {

'+escapeHTML(event.room):''}</p>${event.teacher?`<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">${escapeHTML(event.teacher)}</p>`:''}${event.tag?`<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">${escapeHTML(event.tag)}</p>`:''}<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">${event.source==='custom'?`<span class="text-slate-500">Личная запись · только на этом устройстве</span><button onclick="editExam(${index})" class="px-3 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-xl">Изменить</button>`:event.source_file?`<a href="https://portal.esstu.ru/${event.source_file.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300 underline">Источник вуза</a>`:'<span class="text-slate-500">Из загруженного расписания</span>'}${event.room?`<button class="px-3 bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300 rounded-xl" onclick="sessionRoom(${index})">Аудитория</button>`:''}</div></article>`;
  container.innerHTML=`<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><h2 class="font-bold">Сессия и зачёты</h2><button class="text-xs text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300 px-3" onclick="setScheduleTab('semester')">К расписанию</button></div><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Аттестации из загруженного расписания и ваши записи. Даты без года требуют уточнения.</p><button class="w-full rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold" onclick="openAddExamModal()">+ Добавить личную запись</button>${sessionEvents.length?sessionEvents.map(card).join(''):'<p class="py-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">В загруженном расписании экзамены не найдены. Можно добавить личную запись.</p>'}`;
}

function saveExam(event) {
  event.preventDefault();const form=document.getElementById('examForm');if(!form.reportValidity())return;
  const subject=document.getElementById('exam_subject').value.trim(),date=explicitISODate(document.getElementById('exam_date').value);
  if(!subject || !date || editingExamOwner!==studyOwner()){document.getElementById('examFormError').textContent='Проверьте предмет и дату; расписание должно оставаться тем же.';return;}
  const row={id:editingExamId || crypto.randomUUID(),subject,date,done:document.getElementById('exam_done').checked};
  for(const key of ['time','room','teacher','kind'])row[key]=document.getElementById('exam_'+key).value.trim();
  const rows=getCustomExams().filter(item=>item.id!==editingExamId);rows.push(row);
  try{saveCustomExams(rows);}catch{document.getElementById('examFormError').textContent='Не удалось сохранить на устройстве.';return;}
  closeSimpleDialog('examModal');renderPairsList();
}
function deleteExam() {if(editingExamId && editingExamOwner===studyOwner()){saveCustomExams(getCustomExams().filter(row=>row.id!==editingExamId));closeSimpleDialog('examModal');renderPairsList();}}
function exportSessionICS() {
  const rows=collectSessionEvents().filter(row=>row.date);
  if(!rows.length){alert('Нет записей с полной датой. Уточните год в источнике или добавьте личную запись.');return;}
  const lines=['BEGIN:VCALENDAR','VERSION:2.0','PRODID:-//ESSTU//Session//RU','CALSCALE:GREGORIAN'];
  const stamp=new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g,'').replace(/\.\d{3}/,'');
  for(const row of rows){
    const day=row.date.replace(/-/g,'');lines.push('BEGIN:VEVENT',`UID:${encodeURIComponent(studyOwner()+row.id)}@esstu-session`,`DTSTAMP:${stamp}`,`SUMMARY:${icsEscape((EXAM_LABELS[row.kind] || '')+' '+row.subject)}`,`LOCATION:${icsEscape(row.room)}`,`DESCRIPTION:${icsEscape((row.source==='custom'?'Личная запись. ':'Из расписания вуза. ')+(row.teacher || ''))}`);
    if(row.time){lines.push(`DTSTART;TZID=Asia/Irkutsk:${day}T${row.time.replace(':','')}00`);if(row.end)lines.push(`DTEND;TZID=Asia/Irkutsk:${day}T${row.end.replace(':','')}00`);}
    else lines.push(`DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:${day}`);
    lines.push('END:VEVENT');
  }
  lines.push('END:VCALENDAR');downloadICS(lines,'esstu_session.ics');
}
function downloadICS(lines,name) {
  const folded=lines.map(line=>{let text='',width=0;for(const char of line){const length=new TextEncoder().encode(char).length;if(width+length>75){text+='\r\n ';width=1;}text+=char;width+=length;}return text;});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([folded.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'],{type:'text/calendar;charset=utf-8'})),link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download=name;link.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
}
let campusBuildings=[],campusSelection=null,campusRoomQuery='';
function buildCampusDirectory(rooms) {
  const buildings=new Map();
  for(const room of rooms){const label=decodeEsstuRoom(room.room)?.building;if(!label)continue;if(!buildings.has(label))buildings.set(label,[]);buildings.get(label).push(room.room);}
  return [...buildings].sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b,'ru',{numeric:true})).map(([label,rooms])=>({label,rooms:[...new Set(rooms)].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'ru',{numeric:true}))}));
}
async function openCampusMapModal(building=null) {
  closeSimpleDialog('moreMenuModal');openSimpleDialog('campusMapModal');
  const status=document.getElementById('campusStatus');status.textContent='Загрузка аудиторий…';
  try{
    if(!roomDirectory){const response=await fetch(getApiUrl('/api/rooms'));if(!response.ok)throw Error();roomDirectory=await response.json();}
    campusBuildings=buildCampusDirectory(roomDirectory.rooms || []);campusSelection=building;campusRoomQuery='';document.getElementById('campusRoomSearch').value='';
    renderCampusMap();status.textContent=campusBuildings.length?'Выберите корпус, чтобы найти его аудитории.':'В загруженных данных нет обозначений корпусов.';
  }catch{status.textContent='Не удалось загрузить аудитории. Повторите при подключении к сети.';}
}
function renderCampusMap() {
  const height=Math.max(100,Math.ceil(campusBuildings.length/3)*70+10);
  document.getElementById('campusDiagram').innerHTML=`<svg viewBox="0 0 420 ${height}" class="w-full h-auto" role="group" aria-label="Условная схема корпусов">${campusBuildings.map((building,index)=>{const x=10+(index%3)*138,y=10+Math.floor(index/3)*70,active=campusSelection===building.label;return `<g role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="${escapeHTML(building.label)}" aria-pressed="${active}" onclick="selectCampusBuilding(${index})" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();selectCampusBuilding(${index});}"><rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="124" height="56" rx="10" fill="${active?'var(--campus-solid)':'currentColor'}" fill-opacity="${active?'1':'.06'}" stroke="${active?'var(--campus-accent)':'var(--campus-muted)'}"/><text x="${x+62}" y="${y+24}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${active?'white':'currentColor'}" font-size="12" font-weight="600">${escapeHTML(building.label)}</text><text x="${x+62}" y="${y+43}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${active?'white':'currentColor'}" font-size="10">${building.rooms.length} ауд.</text></g>`;}).join('')}</svg>`;
  const selected=campusBuildings.find(building=>building.label===campusSelection);
  document.getElementById('campusBldCard').innerHTML=selected?`<h3 class="font-bold text-sm">${escapeHTML(selected.label)}</h3><p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Корпус определён по обозначению аудитории в расписании.</p><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">${selected.rooms.map((room,index)=>({room,index})).filter(({room})=>!campusRoomQuery || normSearch(room).includes(campusRoomQuery) || normSearch(selected.label).includes(campusRoomQuery)).map(({room,index})=>`<button data-found="${!!campusRoomQuery}" class="px-3 rounded-xl bg-teal-500/10 text-teal-700 dark:text-teal-300 text-xs" onclick="campusRoom(${index})">${escapeHTML(room)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
}
function selectCampusBuilding(index){campusSelection=campusBuildings[index]?.label;renderCampusMap();document.querySelector('#campusDiagram [aria-pressed="true"]')?.focus();}

async function handleUrlShortcuts() {
  const action=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('action');
  if(action==='rooms')await openRoomsModal();

  if(action==='campus')await openCampusMapModal();

}

function filterCampusRoom(value){
  campusRoomQuery=normSearch(value);
  const matches=campusBuildings.filter(building=>normSearch(building.label).includes(campusRoomQuery)||building.rooms.some(room=>normSearch(room).includes(campusRoomQuery)));
  if(campusRoomQuery)campusSelection=matches[0]?.label || null;
  renderCampusMap();
  document.getElementById('campusStatus').textContent=campusRoomQuery?(matches.length?'Найденные аудитории подсвечены. Корпус определён по обозначению в расписании.':'В известных расписаниях аудитории не найдены.'):'Выберите корпус, чтобы найти его аудитории.';
}


===== static/sw.js =====
SHA-256: 51cd5a8771600e0d0d2cd74db2dd992763089c94c4fe9ff86326774e204315bb

const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';
const CACHE_PREFIX = `esstu-pwa:${self.registration.scope}:`;
const SHELL_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}${CACHE_NAME}`;
const DATA_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}data-v2`;
const MAX_DATA_ENTRIES = 200;
const PIN_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}preferences-v1`;
const PIN_URL = new URL('offline-pins',self.registration.scope).href;
let protectedURLs = null;
async function loadPins(){if(!protectedURLs){const saved=await (await caches.open(PIN_CACHE)).match(PIN_URL);protectedURLs=new Set(saved?await saved.json():[]);}return protectedURLs;}
const SHELL = ['./', './index.html', './app.css', './shortcut-now.svg', './modern.js', './experience.js', './simple-ui.js', './study-tools.js', './accessibility.js', './manifest.json', './icons/app-icon.svg', './icons/app-icon-192.png', './icons/app-icon-512.png', './shortcut-now.png', './shortcut-rooms.png', './shortcut-session.png', './shortcut-campus.png'];
const SHELL_URLS = new Set(SHELL.map(path => new URL(path, self.registration.scope).href));
const API_URL = new URL('api/', self.registration.scope).href;
self.addEventListener('install', event => {
  event.waitUntil(caches.open(SHELL_CACHE).then(cache => cache.addAll(
    SHELL.map(path => new Request(new URL(path, self.registration.scope), {cache:'reload'}))
  )));
});
self.addEventListener('message', event => {
  if (event.data?.type === 'SKIP_WAITING') event.waitUntil(self.skipWaiting());
  if(event.data?.type==='PROTECT_DATA')event.waitUntil((async()=>{
    const urls=(event.data.urls || []).filter(url=>typeof url==='string' && url.startsWith(API_URL)).slice(0,64);
    protectedURLs=new Set(urls);await (await caches.open(PIN_CACHE)).put(PIN_URL,new Response(JSON.stringify(urls)));
  })());
});
async function trimData(cache) {
  const requests = await cache.keys();
  const pins=await loadPins();
  const victims=requests.filter(request=>!pins.has(request.url));
  // FIFO among unprotected responses; current/favourite endpoints are retained.
  await Promise.all(victims.slice(0,Math.max(0,requests.length-MAX_DATA_ENTRIES)).map(request=>cache.delete(request)));
}
// Serialize writes so simultaneous downloads cannot race the entry limit.
let dataWrite = Promise.resolve();
function saveData(cache, request, response) {
  dataWrite = dataWrite.catch(() => {}).then(async () => {
    const headers = new Headers(response.headers); headers.set('X-ESSTU-Schema', '2');
    await cache.put(request, new Response(response.body, {status:response.status,statusText:response.statusText,headers}));
    await trimData(cache);
  });
  return dataWrite;
}
async function fingerprint(response) {
  const etag=response.headers.get('ETag');
  if(etag)return 'etag:'+etag;
  const stored=response.headers.get('X-ESSTU-Content-Hash');
  if(stored)return stored;
  const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',await response.clone().arrayBuffer());
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest),byte=>byte.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}
async function migrateData(response) {
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') throw Error('Unsupported saved data');
  const markSchedule = schedule => {
    if (!schedule?.weeks) return;
    schedule.is_stale_fallback = true;
    schedule.source_mode = 'saved_snapshot';
    // Old data cannot establish that a teacher identity was checked.
    if (schedule.mode === 'teacher' && schedule.identity_ambiguous === undefined) schedule.identity_ambiguous = true;
  };
  markSchedule(payload);
  if (Array.isArray(payload.teachers)) payload.teachers.forEach(markSchedule);
  payload.schema_version = 2;
  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
  headers.set('Content-Type','application/json; charset=utf-8');
  headers.delete('Content-Length'); headers.delete('Content-Encoding'); headers.delete('ETag');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(payload), {status:response.status,headers});
}
self.addEventListener('activate', event => {
  event.waitUntil((async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    const data = await caches.open(DATA_CACHE);
    const legacyKeys = keys.filter(key => key.startsWith('esstu-live-') || key === `${CACHE_PREFIX}data-v1`);
    for (const key of legacyKeys.reverse()) {
      const legacy = await caches.open(key); let migrated = true, shared = false;
      for (const request of await legacy.keys()) {
        if (!request.url.startsWith(self.registration.scope)) { shared = true; continue; }
        if (!request.url.startsWith(API_URL) || await data.match(request)) continue;
        try { await saveData(data, request, await migrateData(await legacy.match(request))); }
        catch (error) { migrated = false; console.warn('ESSTU cache migration failed', error); }
      }
      if (migrated && !shared) await caches.delete(key);
    }
    await trimData(data);
    // Keep failed migrations and caches containing another application's entries.
    await Promise.all(keys.filter(key => key.startsWith(CACHE_PREFIX) && key !== SHELL_CACHE && key !== DATA_CACHE && key !== PIN_CACHE && !legacyKeys.includes(key)).map(key => caches.delete(key)));
    await self.clients.claim();
  })());
});
self.addEventListener('fetch', event => {
  const request = event.request, url = new URL(request.url);
  if (request.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== self.location.origin || !url.href.startsWith(self.registration.scope)) return;
  const isData = url.href.startsWith(API_URL);
  const assetURL = new URL(url); assetURL.search = ''; assetURL.hash = '';
  const isShell = SHELL_URLS.has(assetURL.href);
  // Other assets follow normal HTTP caching, never shell cache-first.
  if (!isData && !isShell) return;
  const background=[];
  const result=(async () => {
    const cache=await caches.open(isData?DATA_CACHE:SHELL_CACHE);
    const shellURL=request.mode==='navigate'?new URL('./',self.registration.scope).href:assetURL.href;
    if(!isData){const shell=await cache.match(shellURL);if(shell)return shell;}
    const saved=isData?await cache.match(request):null;
    if(saved && request.headers.get('X-ESSTU-Cache-Only')==='1')return saved;
    const fetching=fetch(request,{cache:'reload',signal:AbortSignal.timeout(saved?60000:12000)}).then(async response=>{
      if(response.ok){
        try{
          let changed=false;
          if(isData){
            const hash=await fingerprint(response);
            changed=saved && await fingerprint(saved)!==hash;
            const headers=new Headers(response.headers);headers.set('X-ESSTU-Content-Hash',hash);
            await saveData(cache,request,new Response(response.clone().body,{status:response.status,statusText:response.statusText,headers}));
          }else await cache.put(shellURL,response.clone());
          if(changed)for(const client of await self.clients.matchAll({type:'window'}))client.postMessage({type:'DATA_UPDATED',url:request.url});
        }catch(error){console.warn('ESSTU cache write failed',error);}
      }
      return response;
    });
    // Keep a slow transfer alive after serving a saved response.
    background.push(fetching.catch(()=>{}));
    try{
      let response;
      if(saved){
        let timer;
        try{response=await Promise.race([fetching,new Promise(resolve=>{timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve(null),3500);})]);}
        finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
        if(!response)return cachedResponse(saved,'refreshing');
      }else response=await fetching;
      return saved && response.status>=500?cachedResponse(saved,'server-error'):response;
    }catch{return saved?cachedResponse(saved,'offline'):new Response('Нет сохранённой копии. Подключитесь к интернету.',{status:503});}
  })();
  event.respondWith(result);
  event.waitUntil(result.then(()=>Promise.all(background)).catch(()=>{}));
});

function cachedResponse(saved, reason) {
  const headers = new Headers(saved.headers); headers.set('X-ESSTU-Cache', reason);
  return new Response(saved.clone().body, {status:saved.status,statusText:saved.statusText,headers});
}

===== tests/test_audit.py =====
SHA-256: 991dd2ff8a06dfd62fdc7a36cf2dee116099fb133ce71d925c91ceb07e25064c

import http.client
import json
import ssl
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

import app
import parser
from cache_tools import TTLCache, singleflight


class ReliabilityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_ambiguous_room_assignments_are_explicit(self):
        raw = "лаб. Иностранный язык а.15-571 а.15-573 а.15-"
        lesson = parser.parse_single_lesson_chunk(raw)
        self.assertTrue(lesson["parse_warning"])
        self.assertEqual(lesson["room"], "")
        self.assertEqual(lesson["teacher"], "")
        self.assertEqual(lesson["raw"], raw)

    def test_merge_preserves_extra_pairs_and_missing_weeks(self):
        pair = {
            "number": 1,
            "start": "09:00",
            "end": "10:35",
            "lessons": [{"raw": "A", "subject": "A"}],
        }
        late = {
            "number": 7,
            "start": "19:45",
            "end": "21:20",
            "lessons": [{"raw": "B", "subject": "B"}],
        }
        primary = {"weeks": {"1": {"monday": {"pairs": [pair]}}}}
        secondary = {
            "weeks": {
                "1": {"monday": {"pairs": [pair, late]}},
                "2": {"tuesday": {"pairs": [late]}},
            }
        }
        before = deepcopy(secondary)
        merged = parser.merge_teacher_schedules(primary, secondary)
        self.assertEqual(len(merged["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(merged["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"]), 1)
        self.assertIn("tuesday", merged["weeks"]["2"])
        merged["weeks"]["2"]["tuesday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"].clear()
        self.assertEqual(secondary, before)

    def test_dates_do_not_invent_weekdays_or_overwrite_rows(self):
        self.assertIsNone(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("31.02", 0))
        first = parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("15 янв", 0)
        self.assertEqual(first[0], "date_15.01")
        self.assertEqual(first[3], -1)
        self.assertNotIn("Понедельник", first[1])
        self.assertEqual(
            parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("Пнд,14 сентября", 0)[4], "14.09"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("16.01.2026", 0)[4], "16.01.2026"
        )
        html = "<table><tr><td>День</td><td>1</td></tr><tr><td></td><td>09:00 - 10:35</td></tr>"
        html += (
            "".join(
                f"<tr><td>{date}.01</td><td>экз. Физика</td></tr>"
                for date in range(10, 18)
            )
            + "</table>"
        )
        parsed = parser.parse_14row_table(BeautifulSoup(html, "html.parser").table)
        self.assertEqual(len(parsed["weeks"]["1"]), 8)
        self.assertEqual(parsed["weeks"]["2"], {})

    def test_spacer_rows_do_not_change_week(self):
        html = "<table><tr><td></td><td>1</td></tr><tr><td></td><td>09:00 - 10:35</td></tr>"
        html += "<tr><td>Пнд</td><td>лек. A</td></tr>" + "<tr><td></td></tr>" * 10
        html += "<tr><td>Втр</td><td>лек. B</td></tr><tr><td>Пнд</td><td>лек. C</td></tr></table>"
        with self.assertRaises(parser.ScheduleStructureError):
            parser.parse_14row_table(BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml").table)

    def test_missing_teacher_heading_is_not_a_person(self):
        with self.assertLogs("esstu_schedule", level="WARNING"):
            self.assertEqual(
                parser.parse_department_teachers_html(
                    "<table><tr><td>A</td></tr></table>"
                ),
                [],
            )

    def test_meta_cache_and_explicit_invalidation(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as directory:
            path = Path(directory) / "meta.json"
            path.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
            parser._read_teachers_meta.cache_clear()
            with patch.object(parser, "TEACHERS_META_PATH", path):
                for _ in range(10):
                    parser.build_teacher_profile("Иванов И.И.")
                self.assertEqual(parser._read_teachers_meta.cache_info().misses, 1)
                parser.save_teacher_meta_entry("Иванов И.И.", {"degree": "Updated"})
                self.assertEqual(
                    parser.build_teacher_profile("Иванов И.И.")["degree"], "Updated"
                )

    def test_cache_coalesces_concurrent_work(self):
        cache, calls = TTLCache(), []

        @singleflight
        def work(key="A"):
            cached = cache.get(key, 900)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached
            calls.append(key)
            time.sleep(0.02)
            cache.put(key, {"value": key})
            return cache.get(key, 900)

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
            self.assertEqual(len(list(pool.map(lambda _: work(), range(8)))), 8)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["A"])

    def test_fallback_is_structured_and_preserves_dates(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            (root / "bakalavriat").mkdir()
            (root / "bakalavriat/1.htm").write_text("saved", encoding="cp1251")
            (root / "sources.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "bakalavriat/1.htm": {
                            "retrieved_at": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
                            "last_modified": "Mon, 01 Jan 2024 00:00:00 GMT",
                        }
                    }
                )
            )
            with (
                patch.object(app, "DEMO_DIR", root),
                patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=OSError("offline")),
                self.assertLogs(app.LOGGER),
            ):
                page = app.fetch_esstu_html_sync("bakalavriat/1.htm")
            payload = app.apply_page_status({}, page)
            self.assertTrue(payload["is_stale_fallback"])
            self.assertEqual(payload["source_checked_at"], "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z")
            self.assertEqual(payload["last_modified"], "Mon, 01 Jan 2024 00:00:00 GMT")
        self.assertEqual(app.SSL_CONTEXT.verify_mode, ssl.CERT_REQUIRED)


class HTTPRegressionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).resolve().parent)
        root = Path(cls.directory.name)
        assert root.resolve().parent == Path(__file__).resolve().parent
        cls.static = root / "static"
        cls.static.mkdir()
        (cls.static / "index.html").write_text("<html>ok</html>")
        (cls.static / "image.png").write_bytes(b"png")
        (root / "static_evil").mkdir()
        (root / "static_evil/secret").write_text("private")
        cls.patch = patch.object(app, "STATIC_DIR", cls.static)
        cls.patch.start()
        cls.server = app.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), app.ESSTURequestHandler)
        cls.thread = threading.Thread(target=cls.server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        cls.thread.start()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.server.shutdown()
        cls.server.server_close()
        cls.thread.join()
        cls.patch.stop()
        cls.directory.cleanup()

    def request(self, path, headers=None, method="GET", body=None):
        connection = http.client.HTTPConnection(*self.server.server_address, timeout=3)
        connection.request(method, path, body, headers or {})
        response = connection.getresponse()
        result = response.status, dict(response.getheaders()), response.read()
        connection.close()
        return result

    def test_routing_and_traversal(self):
        for path in ["/api/schedule/x", "/api/department/x"]:
            self.assertEqual(self.request(path)[0], 400)
        for path in [
            "/../static_evil/secret",
            "/%2e%2e/static_evil/secret",
            "/missing.js",
            "/api/unknown",
        ]:
            status, _, body = self.request(path)
            self.assertEqual(status, 404)
            self.assertNotIn(b"private", body)
        self.assertEqual(self.request("/api/groups?section=invalid")[0], 400)

    def test_etag_and_binary_mime(self):
        status, headers, body = self.request("/image.png")
        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
        self.assertEqual(headers["Content-Type"], "image/png")
        status, _, body = self.request("/image.png", {"If-None-Match": headers["ETag"]})
        self.assertEqual(status, 304)
        self.assertEqual(body, b"")

    def test_invalid_json_body(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            self.request("/api/teacher/meta", method="POST", body="{")[0], 405
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.request("/api/teacher/meta", method="POST", body="[]")[0], 405
        )

    def test_status_exposes_parser_diagnostics(self):
        from esstu_schedule.data.diagnostics import parsing_status, record_error

        previous = parsing_status()["parse_error_count"]
        with self.assertLogs("esstu_schedule", level="WARNING"):
            record_error("test.htm", "Unexpected structure")
        status, _, body = self.request("/api/status")
        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(body)["parse_error_count"], previous + 1)

    def test_rooms_route_reads_prebuilt_snapshot(self):
        import os

        from esstu_schedule.data.store import write_json_atomic

        path = self.static.parent / "rooms.json"
        write_json_atomic(path, {"count": 1, "rooms": [{"room": "0410", "slots": {}}]})
        with (
            patch.dict(os.environ, {"ESSTU_ROOMS_INDEX": str(path)}),
            patch.object(
                app, "service_all_teachers", side_effect=AssertionError("Must not scan")
            ),
        ):
            status, _, body = self.request("/api/rooms")
        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(body)["rooms"][0]["room"], "0410")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== tests/test_catalog.py =====
SHA-256: cb81448add2f13a1be9a0e732499194dfbf3a549b8ec19a340bac34cf463aeda

from pathlib import Path

from esstu_schedule.catalog import build_catalog
from esstu_schedule.data.diagnostics import parsing_status, reset_diagnostics


def test_missing_catalogs_are_reported(tmp_path):
    reset_diagnostics()
    catalog, institutes, departments = build_catalog(tmp_path, [])
    assert catalog == []
    assert departments == []
    assert all(not value for value in institutes.values())
    status = parsing_status()
    assert status["failed_source_count"] == 2
    assert {item["source"] for item in status["parse_errors"]} == {
        "bakalavriat/craspisanEdt.htm",
        "spezialitet/craspisanEdt.htm",
    }
    reset_diagnostics()


def test_missing_department_is_reported(tmp_path):
    from unittest.mock import patch

    section = tmp_path / "bakalavriat"
    section.mkdir()
    (section / "craspisanEdt.htm").write_text("catalog", encoding="ascii")
    fixture = [
        {"institute": "Test", "departments": [{"caf_id": "Caf5", "name": "Physics"}]}
    ]
    reset_diagnostics()
    with patch(
        "esstu_schedule.catalog.parse_departments_list_html", return_value=fixture
    ):
        build_catalog(Path(tmp_path), [])
    assert "bakalavriat/Caf5.htm" in {
        item["source"] for item in parsing_status()["parse_errors"]
    }
    reset_diagnostics()


===== tests/test_data.py =====

@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "date,expected,reference",
    WEEK_CASES,
    ids=[f"{row[0]}-week-{row[1]}" for row in WEEK_CASES],
)
def test_week_parity(date, expected, reference):
    actual = parser.get_week_num(datetime.fromisoformat(date))
    assert (actual["week_number"], actual["reference_date"]) == (expected, reference)


class CalendarTests(unittest.TestCase):

            parser.get_week_num(datetime(2026, 9, 6, 20, tzinfo=UTC))["week_number"], 2
        )


class ParserContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_frozen_demo_goldens(self):
        for case in json.loads(
            (FIXTURES / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        ):
            with self.subTest(source=case["source"]):
                raw = (FIXTURES / case["html"]).read_bytes()
                self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), case["sha256"])
                html = raw.decode("cp1251")
                with patch.object(parser, "load_teachers_meta", return_value={}):
                    if case["kind"] == "group":
                        actual = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(
                            html, case["id"], case["section"]
                        )
                    else:
                        actual = parser.parse_department_teachers_html(
                            html, case["id"], case["section"]
                        )[0]
                expected = json.loads(
                    (FIXTURES / case["json"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                )
                self.assertEqual(actual, expected)

    def test_correspondence_sunday_and_evening_time_are_in_source(self):
        raw = (FIXTURES / "zo1-1.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
        result = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(raw, "1", "zo1")
        sunday = result["weeks"]["1"]["date_12.04"]
        self.assertEqual(sunday["day_index"], 6)
        self.assertTrue(any(pair["lessons"] for pair in sunday["pairs"]))
        self.assertEqual(result["bells"][-1]["number"], 7)
        self.assertEqual(result["bells"][-1]["start"], "19:40")

    def test_font_colour_cannot_change_week(self):
        html = (FIXTURES / "bakalavriat-1.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
        original = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(html)
        changed = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(
            html.replace("#0000FF", "#ff0000").replace("#0000ff", "#ff0000")
        )
        self.assertFalse(original["is_incomplete"])
        self.assertEqual(original, changed)

    def test_truncated_and_unknown_structures_are_explicit(self):
        html = (FIXTURES / "bakalavriat-1.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
        for kind in ("missing_day", "unknown_day", "bad_time"):
            soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")
            rows = soup.table.find_all("tr")
            if kind == "missing_day":
                rows[3].decompose()
            elif kind == "unknown_day":
                rows[3].td.string = "???"
            else:
                rows[1].find_all("td")[1].string = "unknown"
            reset_diagnostics()
            with self.assertLogs("esstu_schedule", level="WARNING"):
                result = parser.parse_group_schedule_html(str(soup))
            self.assertTrue(result["is_incomplete"])
            self.assertTrue(result["parse_errors"])
            self.assertEqual(result["weeks"], {"1": {}, "2": {}})
            self.assertEqual(parsing_status()["parse_error_count"], 1)

    def test_explicit_headers_and_cell_weekdays(self):
        html = "<table><tr><td>Пары</td><td>1-я</td></tr><tr><td>Время</td><td>09:00–10:35</td></tr>"
        html += '<tr><td colspan="2">II неделя</td></tr><tr><td>Втр</td><td>лек. B</td></tr>'
        html += '<tr><td colspan="2">I неделя</td></tr><tr><td>Птн</td><td>лек. A</td></tr></table>'
        parsed = parser.parse_14row_table(BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml").table)
        self.assertIn("tuesday", parsed["weeks"]["2"])
        self.assertIn("friday", parsed["weeks"]["1"])
        self.assertEqual(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("Вск,12 апреля", 0)[3], 6)
        self.assertEqual(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("Втр,07 апреля", 5)[3], 1)
        self.assertEqual(parser.parse_day_or_date_cell("15 янв", 5)[3], -1)

    def test_metadata_external_edit_invalidates_cache(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as folder:
            file = Path(folder) / "meta.json"
            file.write_text("{}")
            parser._read_teachers_meta.cache_clear()
            with patch.object(parser, "TEACHERS_META_PATH", file):
                parser.load_teachers_meta()
                file.write_text(
                    '{"иванов и.и.":{"degree":"Professor"}}', encoding="utf-8"
                )
                os.utime(
                    file,
                    ns=(file.stat().st_atime_ns, file.stat().st_mtime_ns + 1000000),
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    parser.build_teacher_profile("Иванов И.И.")["degree"], "Professor"
                )

    def test_model_rejects_invalid_interval(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            validate_schedule(
                {
                    "weeks": {
                        "1": {
                            "monday": {
                                "pairs": [
                                    {
                                        "number": 1,
                                        "start": "10:35",
                                        "end": "09:00",
                                        "lessons": [],
                                    }
                                ]
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            )


class ArchitectureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_cold_cache_coalesces_and_force_refresh_waits(self):
        import time

        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
        calls = []

        @cache_tools.stale_while_revalidate
        def example(key="A", force_refresh=False):
            calls.append(key)
            time.sleep(0.02)
            return {"version": len(calls)}

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
            results = list(pool.map(lambda _: example(), range(8)))
        self.assertEqual(len(calls), 1)
        self.assertTrue(all(item["version"] == 1 for item in results))
        self.assertEqual(example(force_refresh=True)["version"], 2)

    def test_failed_refresh_does_not_renew_stale_timestamp(self):
        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
        now = [20000.0]
        calls = []

        @cache_tools.stale_while_revalidate
        def failure_case():
            calls.append(1)
            if len(calls) > 1:
                raise RuntimeError("portal unavailable")
            return {"value": 1}

        with patch.object(cache_tools.time, "time", side_effect=lambda: now[0]):
            failure_case()
            now[0] += 901
            with self.assertLogs(cache_tools.__name__, level="WARNING"):
                failure_case()
                import time

                deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
                while cache_tools._refreshing and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                    threading.Event().wait(0.01)
            self.assertFalse(cache_tools._refreshing)
            self.assertEqual(
                next(iter(cache_tools._service_cache._data.values()))[0], 20000.0
            )
            now[0] += 3000
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                failure_case()

    def test_rooms_http_service_never_scans_schedules(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as folder:
            path = Path(folder) / "rooms.json"
            write_json_atomic(
                path, {"count": 1, "rooms": [{"room": "0410", "slots": {}}]}
            )
            with (
                patch.dict(os.environ, {"ESSTU_ROOMS_INDEX": str(path)}),
                patch.object(
                    app, "service_all_teachers", side_effect=AssertionError("scan")
                ),
                patch.object(app, "service_groups", side_effect=AssertionError("scan")),
            ):
                self.assertEqual(app.service_rooms_index()["count"], 1)
            write_json_atomic(path, {"count": 0, "rooms": []})
            self.assertEqual(read_rooms_index(path)["count"], 0)
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                read_rooms_index(Path(folder) / "missing.json")

    def test_stale_refresh_is_nonblocking_and_coalesced(self):
        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
        now = [10000.0]
        calls = []
        entered = threading.Event()
        release = threading.Event()
        done = threading.Event()

        @cache_tools.stale_while_revalidate
        def service(key="A"):
            calls.append(key)
            if len(calls) > 1:
                entered.set()
                release.wait(3)
            return {"value": len(calls)}

        with patch.object(cache_tools.time, "time", side_effect=lambda: now[0]):
            self.assertEqual(service()["value"], 1)
            now[0] += 901
            first = service()
            self.assertEqual(first["cache_state"], "stale_while_revalidate")
            self.assertTrue(entered.wait(1))
            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
                results = list(pool.map(lambda _: service(), range(16)))
            self.assertTrue(all(result["value"] == 1 for result in results))
            self.assertEqual(len(calls), 2)
            release.set()
            cache_tools._executor.submit(done.set)
            # Wait for the per-key flag to clear, not for a timing assumption.
            import time

            deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
            while cache_tools._refreshing and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                threading.Event().wait(0.01)
            self.assertFalse(cache_tools._refreshing)
            self.assertEqual(service()["value"], 2)


===== tests/test_fallback.py =====
SHA-256: fdcefdc8cdae021b2729fb4dac81a92230b36038f97aa83e2afc42e7198a14af

import hashlib
import json
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest

from esstu_schedule import app, cache_tools


@pytest.mark.parametrize("filename", ["raspisan.htm", "craspisanEdt.htm", "Caf5.htm"])
def test_disabled_html_fallback_reaches_service_snapshot(tmp_path, filename):
    source = tmp_path / "bakalavriat" / filename
    source.parent.mkdir()
    source.write_bytes(b"old local HTML")
    with (
        patch.object(app, "DEMO_DIR", tmp_path),
        patch.object(app.urllib.request, "urlopen", side_effect=OSError("offline")),
        pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="недоступен"),
    ):
        app.fetch_esstu_html_sync(f"bakalavriat/{filename}", allow_group_fallback=False)


def test_allowed_html_fallback_preserves_date_and_hash(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / "bakalavriat" / "1.htm"
    source.parent.mkdir()
    source.write_bytes(b"saved HTML")
    (tmp_path / "sources.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"bakalavriat/1.htm": {"retrieved_at": "2026-10-12T01:00:00Z"}})
    )
    with (
        patch.object(app, "DEMO_DIR", tmp_path),
        patch.object(app.urllib.request, "urlopen", side_effect=OSError("offline")),
    ):
        page = app.fetch_esstu_html_sync("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    assert page.source_mode == "local_fallback"
    assert page.checked_at == "2026-10-12T01:00:00Z"
    assert page.source_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(b"saved HTML").hexdigest()


def test_groups_use_saved_json_when_live_catalog_is_unavailable(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / "bakalavriat" / "raspisan.htm"
    source.parent.mkdir()
    source.write_bytes(b"old local catalog must not replace saved JSON")
    snapshot = {
        "section": "bakalavriat",
        "groups": [{"id": "7", "name": "Saved group"}],
        "source_mode": "saved_snapshot",
        "source_checked_at": "2026-10-12T01:00:00Z",
        "is_stale_fallback": True,
    }
    app.CACHE.clear()
    cache_tools._service_cache.clear()
    try:
        with (
            patch.object(app, "DEMO_DIR", tmp_path),
            patch.object(app.urllib.request, "urlopen", side_effect=OSError("offline")),
            patch.object(app, "load_snapshot", return_value=snapshot) as load,
            patch.object(app, "save_snapshot") as save,
        ):
            result = app.service_groups("bakalavriat", force_refresh=True)
        assert result == snapshot
        load.assert_called_once_with("groups:bakalavriat")
        save.assert_not_called()
    finally:
        app.CACHE.clear()
        cache_tools._service_cache.clear()

import hashlib
import json
