def test_real_headings_do_not_depend_on_font_colour():
    html = (FIXTURES / "zo1-24.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251")
    original = parse_group_schedule_html(html, "24", "zo1")
    assert original["group_name"] == "ЗУ-424-2"
    changed = parse_group_schedule_html(html.replace("#ff00ff", "#123456"), "24", "zo1")
    assert changed == original
    assert not changed["is_incomplete"]


def test_real_caf48_mixed_encoding_has_complete_teachers():
    raw = (FIXTURES / "spezialitet-Caf48.htm").read_bytes()
    teachers = parse_department_teachers_html(
        raw.decode("cp1251"), "Caf48", "spezialitet"
    )
    assert teachers and all(not teacher["is_incomplete"] for teacher in teachers)
    assert any("Скуратова" in teacher["profile"]["short_name"] for teacher in teachers)
    assert repair_mojibake("Р§С‚РІ") == "Чтв"
    assert repair_mojibake("РџРЅРґ") == "Пнд"
    assert repair_mojibake("История России Иванов И.И.") == "История России Иванов И.И."
    for fixture in json.loads(
        (FIXTURES / "review-manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ):
        assert (
            hashlib.sha256((FIXTURES / fixture["fixture"]).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            == fixture["sha256"]
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("age,used", [(0, True), (7, True), (8, False), (None, False)])
def test_group_fallback_has_identity_and_seven_day_expiry(age, used):
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 17, tzinfo=UTC)
    candidate = {
        "group_id": "new",
        "group_name": "A",
        "is_incomplete": True,
        "parse_errors": ["missing"],
    }
    old = {
        "group_id": "old",
        "group_name": "A",
        "is_incomplete": False,
        "weeks": {"1": {}},
        "source_checked_at": (now - timedelta(days=age)).isoformat()
        if age is not None
        else None,
    }
    result = last_good_group(candidate, old, now)
    assert bool(result.get("is_stale_fallback")) == used
    assert old["group_id"] == "old"
    assert not last_good_group({**candidate, "group_name": "B"}, old, now).get(
        "is_stale_fallback"
    )
    assert source_is_recent(old, now) == used


def test_quality_checks_regression_and_absolute_ceiling(tmp_path):
    baseline = tmp_path / "previous"
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    for root, failed in [(baseline, 2), (site, 3)]:
        (root / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
        (root / "api/status.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "group_expected_count": 100,
                    "group_failed_count": failed,
                    "pipeline_failed_count": failed,
                }
            )
        )
    check_parse_quality(site, baseline=baseline)
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "group_expected_count": 100,
                "group_failed_count": 4,
                "pipeline_failed_count": 4,
            }
        )
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        check_parse_quality(site, baseline=baseline)
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "group_expected_count": 100,
                "group_failed_count": 6,
                "pipeline_failed_count": 6,
            }
        )
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        check_parse_quality(site)


def test_code_publication_reuses_old_api_without_faking_freshness(tmp_path):
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    baseline = tmp_path / "old"
    for root in (site, baseline):
        (root / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
    old = {
        "generated_at": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z",
        "group_failed_count": 121,
        "build_id": "old",
        "code_revision": "old",
    }
    (baseline / "api/status.json").write_text(json.dumps(old))
    (baseline / "api/test.json").write_bytes(b'{"real old data":true}')
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "build_id": "tested",
                "code_revision": "new",
                "generated_at": "2026-10-17T00:00:00Z",
            }
        )
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        reuse_api(site, baseline, "wrong")
    reuse_api(site, baseline, "tested")
    result = json.loads((site / "api/status.json").read_text())
    assert (
        result["generated_at"] == old["generated_at"]
        and result["group_failed_count"] == 121
    )
    assert result["build_id"] == "tested" and result["code_revision"] == "new"
    assert (site / "api/test.json").read_bytes() == (
        baseline / "api/test.json"
    ).read_bytes()


def saved_source(root, expected="1146 тпуп", recent=False):
    directory = root / "bakalavriat"
    directory.mkdir()
    (directory / "raspisan.htm").write_text(
        '<table><tr><td>1 курс</td></tr><tr><td><a href="1.htm">'
        + expected
        + "</a></td></tr></table>",
        encoding="cp1251",
    )
    raw = (FIXTURES / "bakalavriat-1.htm").read_bytes()
    (directory / "1.htm").write_bytes(raw)
    checked = datetime.now(UTC) if recent else datetime(2026, 1, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    (root / "sources.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "bakalavriat/1.htm": {
                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
                    "retrieved_at": checked.isoformat(),
                    "last_modified": "Fri, 16 Oct 2026 07:52:00 GMT",
                    "etag": "old-tag",
                }
            }
        )
    )
    return raw


def test_recent_valid_source_is_resumed_without_a_download(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = saved_source(tmp_path, recent=True)
    network = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", network)
    _, metadata = sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    network.assert_not_called()
    assert metadata["reused"]
    assert (tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm").read_bytes() == raw


def test_not_modified_keeps_bytes_and_updates_checked_at(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = saved_source(tmp_path)
    seen = []

    def response(request, timeout):
        seen.append(request)
        raise HTTPError(request.full_url, 304, "Not Modified", {}, None)

    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", response)
    _, metadata = sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    assert seen[0].get_header("If-none-match") == "old-tag"
    assert len(seen) == 1 and metadata["bytes"] == len(raw) and metadata["reused"]
    assert (
        datetime.now(UTC) - datetime.fromisoformat(metadata["retrieved_at"])
    ).total_seconds() < 5
    assert (tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm").read_bytes() == raw


def test_fetch_rejects_wrong_group_before_replacing_saved_source(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    raw = saved_source(tmp_path, expected="Другая группа")

    class Response:
        headers = {}

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *args):
            pass

        def read(self):
            return raw

    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", lambda *args, **kwargs: Response())
    with pytest.raises(sync_departments.SourceDefect, match="не совпадает"):
        sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    assert (tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm").read_bytes() == raw


def test_durable_data_branch_round_trip(tmp_path):
    from scripts.data_branch import restore, save, git

    remote = tmp_path / "remote.git"
    remote.mkdir()
    git(remote, "init", "--bare", "-q")
    sources = tmp_path / "source"
    sources.mkdir()
    (sources / "bytes.htm").write_bytes(b"raw\r\n\xff")
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    (site / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
    (site / "api/status.json").write_text('{"build_id":"checked"}')
    work = tmp_path / "writer"
    work.mkdir()
    git(work, "init", "-q")
    git(work, "config", "core.autocrlf", "false")
    git(work, "remote", "add", "origin", str(remote))
    save(work, site, sources, "revision")
    target = tmp_path / "reader"
    restored = tmp_path / "restored"
    assert restore(target, str(remote), restored)
    assert (restored / "bytes.htm").read_bytes() == b"raw\r\n\xff"
    assert json.loads((target / "api/status.json").read_text())["build_id"] == "checked"


def test_partial_group_errors_do_not_block_all_healthy_data(tmp_path):
    from scripts.check_sync import check_reports

    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        (tmp_path / name).write_text(
            json.dumps({"downloaded": ["valid.htm"], "failed": [{"path": "bad.htm"}]})
        )
    check_reports(tmp_path, allow_partial=True)
    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        (tmp_path / name).write_text(
            json.dumps({"downloaded": [], "failed": [{"path": "bad.htm"}]})
        )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="No source"):
        check_reports(tmp_path, allow_partial=True)


def test_bootstrap_reads_one_published_release_and_detects_a_mid_copy_update(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    from scripts.published_data import bootstrap
    from urllib.parse import urlparse

    values = {
        "api/status.json": {
            "generated_at": "2026-10-17T00:00:00Z",
            "code_revision": "previous",
        }
    }
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        values["api/groups/" + section + ".json"] = {
            "groups": [{"id": "1", "name": "A"}]
        }
        values["api/schedule/" + section + "/1.json"] = {"is_incomplete": False}
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet"):
        values["api/departments/" + section + ".json"] = {"institutes": []}
        values["api/teachers/all/" + section + ".json"] = {"teachers": []}
    for name in ("rooms", "sources", "catalog-status"):
        values["api/" + name + ".json"] = {}

    class Response:
        def __init__(self, value):
            self.value = value

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *args):
            pass

        def read(self):
            return json.dumps(self.value).encode()

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "urllib.request.urlopen",
        lambda request, timeout: Response(
            values[urlparse(request.full_url).path.removeprefix("/site/")]
        ),
    )
    bootstrap(tmp_path / "valid", "https://test.invalid/site/")
    assert (
        json.loads((tmp_path / "valid/api/status.json").read_text())[
            "group_expected_count"
        ]
        == 4
    )
    calls = 0

    def changing(request, timeout):
        nonlocal calls
        path = urlparse(request.full_url).path.removeprefix("/site/")
        if path == "api/status.json":
            calls += 1
            return Response({**values[path], "code_revision": str(calls)})
        return Response(values[path])

    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", changing)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="changed during bootstrap"):
        bootstrap(tmp_path / "invalid", "https://test.invalid/site/")
    assert not (tmp_path / "invalid/api/status.json").exists()


===== tests/test_p0.py =====
SHA-256: fdc04b29a110fd7c6bc66d4842afe78c0e6961e9747be6d800cd3ad15bc163ba

import ssl
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import app
import parser
from tests import test_audit


class ReadOnlyHTTPTests(unittest.TestCase):
    setUpClass = classmethod(test_audit.HTTPRegressionTests.setUpClass.__func__)
    tearDownClass = classmethod(test_audit.HTTPRegressionTests.tearDownClass.__func__)
    request = test_audit.HTTPRegressionTests.request

    def test_post_never_writes_and_has_no_cors(self):
        for path in ["/api/teacher/meta", "/api/groups", "/anything"]:
            with patch.object(parser, "save_teacher_meta_entry") as write:
                status, headers, body = self.request(
                    path,
                    method="POST",
                    body='{"teacher_key":"A","photo":"https://evil.invalid/p.png"}',
                    headers={"Origin": "https://evil.invalid"},
                )
                self.assertEqual(status, 405)
                self.assertNotIn("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", headers)
                self.assertNotIn("POST", headers.get("Allow", ""))
                write.assert_not_called()

    def test_invalid_lengths_and_transfer_encoding(self):
        for length in ["-1", "8193", "nope", "+1"]:
            status, headers, _ = self.request(
                "/api/teacher/meta",
                method="POST",
                body="",
                headers={"Content-Length": length},
            )
            self.assertEqual(status, 400)
            self.assertNotIn("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", headers)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.request(
                "/api/teacher/meta",
                method="POST",
                body="",
                headers={"Transfer-Encoding": "chunked"},
            )[0],
            400,
        )

    def test_preflight_cannot_enable_post(self):
        status, headers, _ = self.request(
            "/api/teacher/meta",
            method="OPTIONS",
            headers={
                "Origin": "https://evil.invalid",
                "Access-Control-Request-Method": "POST",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(status, 405)
        self.assertNotIn("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", headers)
        self.assertNotIn("POST", headers.get("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", ""))
        self.assertNotIn("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", headers)


class LocalAvatarTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_only_existing_local_raster_avatar_is_allowed(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path(__file__).parent) as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            (root / "avatars").mkdir()
            (root / "avatars/photo.webp").write_bytes(b"fixture")
            with patch.object(parser, "AVATAR_ROOT", root):
                self.assertEqual(
                    parser.local_teacher_photo("avatars/photo.webp"),
                    "avatars/photo.webp",
                )
                for value in [
                    "https://esstu.ru/photo.png",
                    "http://evil.invalid/p.png",
                    "//evil.invalid/p.png",
                    "data:image/svg+xml,hi",
                    "javascript:alert(1)",
                    "avatars/../photo.webp",
                    "avatars/not-found.png",
                    "avatars/photo.svg",
                    'avatars/p.png" onerror="x',
                    None,
                ]:
                    self.assertEqual(parser.local_teacher_photo(value), "", str(value))

    def test_legacy_remote_photo_is_ignored_and_writes_reject_it(self):
        with patch.object(
            parser,
            "load_teachers_meta",
            return_value={
                "иванов и.и.": {"photo": "https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=A"}
            },
        ):
            profile = parser.build_teacher_profile("Иванов И.И.")
            self.assertEqual(profile["photo"], "")
            self.assertTrue(profile["initials"])
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            parser.save_teacher_meta_entry(
                "Иванов И.И.", {"photo": "https://evil.invalid/p.png"}
            )

    def test_tls_chain_and_hostname_checks_stay_enabled(self):
        context = app.create_esstu_ssl_context()
        self.assertEqual(context.verify_mode, ssl.CERT_REQUIRED)
        self.assertTrue(context.check_hostname)
        if hasattr(ssl, "OP_LEGACY_SERVER_CONNECT"):
            self.assertTrue(context.options & ssl.OP_LEGACY_SERVER_CONNECT)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== tests/test_publication.py =====
SHA-256: c9befc5122ef14c8faeeb256c0dbbdcedf7a80bb3166c18b5629fe8bc3759b01

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from esstu_schedule.catalog import source_metadata, stamp_schedule
from esstu_schedule.data.diagnostics import parsing_status, reset_diagnostics
from esstu_schedule.data.provenance import build_provenance, source_manifest
from esstu_schedule.parser import parse_group_schedule_html
from scripts.check_sync import check_reports


def test_source_fingerprints_are_actual_bytes_and_not_a_new_check(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    source = tmp_path / "bakalavriat" / "1.htm"
    source.parent.mkdir()
    source.write_bytes(b"old source")
    metadata = {"bakalavriat/1.htm": {"retrieved_at": "2026-10-12T01:00:00Z"}}
    (tmp_path / "sources.json").write_text(json.dumps(metadata))
    manifest = source_metadata(tmp_path, with_hashes=True)
    assert (
        manifest["bakalavriat/1.htm"]["sha256"]
        == hashlib.sha256(b"old source").hexdigest()
    )
    schedule = {"source_file": "bakalavriat/1.htm", "weeks": {}}
    stamp_schedule(schedule, manifest, "2026-10-16T03:00:00Z")
    assert schedule["source_checked_at"] == "2026-10-12T01:00:00Z"
    assert schedule["source_sha256"] == manifest["bakalavriat/1.htm"]["sha256"]
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_SHA", "1234567890")
    first = build_provenance(manifest, tmp_path)
    assert first["code_revision"] == "1234567890"
    assert first["source_checked_at_latest"] == "2026-10-12T01:00:00+00:00"
    source.write_bytes(b"changed source")
    second = build_provenance(source_manifest(tmp_path, metadata), tmp_path)
    assert first["sources_sha256"] != second["sources_sha256"]


def test_unknown_freshness_stays_unknown(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_SHA", "test")
    result = build_provenance({"file.htm": {"sha256": "a"}}, tmp_path)
    assert result["source_checked_at_latest"] is None
    assert result["sources_without_check_date"] == 1


def test_unparsed_cells_are_counted_separately_from_broken_tables():
    reset_diagnostics()
    html = "<table><tr><td>Пары</td><td>1-я</td></tr><tr><td>Время</td><td>09:00-10:35</td></tr>"
    html += '<tr><td colspan="2">I неделя</td></tr><tr><td>Пнд</td><td>лаб. Иностранный язык а.15-571 а.15-573 а.15-</td></tr></table>'
    schedule = parse_group_schedule_html(html)
    assert not schedule["is_incomplete"]
    status = parsing_status()
    assert status["parse_error_count"] == 0
    assert status["unparsed_cell_count"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failed", [[], [{"path": "1.htm", "error": "unavailable"}]])
def test_partial_sync_cannot_succeed_silently(tmp_path, failed):
    for name in ("department-sync.json", "group-sync.json"):
        (tmp_path / name).write_text(
            json.dumps({"downloaded": ["ok.htm"], "failed": failed})
        )
    if failed:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
            check_reports(tmp_path)
    else:
        check_reports(tmp_path)


def test_mit_license_has_no_placeholders():
    license_text = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "LICENSE").read_text(
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    )
    assert "MIT License" in license_text
    assert "[year]" not in license_text and "[fullname]" not in license_text


===== tests/test_publication_quality.py =====
SHA-256: c490b9ab72319bd5f90bd1e193f610b725f74b02e26cf84c4a53fe8836405941

import json
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from scripts.check_sync import check_parse_quality
from scripts import sync_departments, sync_esstu


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "failed,blocked", [(0, False), (5, False), (6, True), (100, True)]
)
def test_publication_error_rate_limit(tmp_path, failed, blocked):
    (tmp_path / "api").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "api/status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"group_expected_count": 100, "group_failed_count": failed})
    )
    if blocked:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Publication blocked"):
            check_parse_quality(tmp_path, max_error_rate=0.05)
    else:
        check_parse_quality(tmp_path, max_error_rate=0.05)


def test_failed_department_phase_still_refreshes_independent_groups(monkeypatch):
    groups = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        sync_esstu, "sync_departments", lambda: {"failed": [{"path": "catalog"}]}
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_esstu, "sync_groups", groups)
    sync_esstu.sync_all()
    groups.assert_called_once()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "module,entry,expected",
    [(sync_departments, "sync", 2), (sync_esstu, "sync_groups", 4)],
)
def test_unavailable_live_catalog_never_fans_out_over_saved_pages(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, module, entry, expected
):
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        directory = tmp_path / section
        directory.mkdir()
        (
            directory / ("craspisanEdt.htm" if entry == "sync" else "raspisan.htm")
        ).write_text("saved catalog must not be used to fan out")
    fetch = Mock(side_effect=OSError("portal timed out"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "fetch_page", fetch)
    report = getattr(module, entry)()
    assert fetch.call_count == expected
    assert len(report["failed"]) == expected
    assert not report["downloaded"]


===== tests/test_release.py =====
SHA-256: 85440f61bad19f92dda32bb1d1745cbb2eff8d55451a0156e0f82b980cc7e489

import re
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from scripts.build_static_site import (
    build_id,
    source_modified_iso,
    stamp_shell,
    write_api_json,
)

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def test_release_version_is_consistent_and_data_cache_is_stable():
    html = (ROOT / "static/index.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    version = re.search(r'content="([0-9.]+)" name="app-release"', html)[1]
    worker = (ROOT / "static/sw.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert 'content="__BUILD_ID__" name="app-version"' in html
    assert "const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';" in worker
    experience = (ROOT / "static/experience.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert f"const APP_VERSION = '{version}';" in experience
    assert "const DATA_CACHE = `${CACHE_PREFIX}data-v2`;" in worker


def test_shell_hash_changes_with_assets_but_not_data_or_time(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "index.html").write_text(
        '<meta content="__BUILD_ID__" name="app-version"/>'
    )
    (tmp_path / "sw.js").write_text("const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';")
    css = tmp_path / "app.css"
    css.write_text("body{color:red}")
    (tmp_path / "api").mkdir()
    api = tmp_path / "api/status.json"
    api.write_text('{"generated_at":"first"}')
    first = stamp_shell(tmp_path)
    assert first in (tmp_path / "sw.js").read_text()
    assert (
        f'content="{first}" name="app-version"' in (tmp_path / "index.html").read_text()
    )
    api.write_text('{"generated_at":"later"}')
    assert build_id(tmp_path) == first
    assert stamp_shell(tmp_path) == first
    css.write_text("body{color:blue}")
    second = stamp_shell(tmp_path)
    assert second != first
    assert second in (tmp_path / "sw.js").read_text()
    (tmp_path / "icon.svg").write_text("<svg/>")
    assert build_id(tmp_path) != second


def test_workflows_pin_runner_and_action_revisions():
    for file in (ROOT / ".github/workflows").glob("*.yml"):
        text = file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "ubuntu-latest" not in text
        assert "timeout-minutes:" in text
        assert "node-version: '22'" not in text or "ubuntu-24.04" in text
        for action in re.findall(r"uses: (actions/[^\s]+)", text):
            assert re.fullmatch(r"actions/[a-z-]+@[0-9a-f]{40}", action), action


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "worker,html",
    [
        ("const OTHER = 'x';", '<meta content="__BUILD_ID__" name="app-version"/>'),
        ("const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';", '<meta name="other"/>'),
    ],
)
def test_missing_build_marker_fails_without_partial_writes(tmp_path, worker, html):
    (tmp_path / "sw.js").write_text(worker)
    (tmp_path / "index.html").write_text(html)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="exactly one"):
        stamp_shell(tmp_path)
    assert (tmp_path / "sw.js").read_text() == worker
    assert (tmp_path / "index.html").read_text() == html

        (None, None),
        ("Static synchronization", None),
    ],
)
def test_source_modified_time_is_real_iso_or_unknown(value, expected):

        [
            sys.executable,

    import json

    path = tmp_path / "api/test.json"
    write_api_json(path, {"count": 1})
    assert json.loads(path.read_text()) == {"count": 1, "schema_version": 2}

def test_worker_only_changes_hash_and_every_local_asset_is_precached(tmp_path):
    import json
    import shutil
    from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
    from scripts.build_static_site import shell_paths

    shutil.copytree(ROOT / "static", tmp_path, dirs_exist_ok=True)
    (tmp_path / "avatars").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (tmp_path / "avatars/test.png").write_bytes(
        (ROOT / "static/icons/app-icon-192.png").read_bytes()
    )
    first = stamp_shell(tmp_path)
    worker = tmp_path / "sw.js"
    worker.write_text(
        worker.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + "\n// worker-only behavior change\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    second = stamp_shell(tmp_path)
    assert first != second and build_id(tmp_path) == second
    shell = json.loads(
        re.search(r"const SHELL = (\[.*?\]);", worker.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[1]
    )
    assert "./avatars/test.png" in shell
    assert shell == shell_paths(tmp_path)
    html = BeautifulSoup((tmp_path / "index.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8"), "lxml")
    for tag in html.find_all(["script", "link", "img"]):
        url = tag.get("src") or tag.get("href") or ""
        if url and not url.startswith(("http:", "https:", "data:", "#")):
            assert "./" + url.removeprefix("./") in shell


===== tests/test_rooms.py =====
SHA-256: ab5dbcdcbf4551760ee0b7231ebb73abed77d140354dd72a7a93775eec95a997

import unittest

from rooms import build_rooms_index, normalize_room


class RoomsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_slot_order_does_not_depend_on_schedule_loading_order(self):
        def schedule(number, start, subject):
            return {
                "weeks": {
                    "1": {
                        "friday": {
                            "pairs": [
                                {
                                    "number": number,
                                    "start": start,
                                    "end": "14:35",
                                    "lessons": [{"room": "0105", "subject": subject}],
                                }
                            ]
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

        late = schedule(3, "13:00", "Физика")
        early = schedule(1, "09:00", "История")
        parallel = schedule(3, "13:00", "Алгебра")
        forward = build_rooms_index([late, early, parallel, late])
        reverse = build_rooms_index([parallel, early, late])
        self.assertEqual(forward, reverse)
        slots = forward["rooms"][0]["slots"]
        self.assertEqual(list(slots), ["1:friday:1", "1:friday:3"])
        self.assertEqual(
            [row["subject"] for row in slots["1:friday:3"]], ["Алгебра", "Физика"]
        )

    def test_board_annotation_does_not_create_another_room(self):
        self.assertEqual(normalize_room("0410и/д"), "0410")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_room(" 0410 и/д "), "0410")

    def test_collisions_and_duplicates(self):
        schedule = {
            "group_name": "A",
            "weeks": {
                "1": {
                    "monday": {
                        "pairs": [
                            {
                                "number": 1,
                                "start": "09:00",
                                "end": "10:35",
                                "lessons": [
                                    {"room": " 0410 ", "subject": "Physics"},
                                    {"room": "0410", "subject": "Math"},
                                    {"room": "", "subject": "Remote"},
                                ],
                            }
                        ]
                    }
                }
            },
        }
        result = build_rooms_index([schedule, schedule])
        self.assertEqual(result["count"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(result["rooms"][0]["slots"]["1:monday:1"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(result["rooms"][0]["room"], "0410")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== tests/test_smoke.py =====
SHA-256: c36591e85784d51a5eddd3f6e160251e7c7ec8445e52fd6af38461b75a718770

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pytest

from scripts.smoke_site import check_site


def make_site(root, generated):
    api = root / "api"
    api.mkdir()
    (api / "status.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "generated_at": generated,
                "schema_version": 2,
                "build_id": "test-build",
            }
        )
    )
    for section in ("bakalavriat", "spezialitet", "zo1", "zo2"):
        (api / "groups").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (api / "groups" / f"{section}.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"groups": [{"id": "99", "name": "Any new group"}]})
        )
        schedules = api / "schedule" / section
        schedules.mkdir(parents=True)
        (schedules / "99.json").write_text("{}")


def test_live_smoke_accepts_arbitrary_names_and_order(tmp_path):
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 17, tzinfo=UTC)
    make_site(tmp_path, now.isoformat())
    check_site(tmp_path, now=now, minimum_groups=4)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("delta", [-timedelta(hours=1), timedelta(hours=1)])
def test_smoke_rejects_stale_or_future_builds(tmp_path, delta):
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 17, tzinfo=UTC)
    make_site(tmp_path, (now + delta).isoformat())
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="generated_at"):
        check_site(tmp_path, now=now, minimum_groups=4)


def test_smoke_rejects_truncated_publication(tmp_path):
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 17, tzinfo=UTC)
    make_site(tmp_path, now.isoformat())
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Too few groups"):
        check_site(tmp_path, now=now, minimum_groups=100)
    (tmp_path / "api/schedule/zo2/99.json").unlink()
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Missing schedule"):
        check_site(tmp_path, now=now, minimum_groups=4)


===== tests/test_source_quality.py =====
SHA-256: 2eec146702bb198116f53e8a2edae2f15742fd8995301c7bce5521d9a9294e93

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pytest
from esstu_schedule.identity import resolve_catalog
from esstu_schedule.parser import normalize_group_name
from scripts.group_freshness import group_freshness
from scripts.publication import reuse_api
from scripts.check_sync import check_parse_quality
from scripts.build_static_site import stamp_shell
from scripts import sync_runtime, sync_departments


def group(gid, name):
    return {"id": str(gid), "name": name, "norm_name": normalize_group_name(name)}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "catalog,page,reason,recovered",
    [
        ("...", "ЗМ-314", None, True),
        ("ЗУ-396-1", "ЗУ-395-1", "group_name_mismatch", False),
        ("ЗМ125ппрс", "ЗМ274", "group_name_mismatch", False),
        ("ЗУ-396-1", "....", "source_unfilled", False),
        ("...", "1", "heading_missing", False),
    ],
)
def test_source_identity_categories(catalog, page, reason, recovered):
    groups, issues, repairs = resolve_catalog(
        [group(1, catalog)], {"1": {"group_id": "1", "group_name": page}}
    )
    assert issues.get("1", {}).get("reason") == reason
    assert bool(repairs) == recovered
    assert groups[0]["id"] == "1"
    assert groups[0]["name"] == (page if recovered else catalog)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("expected", ["ЗУ-396-1", "..."])
def test_other_catalog_group_is_never_relabelled(expected):
    groups, issues, repairs = resolve_catalog(
        [group(1, expected), group(2, "ЗУ-395-1")],
        {"1": {"group_id": "1", "group_name": "ЗУ-395-1"}},
    )
    assert issues["1"]["reason"] == "duplicate_page"
    assert issues["1"]["duplicate_of"] == ["2"]
    assert groups[0]["name"] == expected and not repairs


def test_two_placeholder_pages_with_same_name_are_ambiguous():
    _, issues, repairs = resolve_catalog(
        [group(1, "..."), group(2, "....")],
        {str(i): {"group_id": str(i), "group_name": "ЗМ-314"} for i in (1, 2)},
    )
    assert all(item["reason"] == "duplicate_page" for item in issues.values())
    assert not repairs


def test_source_failures_do_not_consume_pipeline_budget(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "api").mkdir()
    status = {
        "group_expected_count": 663,
        "group_failed_count": 132,
        "pipeline_failed_count": 0,
        "source_issue_count": 132,
    }
    (tmp_path / "api/status.json").write_text(json.dumps(status))
    check_parse_quality(tmp_path)
    status["pipeline_failed_count"] = 34
    (tmp_path / "api/status.json").write_text(json.dumps(status))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="pipeline failures"):
        check_parse_quality(tmp_path)


def test_parser_change_rebuilds_saved_api_without_faking_source_dates(tmp_path):
    previous = tmp_path / "old"
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    for root in (previous, site):
        (root / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
    old = {
        "parser_revision": "old",
        "build_id": "old",
        "generated_at": "2026-10-10T00:00:00Z",
    }
    current = {
        **old,
        "parser_revision": "new",
        "build_id": "tested",
        "generated_at": "2026-10-17T00:00:00Z",
        "source_checked_at_latest": "2026-10-10T00:00:00Z",
        "group_expected_count": 663,
        "pipeline_failed_count": 0,
    }
    for root, status in ((previous, old), (site, current)):
        (root / "api/status.json").write_text(json.dumps(status))
    (site / "api/fixed.json").write_text('{"parser_fix":true}')
    reuse_api(site, previous, "tested")
    result = json.loads((site / "api/status.json").read_text())
    assert result["api_reparsed_from_sources"]
    assert result["source_checked_at_latest"] == current["source_checked_at_latest"]
    assert (site / "api/fixed.json").exists()


def test_freshness_counts_old_and_unknown_groups_even_with_one_fresh_page():
    now = datetime(2026, 10, 17, tzinfo=UTC)
    schedules = [
        {
            "section": "zo1",
            "group_id": str(i),
            "source_checked_at": (now - timedelta(days=i)).isoformat()
            if i < 3
            else None,
        }
        for i in range(4)
    ]
    result = group_freshness(schedules, now)
    assert result["groups_checked_24h"] == 2
    assert result["groups_checked_24h_ratio"] == 0.5
    assert len(result["group_check_dates"]) == 4


def test_bad_parser_candidate_does_not_block_a_code_release(tmp_path):
    previous = tmp_path / "old"
    site = tmp_path / "site"
    for root in (previous, site):
        (root / "api").mkdir(parents=True)
    old = {
        "parser_revision": "old",
        "build_id": "old",
        "generated_at": "2026-10-10T00:00:00Z",
    }
    candidate = {
        **old,
        "parser_revision": "new",
        "build_id": "tested",
        "code_revision": "new-code",
        "generated_at": "2026-10-17T00:00:00Z",
        "group_expected_count": 663,
        "pipeline_failed_count": 100,
    }
    for root, status in ((previous, old), (site, candidate)):
        (root / "api/status.json").write_text(json.dumps(status))
    (previous / "api/good.json").write_bytes(b'{"saved":true}')
    (site / "api/bad.json").write_bytes(b'{"saved":false}')
    reuse_api(site, previous, "tested")
    result = json.loads((site / "api/status.json").read_text())
    assert result["build_id"] == "tested" and result["parser_revision"] == "old"
    assert result["generated_at"] == old["generated_at"]
    assert (
        result["parser_rebuild_error"]
        and result["parser_rebuild_candidate_revision"] == "new"
    )
    assert (site / "api/good.json").read_bytes() == b'{"saved":true}'
    assert not (site / "api/bad.json").exists()


def test_soft_deadline_does_not_schedule_the_rest_of_a_large_catalog(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_runtime, "_deadline", None)
    calls = []

    def fetch(path):
        calls.append(path)
        sync_runtime.set_deadline(0)
        return path

    results = list(sync_runtime.download_targets(map(str, range(100)), fetch))
    assert 1 <= len(calls) <= 4
    assert len(results) == len(calls)
    assert sync_runtime.remaining() == 0


def test_negative_page_is_downloaded_once_per_day_and_keeps_positive_bytes(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    from tests.test_followup import saved_source

    saved_source(tmp_path, expected="Другая группа")
    target = tmp_path / "bakalavriat/1.htm"
    raw = target.read_bytes()
    calls = []

    class Response:
        headers = {}

        def __enter__(self):
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *args):
            pass

        def read(self):
            return raw

    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_departments, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sync_runtime, "_deadline", None)

    def urlopen(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append(1)
        return Response()

    monkeypatch.setattr("urllib.request.urlopen", urlopen)
    for _ in range(2):
        with pytest.raises(sync_departments.SourceDefect):
            sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert target.read_bytes() == raw
    assert (tmp_path / ".negative/bakalavriat/1.json").exists()


def test_shell_budget_rejects_an_accidentally_bundled_large_file(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "index.html").write_text(
        '<meta content="__BUILD_ID__" name="app-version">'
    )
    (tmp_path / "sw.js").write_text("const CACHE_NAME = 'esstu-live-__BUILD_ID__';")
    (tmp_path / "accidental.bin").write_bytes(b"0" * (5 * 1024 * 1024))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="5 MiB"):
        stamp_shell(tmp_path)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "gid,expected,reason",
    [
        ("97", "ЗМ-314", None),
        ("3", "ЗУ-436-1", "duplicate_page"),
        ("68", "ЗМ126ппрс", "source_unfilled"),
    ],
)
def test_real_saved_correspondence_pages(gid, expected, reason):
    from pathlib import Path
    from esstu_schedule.parser import parse_groups_list_html
    from esstu_schedule.data.repository import group_schedule

    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "demo_data/zo1"
    groups = parse_groups_list_html(
        (root / "raspisan.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), "zo1"
    )
    schedule = group_schedule(
        (root / f"{gid}.htm").read_bytes().decode("cp1251"), gid, "zo1"
    )
    resolved, issues, _ = resolve_catalog(groups, {gid: schedule})
    assert next(item["name"] for item in resolved if item["id"] == gid) == expected
    assert issues.get(gid, {}).get("reason") == reason


def test_bootstrap_only_accepts_matching_source_artifact(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import hashlib
    from pathlib import Path
    from scripts import source_artifact

    expected = {
        "bakalavriat/1.htm": {
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"exact published bytes").hexdigest(),
            "retrieved_at": "2026-10-16T00:00:00Z",
        }
    }
    artifacts = {
        "artifacts": [
            {
                "id": i,
                "expired": False,
                "workflow_run": {"head_branch": "main", "id": i},
            }
            for i in (1, 2)
        ]
    }
    calls = []

    def run_gh(*args):
        if args[0] == "api":
            return json.dumps(artifacts)
        calls.append(args[2])
        root = Path(args[-1])
        (root / "bakalavriat").mkdir()
        (root / "sources.json").write_text("{}")
        (root / "bakalavriat/1.htm").write_bytes(
            b"wrong bytes" if args[2] == "1" else b"exact published bytes"
        )
        return ""

    monkeypatch.setattr(source_artifact, "run_gh", run_gh)
    target = tmp_path / "sources"
    assert source_artifact.restore_artifact("owner/repo", target, expected)
    assert calls == ["1", "2"]
    assert json.loads((target / "sources.json").read_text()) == expected


===== tests/test_study.py =====
SHA-256: acc59924c2d3ce168fbc0820b861aa1930e9e5e5a49b96e2e248f54de5c2eb54

import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from parser import extract_tags, parse_single_lesson_chunk, parse_teacher_cell_lessons


def test_teacher_only_chunks_keep_identity_without_inventing_subject():
    rows = json.loads(
        (Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures/teacher-only-chunks.json").read_text(
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
    )
    for row in rows:
        lesson = parse_single_lesson_chunk(row["raw"])
        assert lesson["subject"] == "Предмет не указан"
        assert lesson["type"] == "Требуется сверка"
        assert lesson["teacher"] == row["teacher"]
        assert lesson["room"] == row["room"]
        assert lesson["parse_warning"]
        assert lesson["raw"] == row["raw"]


def test_teacher_suffix_does_not_hide_a_real_subject():
    lesson = parse_single_lesson_chunk("лек. Математика ДАНЗАНОВА С.В. - а.0105")
    assert lesson["subject"] == "Математика"
    assert lesson["teacher"] == "Данзанова С.В."
    assert not lesson["parse_warning"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "raw,subject,teacher",
    [
        (
            "Экономическая теория АЛЕКСЕЕВА Г.Е. а.701 и/п",
            "Экономическая теория",
            "Алексеева Г.Е.",
        ),
        (
            "Правовые основы профессиональной деятельности УХИНЖАПОВА Э.В. а.701 и/п",
            "Правовые основы профессиональной деятельности",
            "Ухинжапова Э.В.",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_teacher_and_room_with_panel_annotation(raw, subject, teacher):
    from rooms import build_rooms_index
    from teachers import teachers_from_groups

    lesson = parse_single_lesson_chunk(raw)
    assert (lesson["subject"], lesson["teacher"], lesson["room"]) == (
        subject,
        teacher,
        "701 и/п",
    )
    assert not lesson["parse_warning"] and not lesson.get("needs_review")
    schedule = {
        "group_name": "A",
        "weeks": {
            "1": {
                "monday": {
                    "pairs": [
                        {
                            "number": 1,
                            "start": "09:00",
                            "end": "10:35",
                            "lessons": [lesson],
                        }
                    ]
                }
            }
        },
    }
    assert (
        teachers_from_groups([schedule])[0]["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0][
            "lessons"
        ][0]["subject"]
        == subject
    )
    assert build_rooms_index([schedule])["rooms"][0]["room"] == "701"


def test_unrecognized_room_annotation_requests_review():
    raw = "Экономика АЛЕКСЕЕВА Г.Е. а.701 и/к"
    lesson = parse_single_lesson_chunk(raw)
    assert lesson["needs_review"] and lesson["parse_warning"]
    assert lesson["raw"] == raw
    assert lesson["room"] == ""


class StudyParsingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_subgroup_markers(self):
        self.assertEqual(extract_tags("11 п/г"), [])
        self.assertEqual(extract_tags("Физика 1"), [])
        self.assertEqual(extract_tags("1-я подгруппа"), ["1 п/г"])
        self.assertEqual(extract_tags("2 п/г"), ["2 п/г"])

    def test_attestation_types_for_groups_and_teachers(self):
        for prefix, expected in [
            ("экз.", "exam"),
            ("зач.", "test"),
            ("конс.", "consultation"),
        ]:
            with self.subTest(prefix=prefix):
                self.assertEqual(
                    parse_single_lesson_chunk(prefix + " Физика")["type_code"], expected
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    parse_teacher_cell_lessons("1146 а.0410 " + prefix + " Физика")[0][
                        "type_code"
                    ],
                    expected,
                )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== tests/test_sync.py =====
SHA-256: ac002982277106b15d67369dff9297f865db685d6e782e496f1136aa318a3881

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import sync_departments


class SyncValidationTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_invalid_response_preserves_last_good_snapshot(self):
        class Response:
            headers = {}

            def __enter__(self):
                return self

            def __exit__(self, *args):
                pass

            def read(self):
                return b"<html>Service temporarily unavailable</html>"

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
            dir=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
        ) as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            self.assertEqual(root.resolve().parent, Path(__file__).resolve().parent)
            target = root / "bakalavriat" / "1.htm"
            target.parent.mkdir()
            target.write_bytes(b"last good snapshot")
            with (
                patch.object(sync_departments, "ROOT", root),
                patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=Response()),
            ):
                with self.assertRaises((ValueError, AttributeError)):
                    sync_departments.fetch_page("bakalavriat/1.htm")
            self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), b"last good snapshot")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


===== tests/test_teachers.py =====
SHA-256: 69dea4c4e8577b4e3f270f434d4500664b94af445367e7da5c79d26651e8baf8

import unittest
from copy import deepcopy

from parser import parse_teacher_cell_lessons
from teachers import combine_teacher_catalog, teachers_from_groups


class TeacherIndexTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_preserves_source_and_deduplicates(self):
        schedule = {
            "section": "bakalavriat",
            "group_name": "A",
            "weeks": {
                "1": {
                    "monday": {
                        "pairs": [
                            {
                                "number": 2,
                                "start": "10:45",
                                "end": "12:20",
                                "lessons": [
                                    {
                                        "teacher": "Семёнов А.В.",
                                        "subject": "Физика",
                                        "room": "0410",
                                    },
                                    {"teacher": "", "subject": "Без преподавателя"},
                                ],
                            }
                        ]
                    }
                }
            },
        }
        teachers = teachers_from_groups([schedule, schedule])
        self.assertEqual(len(teachers), 1)
        teacher = teachers[0]
        self.assertTrue(teacher["partial"])
        pair = teacher["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]
        self.assertEqual(len(pair["lessons"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(pair["lessons"][0]["groups"], "A")
        self.assertEqual(pair["lessons"][0]["subject"], "Физика")
        self.assertEqual(pair["start"], "10:45")

    def test_no_invented_teachers(self):
        self.assertEqual(teachers_from_groups([]), [])

    def test_official_cell_without_type(self):
        lesson = parse_teacher_cell_lessons("1146 тпуп а.0410 и/д")[0]
        self.assertEqual(lesson["subject"], "Предмет не указан в источнике")
        self.assertEqual(lesson["groups"], "1146 тпуп")
        self.assertEqual(lesson["room"], "0410 и/д")
        lesson = parse_teacher_cell_lessons("К96/1 а.0425 География")[0]
        self.assertEqual(lesson["subject"], "География")

    def test_official_priority_and_group_supplement(self):
        lesson = {
            "subject": "Физика",
            "room": "0410",
            "groups": "A",
            "source_type": "official_caf",
            "source_file": "bakalavriat/Caf5.htm",
        }
        official = {
            "teacher_key": "A",
            "caf_id": "Caf5",
            "weeks": {
                "1": {
                    "monday": {
                        "pairs": [
                            {
                                "number": 1,
                                "start": "09:00",
                                "end": "10:35",
                                "lessons": [lesson],
                            }
                        ]
                    }
                }
            },
        }
        derived = deepcopy(official)
        derived["caf_id"] = ""
        derived["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"][0]["source_type"] = (
            "group_derived"
        )
        derived["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"].append(
            {"subject": "Математика", "room": "0420"}
        )
        result = combine_teacher_catalog([official], [derived])[0]
        lessons = result["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"]
        self.assertEqual(len(lessons), 2)
        self.assertEqual(lessons[0]["source_type"], "official_caf")
        self.assertEqual(result["caf_ids"], ["Caf5"])
        self.assertFalse(result["identity_ambiguous"])
        other_caf = deepcopy(official)
        other_caf["caf_id"] = "Caf6"
        candidate = combine_teacher_catalog([official, other_caf], [derived])[0]
        self.assertTrue(candidate["identity_ambiguous"])
        self.assertEqual(candidate["identity_source_caf_ids"], ["Caf5", "Caf6"])

        # The same subject and room do not imply the same group or lesson type.
        parallel = deepcopy(official)
        parallel_lesson = parallel["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"][0]
        parallel_lesson["groups"] = "B"
        result = combine_teacher_catalog([official, parallel], [])[0]
        lessons = result["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"]
        self.assertEqual([item["groups"] for item in lessons], ["A", "B"])
        parallel_lesson["groups"] = "A"
        parallel_lesson["type_code"] = "lab"
        result = combine_teacher_catalog([official, parallel], [])[0]
        self.assertEqual(len(result["weeks"]["1"]["monday"]["pairs"][0]["lessons"]), 2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
